Costco Help Guide

Costco’s own member help, gathered into one screen-reader-friendly document

2026-08-03

Costco Help Guide

This guide gathers Costco’s own answers for its members, as they stood on 3 August 2026. The text is Costco’s; nothing has been summarised or rewritten.

Costco is a membership warehouse club, so a good deal of this is about the membership itself – what the tiers cost, how to renew, downgrade or cancel, and how the household card works. The rest covers ordering on costco.com and in the app, Costco Logistics deliveries and installation, the return policy, the Citi card, taxes and fees, and what happens at the warehouse.

The arrangement is Costco’s own, but it does not reach every article. Costco names eighteen categories in its own navigation and lists articles under them, and those names are used here. It builds the trail on each article by script rather than printing it, so an article Costco has not listed under a category cannot be placed from the page itself. 236 of the 274 articles are filed under Costco’s own headings; the remaining 38 are gathered under Miscellaneous, in alphabetical order like everything else.

On medicines. Costco runs pharmacies and this guide includes its help about the pharmacy service – transfers, refills, insurance and delivery. None of it is advice about a medicine. For anything concerning a drug itself, ask a pharmacist.

On scams. Costco publishes its own list of impersonation scams using its name, and those articles are here. If a message asks you to pay in an unusual way, Costco’s own guidance is the thing to read first.

This document holds 19 categories, 274 articles and about 64,940 words.

Citi Visa

How can I apply for the Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

How can I apply for the Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi?

Body

Thanks for your interest in the Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi. There are three ways to apply:

  1. Stop by the membership counter at your local Costco warehouse and ask for an application.
  2. Apply online here. Or, visit Costco.com and enter “Citi Visa” into the search bar.
  3. Call 1-800-259-3052.

How can I estimate my Costco Cash Rewards from Citi?

Updated 01/22/2025

Title

How can I estimate my Costco Cash Rewards from Citi?

Body

There’s no limit to the annual cash back rewards you can earn on purchases with the Costco Anywhere Visa Card by Citi! Whether you’re filling your tank, dining out, travelling the world, or shopping at your favorite stores

There’s no limit to the annual cash back rewards you can earn on purchases with the Costco Anywhere Visa Card by Citi!

Whether you’re filling your tank, dining out, travelling the world, or shopping at your favorite stores, you will earn cash back everywhere Visa is accepted.

To estimate your Costco Cash Rewards from Citi, visit citi.com/costcorewards.

I haven’t received my new Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi, what do I do?

Updated 03/03/2026

Title

I haven’t received my new Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi, what do I do?

Body

If you have not received a Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi, please contact Citi to verify your address is up-to-date and request a new card.

Is my reward certificate from Citi accepted on Costco.com?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Is my reward certificate from Citi accepted on Costco.com?

Body

Credit Card Reward Certificates earned on a Costco Anywhere Visa by Citi account can be used for purchases at Costco warehouses only. If you’d like to use your Costco Cash Reward Certificate for online purchases, you can stop by your local Costco warehouse and use the certificate to purchase a Costco Shop Card, since the Costco Shop Card can be used for online purchases.

For more information, please see the Citi’s cash back rewards terms and conditions.

My Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi isn’t working, what do I do?

Updated 03/03/2026

Title

My Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi isn’t working, what do I do?

Body

If you have received your Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi but are unable to use it for purchases, it’s possible that your Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi card has not yet been activated. To check, simply visit Citi here.

If your card has already been activated, the customer service team at Citi will be glad to help you troubleshoot the issue. You can find their customer service information here.

My information is wrong on my Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi, what do I do?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

My information is wrong on my Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi, what do I do?

Body

Here’s what to do if you find any incorrect information on your Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi:

  • If the error is on the FRONT of the card, please contact Citi customer service at 1-855-378-6467.
  • If the error is on the BACK of the card, please contact the Costco Member Services Center at 1-800-774-2678.

Updated 09/05/2023

Title

What do I do if I have questions about my Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi?

Body

Good news: Answers are just a phone call away! Please contact Citi directly using the information below:

Existing Cardholders:

  1. www.citi.com
  2. Consumer cards: 1-855-378-6467
  3. Business cards: 1-855-378-6468

When will I receive my Costco Cash Rewards from Citi?

Updated 12/24/2024

Title

When will I receive my Costco Cash Rewards from Citi?

Body

Costco Cash Rewards from Citi are issued annually with your February credit card billing statement. If you’ve opted for paperless statements, the reward will be sent via email. You can also access it through the Citi mobile app or on Citi.com.

Costco Cash Rewards from Citi expire on December 31 of the year they are distributed.

Rewards of $300 or more are eligible for direct deposit through www.citicards.com or the Citi mobile app. Amounts under $300 must be redeemed at a warehouse location for merchandise, cash or check. Additionally, rewards under $300 are not eligible for direct deposit; the option will not appear on your Citi account.

If you need assistance, please contact Citi.

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Where do I pay my credit card bill?

Body

Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi payments are made directly to Citi. Here’s the contact information you’ll need.

Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi:

  • www.citicards.com
  • Consumer cards: 1-855-378-6467
  • Business cards: 1-855-378-6468

Costco Services

How can I upgrade my membership before signing up for Costco Services?

Updated 09/01/2023

Title

How can I upgrade my membership before signing up for Costco Services?

Body

If you’d like to upgrade your membership before shopping or signing up for Costco Services, just sign in to your Costco.com account and select Account Details. Once your membership has been verified, you can go ahead and upgrade. You’re also welcome to visit the membership counter next time you’re at a Costco warehouse. Or, contact us.

Where can I find information about Costco Travel?

Body

For more information on our Costco Travel program, please visit our Costco Travel website at www.costcotravel.com.

Costco Travel can also be reached by calling them directly at 1-877-849-2730.

Where can I find information about the Costco Auto Program?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Where can I find information about the Costco Auto Program?

Body

All Costco members have the option of substantial savings on their next vehicle purchase. You also can save 15% on parts, service and accessories at participating service centers nationwide. You’ll find all the details on the Costco Auto Program website. Or, if you’re on Costco.com, just enter the terms “Auto Buying” into the search engine near the top of the page.

For additional assistance, feel free to call the Costco Auto Program at 1-800-755-2519 (Monday to Friday from 6 a.m. to 7 p.m. and on weekends between 7 a.m. to 5 p.m. PT).

Costco Career FAQs

Updated 02/20/2025

Title

Costco Career FAQs

Body

How do I apply for a job at a U.S. Costco?
What is the status of my online employment application?
How can I get help with my online employment application?
Where can I find employment information about Club Demonstration Services (CDS)?

HOW DO I APPLY FOR A JOB AT A U.S. COSTCO?

For information about employment at Costco and to access an online application, please visit the Costco careers page.

WHAT IS THE STATUS OF MY ONLINE EMPLOYMENT APPLICATION?

Application updates aren’t sent out by email or via messaging apps, so we would recommend contacting the location you applied to directly. You can find the location and phone numbers of all locations right here.

HOW CAN I GET HELP WITH MY ONLINE EMPLOYMENT APPLICATION?

If you’re experiencing technical difficulties while applying online, please contact Costco Human Resources at 1-800-284-4882 option 2 and then option 3. You can also find additional information about employment at Costco and links to our online applications here.

WHERE CAN I FIND EMPLOYMENT INFORMATION ABOUT CLUB DEMONSTRATION SERVICES (CDS)?

CDS is the company that employs the people who provide product samples in our warehouse locations. They work to create meaningful connections with our members to influence purchase decisions and increase sales. If you’re interested in joining the team, please visit CDS to learn more.

Does Costco Customer Service offer email support?

Updated 03/03/2026

Title

Does Costco Customer Service offer email support?

Body

Costco does not offer email support. In order to provide real-time assistance to our members, we currently offer phone and chat support.

Please use our Feedback options to provide us with your comments and suggestions.

How can I disable my pop-up blocker to submit a rebate form?

Updated 09/01/2023

Title

How can I disable my pop-up blocker to submit a rebate form?

Body

When submitting a rebate on Costco.com, the pop-up blocker on your computer will need to be disabled. Or, you can hold the CTRL key while selecting “OK” to allow the pop-up window.

If your pop-up blocker is already disabled and you continue to have difficulty submitting through our website, there may be additional settings on your computer that are keeping the link from opening.

If you believe that’s the case, you can go directly to our Manufacturer Rebates page to submit your rebate.

You can also submit rebates through standard mail. Please mail your completed rebate form to:

Costco Wholesale
C/O APF
P.O. Box 1015
Grand Rapids, MN 55745

How can I get the advertised instant savings on Costco.com?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

How can I get the advertised instant savings on Costco.com?

Body

For products on Costco.com with instant savings, there will not be a coupon code for you to enter. The instant savings will be automatically deducted during checkout and then reflected in the cart.

How can I place an order to ship to multiple addresses?

Updated 04/09/2026

Title

How can I place an order to ship to multiple addresses?

Body

Sending orders to multiple addresses on Costco.com is only a matter of a few clicks. Here’s what to do:

  1. Add the items you’re ordering to your cart and select View Cart to determine if they are eligible to ship to multiple addresses.
  2. Next, select Account, Address Book, then Add New Address.
  3. Add all of the addresses you will be shipping the items to and once complete, select Cart.
  4. Once you’re back in the cart, select Checkout.
  5. Select Ship to Multiple Addresses.
  6. Select a saved address from the dropdown menu for each item in your cart.
  7. When you’re finished, select the Continue button and finish placing your order.

How can I view Costco.com Member Only Item pricing?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

How can I view Costco.com Member Only Item pricing?

Body

It is our mission to continually provide our members with quality goods and services at the lowest possible prices. Our members now have access to Member Only Items on Costco.com.

Purchasing a Costco Membership:

To purchase a Costco membership online, visit Costco.com, select Membership near the top of our webpage, then click Shop Memberships. Select your membership of choice: Executive, Business or Gold Star. Complete the checkout process. You will immediately receive an order confirmation email that will include your new membership number.

Please remember to update your online Account Details to include your membership number once you receive it.

Viewing Details of Member Only Items:

You must be logged in to Costco.com and have a valid Costco Membership number saved in your online account to view pricing and/or purchase Member Only Items. When viewing product details, you will be prompted to login to your account to display pricing and enable the Add to Cart button.

If you are currently a Costco member attempting to purchase a Member Only item and are unable to see the price, a simple update to add your membership number to your account is required.

Updating Your Costco.com Account:

To update your account information:

  1. Select Sign In / Register found in the upper right-hand corner of the Costco.com homepage.
  2. Sign in using your email address and password.
  3. Choose Account Details in your Account.
  4. Select Edit next to Membership Number and enter your 12 digit Costco Membership number. Do not enter the prefix “7111” if it appears as part of your membership number.
  5. Select Update. Your membership number will now be linked to any order you place on Costco.com.

Upon your return to the product page, pricing will be available. You may now add the item to your cart and complete the checkout process.

How do I find Costco member-only savings online?

Body

To search for specific Costco member-only savings items online, head over to Costco.com and enter either the product name or item number into the search engine. If the product doesn’t come up in search, that means the product is currently unavailable for purchase on Costco.com.

If the item is currently unavailable, please check back frequently as the website is updated daily. Also, please be sure to look for the “valid-through” dates shown on each savings flyer.

Another option is to select View Warehouse Savings, which can be found at the top of our home page. There, plenty of savings are available for online items, too. Savings that can be applied to purchases made online will include a red icon that reads “Shop Now.” Savings that are valid only on Costco.com can be found toward the end of the collection.

Finally, we encourage you to check out any limited-time offers (also listed on our homepage).

Why am I experiencing an error while attempting to chat?

Updated 05/29/2026

Title

Why am I experiencing an error while attempting to chat?

Body

We apologize if you are experiencing errors with chat. The following steps may help to resolve the issue:

  1. We recommend clearing the cache and cookies in your internet browser.
  2. Ensure you’re only logged into one electronic device at a time. For example, if you logged into our website on your tablet, but switched to your laptop.
  3. You can try “forgetting” your current Wi-Fi network, then re-connect (make sure you know your login credentials!).
    • For iOS devices, you can also try disabling Wi-Fi networking by going to Settings > Privacy > Location Services > System Services.
  4. Make sure your browser has been updated to the most recent version. Older versions may cause errors. Our website supports the following:

Desktop browsers:

  • Internet Explorer (version 9 and above)
  • Microsoft Edge
  • Safari (version 9 and above)
  • Firefox
  • Chrome

Mobile browsers:

  • iOS Safari (version 9 and above)
  • Chrome

If you’re still encountering issues with chat after trying the steps above, please give us a call. Our representatives will gladly assist you! In addition, please help us improve our chat service by sharing your feedback.

Why am I experiencing site errors?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Why am I experiencing site errors?

Body

We apologize if you are experiencing errors on our website. The following steps may help to resolve the issue:

  1. We recommend clearing the cache and cookies in your internet browser.
  2. Remove the item from your cart and then add it again. If you’re encountering difficulties while attempting to check out, this may work as a reset and correct the error.
  3. Ensure you’re only logged into one electronic device at a time. For example, if you logged into our website on your tablet, but switched to your laptop.
  4. Make sure your browser has been updated to the most recent version. Older versions may cause errors. Our website supports the following:

Desktop browsers:

  • Microsoft Edge
  • Safari (version 9 and above)
  • Firefox
  • Chrome

Mobile browsers:

  • iOS Safari (version 9 and above)
  • Chrome

If you’re still encountering errors after trying the steps above, please contact us. Our representatives will gladly assist you!

Why am I not receiving emails from Costco?

Updated 03/21/2024

Title

Why am I not receiving emails from Costco?

Body

If you use Gmail or Yahoo Mail, emails from Costco about the latest news, promotions, special offers and other information will have an unsubscribe link listed next to the sender’s name/email address (costco@digital.costco.com).

If you select this unsubscribe option, your email address will be forever opted out from receiving such emails, even if you opt back in at a later time. If you wish to begin receiving emails from Costco again, you can sign up using a different email address.

Why does it say my email address is already in use?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Why does it say my email address is already in use?

Body

It’s possible that your email address has already been registered in our system. To determine if this is the case, head to our sign-in page and enter your email address. When asked for your password, try a password that you might have used when registering.

If that doesn’t work, try resetting your password. Here’s how:

On the sign-in page, select Forgot Password?, enter your email address, and hit Send Verification Code. Once you’ve done so, we’ll send you an email with a verification code to complete the process. Please keep in mind that this link will expire after 15 minutes, so the sooner, the better! If you have any trouble finding the email, it’s always a good idea to check your spam folder. Enter the verification code, then select Verify Code. Then, enter your new password and select Update.

Discounts, Savings, & Promos

Executive Membership Instacart Offer: Monthly Credit of $10 Off $150+ (pre tax) FAQ

Updated 06/30/2025

Title

Executive Membership Instacart Offer: Monthly Credit of $10 Off $150+ (pre tax) FAQ

Body

Answers to frequently asked questions regarding Instacart orders for Executive Members are below:

Do you need to be a Costco Member?
I’m a Costco Executive Member; how do I use the Executive Member benefit?
How do I connect my Costco Executive Membership to my Instacart account?
I’m a Costco Gold Star Member. How do I become an Executive Member?
How do I redeem my credit?
Is sameday.costco.com delivery available in all areas?
When is the promotion valid?
Is this offer available on Costco.com?
Is this offer available on Instacart Marketplace?
Is a sameday.costco.com account required for purchase?
Are there any exclusions?
Can multiple transactions count towards the promotional total?
Can you use the credit more than once?
Is there a minimum spend to qualify for this promotion?

Do you need to be a Costco Member?

Yes. An active Costco Executive Membership is required.

****I’m a Costco Executive Member; how do I use the benefit?

Head to sameday.costco.com and sign in to costco.com account (or create one!). From there, build your basket to $150+ pre tax (excluding alcohol and prescription medications items), and the $10 credit will automatically be applied at checkout.

How do I connect my Costco Executive Membership to my Instacart account?

Head to instacart.com/costco, and select “Join Costco Today” underneath the Costco logo. From there, select “Link Membership” and follow the instructions. Once a valid Executive Membership number is linked, the $10 credit will automatically apply to eligible order totals of $150+ pre tax.

I’m a Costco Gold Star Member. How do I become an Executive Member?

Head to costco.com/upgrade-membership.html, and follow the steps to upgrade your membership. Once you’re upgraded to Executive, enter your membership number on Instacart.com/costco or at sameday.costco.com, and the credit will automatically apply to eligible order totals of $150+ pre tax.

How do I redeem my credit?

The $10 credit will automatically apply at checkout on eligible orders of $150+ pre tax.

Is sameday.costco.com delivery available in all areas?

No. sameday.costco.com is not available in all regions. Please enter your delivery address on the site to see if delivery is available in your area.

When is the promotion valid?

Credits will be applied to valid Executive Members’ Same-Day or Marketplace accounts on the first of every month, and will expire on the last day of each month.

Is this offer available on Costco.com?

No. This offer is only available on sameday.costco.com with an active Costco Executive Membership and a sameday.costco.com account.

Is this offer available on Instacart Marketplace?

Yes. The credit is available on sameday.costco.com with an active Costco membership and instacart.com/costco (with a valid Costco Executive Membership linked).

Is a sameday.costco.com account required for purchase?

A sameday.costco.com account is required for purchase.

Are there any exclusions?

Yes. Alcohol and prescription medications do not count towards the $150+ pre tax order minimum.

Can multiple transactions count towards the promotional total?

No. A single transaction of $150+ pre tax is required.

Can you use the credit more than once?

No. The credit is only eligible for one order of $150+ pre tax each month. Credits will not roll over to the next month, but a new credit will be applied on the first of each month.

Is there a minimum spend to qualify for this promotion?

Yes. The minimum order total must reach $150+ pre tax on eligible merchandise (after discounts and tip), excluding alcohol and prescription co-pays.

Instant Savings Coupon - You’ve Received a Costco Shop Card Letter or Email

Updated 12/26/2025

Title

Instant Savings Coupon - You’ve Received a Costco Shop Card Letter or Email

Body

We experienced a temporary system issue that impacted select instant savings coupons on November 13, 2025. We notified affected members by letter and email; we sincerely apologize for the error.

Members who were notified by email should expect to receive it by Saturday, November 22nd, 2025 and will find a Digital Shop Card included in their email. For those members without an email address on file, a letter containing a physical Shop Card will be sent via USPS during the week of November 24.

Each Shop Card will be issued for the full value of the instant savings coupon, including any applicable sales tax.

Emails will come from costco@digitalshopcard.costco.com with the subject line: “You just received a Costco Digital Shop Card.”

FAQs:

I purchased more than one affected item that qualified for the instant savings on 11/13/25. Does my Shop Card amount include more than one item?

Yes, if you purchased more than one quantity or more than one item that qualified for the instant savings on 11/13/25, your Shop Card amount includes the value of those combined savings coupons.

Were the affected instant savings available for in warehouse purchases and/or online at Costco.com?

The affected coupons were for in-warehouse purchases only.

What items were affected?

There were 138 items affected. You can view your warehouse receipt from your purchases on 11/13/25 by selecting Orders & Returns in the upper right corner of the Costco.com home page. Under Orders & Purchases, select the In-Warehouse tab. For additional assistance on how to view your receipts, view this article.

How do I know which products were affected?

You can find a list of impacted products here.

Tire Center FAQs

Updated 06/10/2024

Title

Tire Center FAQs

Body

Answers to frequently asked questions about the Tire Center are below:

How can I schedule an appointment at my local Tire Center?
What services does the Costco Tire Center provide?
How are Costco.com tire orders delivered and installed?
What is Costco’s tire replacement location policy?
How can I find tires on Costco.com?
Can I cancel my tire order?
Can I exchange my tire order?
Will the Costco Tire Center install any size tire?

Can I put air in my tires at Costco?

HOW CAN I SCHEDULE AN APPOINTMENT AT MY LOCAL TIRE CENTER?

Scheduling an appointment at your local Tire Center is easy! Simply visit Costcotireappointments.com and follow these steps:

  1. If you’ve created a Costco.com account in the past, you can Login using the email address and password you previously created.
  2. Your default Tire Center location will be selected. If you need to change the location, select “Change” (if you ordered new tires on Costco.com, you should select the same location where your tires will be delivered).
  3. Select your vehicle type. Then, select “Schedule an Appointment”.
  4. Select your desired services (if you ordered new tires and need them installed, select “Costco Purchased New Tire Installation”. You will be asked to provide the date you purchased your tires).
  5. Select your desired appointment date and time.
  6. Enter your contact information and if you’d like any additional Add on Services. If you’d like a text message reminder, check the appropriate box.
  7. Click “Schedule Appointment”. You’ll receive a confirmation email immediately, and you’ll also get a reminder email the day before your appointment.

If you ever need help scheduling an appointment, feel free to get in touch with the Tire Center at your local Costco.

WHAT SERVICES DOES THE COSTCO TIRE CENTER PROVIDE?

The Costco Tire Center offers several installation and maintenance services to get our members on the road, including rotation, balance, nitrogen inflation, nitrogen conversion, and flat repair. Your local Costco Tire Center team will be glad to discuss any of these services in-depth. Find your local Tire Center here

Please note that:

  • Costco will not install golf cart or ATV tires
  • Costco will not install UTV tires that will be used in street applications
  • Costco will install any loose trailer tire with the exact same size and load index tire that is currently on the wheel
  • Costco will only install trailer tires on a trailer if the owner of the trailer has the documentation stating the wheel torque specification from the trailer manufacturer
  • Costco will only install Costco-purchased tires

HOW ARE COSTCO.COM TIRE ORDERS DELIVERED AND INSTALLED?

Your Costco.com tire order will arrive at your selected warehouse in approximately 5-10 business days from the time of your order. You will be notified by phone or email when your tires arrive at the warehouse and you will have 14 days for installation.

For Alaska and Hawaii orders, the estimated delivery timeframe will be approximately 10-20 business days from the time of your order.

For additional details please head to our Tire Site and select your warehouse location to view the tire warranty and Tire Center FAQs.

Please note that:

  • Golf, Trailer and ATV tires must be shipped directly to your home and do not include installation fees
  • Costco will not install golf cart or ATV tires
  • Costco will not install UTV tires that will be used in street applications
  • Costco will install any loose trailer tire with the exact same size and load index tire that is currently on the wheel
  • Costco will only install trailer tires on a trailer if the owner of the trailer has documentation stating the wheel torque specification from the trailer manufacturer
  • Costco will only install Costco-purchased tires

WHAT IS COSTCO’S TIRE REPLACEMENT LOCATION POLICY?

We have a policy to always install the least worn tires to the rear axles on the vehicle. For example, if only one tire needs to be installed, it will be installed on the rear of the vehicle. If two new tires are installed, they will also be installed on the rear of the vehicle.

This is based on major tire manufacturers’ fitment guidelines, which specifically caution against installing replacement tires on the front axle, due to safety reasons. If you have further questions, please visit our Tire Site.

HOW CAN I FIND TIRES ON COSTCO.COM?

You can find the exact tires for your vehicle as set by the tire manufacturers right here. Your safety is our top priority. As part of that commitment, our Tire Centers will only install tires that are suggested by the manufacturer for your make and model of car. We’ve designed the tire search on Costco.com to only display results that match up with your vehicle.

Once you select the warehouse you’d like to install your tires, simply enter the specific year, make and model of your vehicle in our tire search, and you should have some great options at your fingertips.

CAN I CANCEL MY TIRE ORDER?

We are unable to cancel tire orders that have already been shipped and are in transit to your local Costco Wholesale Tire Center.

If you don’t want to keep the tires, there are two return options:

  • For an immediate refund, stop by the Costco Wholesale Tire Center when the tires arrive.
  • Contact Us, and we’ll submit the refund request for you. If you’d like the refund processed by Costco.com, you’ll receive your credit back to your original method of payment within three to four business days.

CAN I EXCHANGE MY TIRE ORDER?

We don’t have the ability to process exchanges for tire orders placed on Costco.com. If you’re not completely satisfied with your order, stop by the Costco Wholesale Tire Center when the tires arrive for a refund. If you’d like the refund processed by Costco.com, Contact Us and you’ll receive the credit back to your original method of payment within three to four business days.

If you return or cancel your tire order and you need a different set of tires, you’re welcome to place another order on Costco.com.

WILL THE COSTCO TIRE CENTER INSTALL ANY SIZE TIRE?

Costco only installs authorized tire fitments, for safety reasons. This is why we advise that, before purchasing, our customers make sure the tire(s) they select meet the following vehicle manufacturers’ Original Equipment (OE) specifications.

  • It must be a size authorized by the vehicle manufacturers.
  • It has an equal or greater OE Speed Rating. Not all tires have a speed rating requirement.
  • An equal or greater OE Load Index. If you are unsure of your vehicle’s fitment requirements, please contact a local Costco Tire Center sales associate.

If you’d like to find your vehicle’s tires or need more information, visit our Tire Site.

CAN I PUT AIR IN MY TIRES AT COSTCO?
There is a self inflation station at our locations that is open during the same hours as our gas station.

Digital Membership Card - Payment FAQs

WHAT IS THE DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD?

Through the Costco Mobile App, you can access a digital version of your membership card. Costco’s app will also allow you to renew your membership and view your 2% reward balance on your phone.

HOW DO I DOWNLOAD MY DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD?

In order to access your Digital Membership Card, you must have an active Costco membership and a mobile device running supported iOS or Android operating systems. Please note that the Digital Membership Card will not operate on tablet devices.

To access your Digital Membership Card:

  1. Download the newest version of the Costco app from the App Store® or the Google Play™ store.
  2. Launch the Costco app, then tap the Card icon at the bottom of the screen. The login page will appear.
  3. Follow instructions on the login page to sign in with an existing Costco.com account. If you do not have an existing Costco.com account, create one now. Each Digital Membership Card is tied to a specific Costco.com account. You cannot use the same email address for multiple Digital Membership Cards.
  4. Verify your membership. If you have not previously verified your membership, you will be prompted to do so. To learn more about verifying online, click here.
  5. View your Digital Membership Card. After successfully verifying your membership, the Digital Membership Card will appear on your screen.

I HAVE MORE THAN ONE PERSON ON MY MEMBERSHIP. WHY CAN’T I SEE ALL THE DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARDS IN THE APP?

Each Digital Membership Card will allow for one individual’s membership card per device. Though Primary and Household members are on the same membership, each person has a unique membership number. Each member must have a separate Costco.com account to access a Digital Membership Card. Only Business members who also have an additional membership linked to the same account may see multiple membership cards on a single Digital Membership Card account.

If your Household member would like to access their Digital Membership Card on their device, they will need to create their own Costco.com account with their unique membership number. Learn how to create your own Costco.com account here.

HOW DO I UPDATE MY DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD PHOTO?
Please visit your local warehouse membership counter to update your photo. (Note: if you are a Costco Anywhere Visa® credit card holder, taking a new photo will automatically issue you a new credit card. The credit card number will not change.)

WHAT SHOULD I DO IF MY DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD IS DISPLAYING INACCURATE INFORMATION?

If your Digital Membership Card displays inaccurate information, please visit your local Membership counter or contact us using the link on the left of this page to update your information.

HOW DO I TRANSFER MY DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD ONTO A NEW PHONE?

On your new phone, open your Costco App and tap the membership tab. You will be prompted to transfer your Digital Membership Card onto your new device. You can transfer your digital card to a new phone one time before you need to visit the Membership counter or contact us using the link on the left of this page to reset your digital card. Transfers include uninstalling and reinstalling the app, even on the same phone. Screenshot versions of the digital card cannot be scanned at Costco registers.

WHAT IF MY PHONE IS LOST OR STOLEN?

You will need to transfer your Digital Membership Card to your new phone at the membership counter of your local Costco. Once you do that, you can repeat the steps to add your Visa card.
You’ll also want to change your Costco.com account password and reinstall your digital card on your new phone.

CAN I PAY USING MY DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD?

If you have a Visa card, you can add it as a payment method to your Digital Membership Card. Read more details here.

CAN I ADD THE DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD TO MY APPLE WALLET?

The Digital Membership Card is designed to offer members advanced features and will not be available in Apple Wallet.

WHERE CAN I USE MY DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD?

You can use your Digital Membership Card at any of the registers in the following areas:

  • Warehouse entry
  • Check-out (assisted and self check-out lanes)
  • Returns
  • Pharmacy
  • Hearing Aid
  • Optical
  • Tire Center
  • Gas Station

CAN I STILL USE MY PHYSICAL MEMBERSHIP CARD?

Yes, your physical card will still be accepted at all locations.

Digital Membership Card - Payment FAQs

Updated 06/19/2026

Title

Digital Membership Card - Payment FAQs

Body

Answers to frequently asked questions about Payments in the Digital Membership Card are below:

What credit card can I add to my Digital Membership Card?
How do I add my credit card to my Digital Membership Card?
Can I add multiple credit cards to my Digital Membership Card?
Do I need to be connected to the internet to use my Digital Membership Card?
What if my card is lost or stolen?
What if my phone is lost or stolen?
Can I add a temporary credit card/instant credit to my Digital Membership Card?

Digital Membership Card - General FAQs

WHAT CREDIT CARD CAN I ADD TO MY DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD?

Currently, the Digital Membership Card is only compatible with any Visa card.

Note: You can only add a Visa debit or credit card associated with a U.S. billing address.

HOW DO I ADD MY CREDIT CARD TO MY DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD?

  1. Make sure you’re connected to the internet.
  2. Sign in to the mobile app and open your Digital Membership Card.
  3. Select Add Payment.
  4. Enter your Visa Card’s Expiration Date, CVV and ZIP Code.
  5. Tap Save.
    • You may be asked to verify your card by following these steps:
      1. Select a method to receive a verification code, then select Continue.
      2. Enter the verification code that’s sent to you, then select Continue.

Your Visa Card is now an active payment method in your Digital Membership Card and is ready to be used at Costco registers.

CAN I ADD MULTIPLE CREDIT CARDS TO MY DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD?

Not at this time.

DO I NEED TO BE CONNECTED TO THE INTERNET TO USE MY DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD?

You must be connected to the internet when adding your Visa card to your Digital Membership Card. You do not need an internet connection to view your Digital Membership Card; however to use your Digital Membership Card as payment, it will need to be connected via the internet for the payment token to refresh.

WHAT IF MY CARD IS LOST OR STOLEN?

If your Visa card is lost or stolen, please report it to your credit card provider. They will issue you a new credit card. Once you receive your new credit card in the mail, you can add it to your Digital Membership Card.

WHAT IF MY PHONE IS LOST OR STOLEN?

You will need to transfer your Digital Membership Card to your new phone at the membership counter of your local Costco. Once you do that, you can repeat the steps to add your Visa card.

CAN I ADD A TEMPORARY CREDIT CARD/INSTANT CREDIT TO MY DIGITAL MEMBERSHIP CARD?

No. You’ll need the credit card’s expiration date and CVV in order to add it to the Digital Membership Card.

WHAT IF I RECEIVE AN ERROR MESSAGE?

During the process of adding your Visa Card to your Digital Membership Card, you may encounter error messages which are explained below. Please note that if you receive an error message and then choose to select Back to Digital Membership, you will be taken back to the Digital Membership Card screen without your credit card being added to your account. You will then have to start the process again from the beginning.

Verification Code Error Message:
If you enter the verification code too many times you will receive an error message. As a security measure, once you have exceeded the maximum number of attempts, you will have to log out and then back in to try again.

CVV Code Error Message:
If you enter the CVV code too many times you will receive an error message. As a security measure, once you have exceeded the maximum number of attempts, you will need to wait 24 hours before trying again.

Credit Card Error Information:
If you receive an error message while entering the credit card information, it’s possible that you have entered the card information incorrectly. Please verify and enter correct card information. If you still continue to receive the error message, it may be due to one of the following issues:

  • There is an issue with your information with your credit card provider (you will be asked to call the number on the back of your credit card).
  • The CVV or Expiration Date you have entered is incorrect (you will be allowed to enter it again).

Title

Do I need to renew my membership?

Body

For your security - we’re unable to take payment information over Chat.

The easiest way to renew your membership is during checkout the next time you’re shopping in the warehouse.

Want to do it online instead? Simply sign in, then go to your Costco.com Account and select Renew Membership.

Thank you for your membership - we truly value your business!

How can I add additional members to my Business Membership?

Updated 09/01/2024

Title

How can I add additional members to my Business Membership?

Body

You can purchase additional Business Membership cards at any time during the renewal year. The annual cost is $65 per card (the additional Business Membership card plus one Household Member card, which can be assigned to anyone over 16 at the same address).

There are four ways to add a Business Membership card:

  1. Head to Costco.com and sign in to access your account details. Once your membership has been verified, select the Business Details section to manage your additional cardholders.
  2. Visit the membership counter at any Costco warehouse. You’ll find locations and phone numbers for all of our Costco locations right here.
  3. Give Member Services a call at 1-800-774-2678.
  4. Fax your request to 425-313-6870 (Attn: Member Services) on company letterhead.

    If you’re faxing a letter, be sure to include the names, addresses, and phone numbers for anyone you’re adding, along with the signature of the Primary Member or Business Account Manager for authorization. Please also list a contact name and phone number—a representative will call to obtain a credit card number to charge any applicable fees.

Title

How can I cancel my Costco Membership?

Body

To cancel a membership, the Primary Member can do one of the following:

  • Speak with a team member at any warehouse membership counter and receive an immediate refund, or…
  • Call our Member Services Center at 1-800-774-2678 and speak to a live agent.

How can I get a replacement Costco Card?

How can I renew my Costco membership?

Updated 03/02/2026

Title

How can I renew my Costco membership?

Body

There are several options for renewal:

  • To renew online, please head here.
  • You’re always welcome to renew your membership in person—simply do so while you’re checking out at a Costco location, it’s that simple!
  • If you prefer a phone call, the number is 1-800-774-2678.
  • If you’d like to renew by mail, please send it to the following address: Costco Wholesale, P.O. Box 34783, Seattle, WA 98124-1783.

Please keep in mind that active memberships can only be renewed 90 days prior to the expiration date.

If your membership is expired or inactive, you can renew online within 90 days after the date of expiration, or any time in person or by phone.

Also, to help make things even easier for you, you can continue using your existing membership cards even after you renew.

How can I reset my Costco.com password?

Updated 08/28/2025

Title

How can I reset my Costco.com password?

Body

If you’ve forgotten your password, please head first to the sign in page. Select “Forgot Password?,” then enter your email address and then hit “Send Password Reset Link.” We’ll email you a reset link which will be good for 12 hours. If you don’t see an email from us, be sure to check your spam folder.

On the sign-in page, select Forgot Password?, enter your email address, and select Send Verification Code. Once you’ve done so, we’ll send you an email with a verification code to complete the process. Please keep in mind that this link will expire after 15 minutes, so the sooner, the better! If you have any trouble finding the email, it’s always a good idea to check your spam folder. Enter the verification code, then select Verify Code. Then, enter your new password and select Update.

In the event that you’d simply like to change your existing password, here’s what to do:

  1. Sign in
  2. Head over to Account Details in your Costco.com Account.
  3. Select Edit next to your password. You’ll be sent a verification code via email.
  4. Enter your verification code.
  5. Enter your new password and select Update.

To review or make changes to your membership information on our website, select Sign In / Register, in the upper right-hand corner of the Costco.com home page.

(Keep in mind, you will need to be the Primary Member or Account Manager and verified before you can review or make any membership changes online.)

Once signed in, select Account in the upper right of the homepage and then select Account Details.

Once on Account Details, you can review information such as:

  • Address
  • Phone number
  • Estimated 2% reward (if you’re an Executive member)

You can use the edit feature to update your information, as well as remove and add cardholders (as allowed by membership type).

How do I register my membership number on Costco.com?

Updated 09/01/2023

Title

How do I register my membership number on Costco.com?

Body

Your membership number is 12 digits long. Membership numbers start with either a 1, 3 or 8 and should be keyed without any preceding zeros.

If you are a registered shopper, please first log in to your Costco.com account. Select Account Details in your Account. There, you can enter your Costco membership number to store for future purchases.

If you have not yet registered for an account on Costco.com, visit our registration page. Select Create Account, then you will be required to provide your email address, create a password, and enter your membership number. When you register, your purchases through Costco.com will automatically be linked to your membership number.

If you are still having trouble registering your membership number online, please feel free to contact us.

How do I register on Costco.com?

Updated 08/28/2025

Title

How do I register on Costco.com?

Body

If you’ve never registered with Costco.com before, you’ll need to create an account. Don’t worry—it’s easy! Here’s how: 1. Go to the Sign in page, which you can access by selecting “Sign In / Register” in the top right corner of our homepage.

Each Costco.com shopper will need to register their online shopping account in their own name.

If you’ve never registered with Costco.com before, you’ll need to create an account. Don’t worry—it’s easy! Here’s how:

  1. Select Sign In / Register in the top right corner of the Costco.com homepage.
  2. Select Create Account, then enter your e-mail address, a password, and — if you’re a member — the number found on your membership card. If you happen to have a Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi, just look for your membership number on the back of the card, directly to the right of your picture.
  3. Once you select the Create Account button, your account will be created.

Please keep in mind that being an active Costco member does not automatically register you on Costco.com. Your membership number is not linked to your account until you add it, either when you register or you update your Account Details in your Costco.com Account.

Is there a fee to upgrade to Executive membership?

Updated 09/01/2024

Title

Is there a fee to upgrade to Executive membership?

Body

To upgrade to an Executive Membership, there is a $65 upgrade fee (plus sales tax). This fee is prorated based upon the number of months left in your current membership year. The annual Executive Membership renewal fee is $130 (plus the fees for any Affiliate Cardholders and sales tax where applicable).

If you’re the Primary Member or Account Manager on your Costco membership, you may upgrade your membership online, in person at your local warehouse, or by calling 1-800-774-2678.

What changes can I make to my Costco membership online?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

What changes can I make to my Costco membership online?

Body

Costco members can make several changes to their membership online, such as:

  • Renew the membership
  • Upgrade to an Executive Membership
  • Add or change a Household cardholder
  • Update the address, phone number and email
  • Change communication preferences
  • Add an Account Manager
  • Add or remove membership Auto Renewal
  • Update Auto Renewal payment method

To get started, visit your Account Details and verify your membership. Or, verify your membership at a Costco membership counter.

What is a Household Card?

Updated 03/03/2026

Title

What is a Household Card?

Body

In addition to their own membership card, Primary Members (and Affiliate Members on Business memberships) receive one free Household Card they can assign to someone over the age of 16 who lives at the same address. When they receive their membership card at the counter, the designated household member must have their photo taken.

To add someone as your Household Cardholder, visit the membership counter of your local Costco. Or, if your Costco.com account is verified, you can manage your Household Card on the Account Details page of your Costco.com Account.

What is needed to change the Primary Cardholder on a Business Membership and make changes?

There are several ways to change the Primary Cardholder for a Business Membership.

  1. The current Primary Cardholder can stop by the membership counter at any warehouse, where a member of our team will be happy to complete the process.
  2. The Primary Cardholder can contact us using the links on the left of this page.
  3. Fax or mail us a letter with the requested changes. Just make sure that it’s on company letterhead and signed by a title holder of the company. Also, please be sure to include their title, the membership number, the business address, and the business phone number.

    For fax, send the request to 425-313-6870 (Attn: Member Services).

    For postal mail, send it to:
    Costco Wholesale

    800 Lake Drive
    Issaquah, WA 98027

Why was the address associated with my Costco membership automatically updated?

Title

Why was the address associated with my Costco membership automatically updated?

Body

Costco utilizes a program through the US Postal Service called the National Change of Address (NCOA). This tool helps us receive address updates for our members.

If you feel that your address has been improperly updated, you can find address update instructions right here.

You can also stop by the membership counter at your local Costco warehouse for assistance.

You’re enrolled in Auto Renew! Email Confirmation

Updated 04/21/2026

Title

You’re enrolled in Auto Renew! Email Confirmation

Body

If you received an email with the subject line “You’re enrolled in Auto Renew!” from the email address noreply-membership@costco.com, it is a legitimate email from Costco.

This email is sent as confirmation that you set your membership up with automatic renewal of your yearly membership fees.

If you do not wish to be set up for automatic renewal of your membership, you can select one of the following methods to remove the the option from your account:

  • Manage your auto renewal online under My Account on Costco.com. For more information on managing your account, see How can I manage membership Auto Renewal online?.
  • Speak with a team member at any warehouse membership counter and receive an immediate refund.
  • Call our Member Services Center at 800-774-2678 or use the Chat Us feature to contact a live agent.

Membership Info

Can I give a Costco membership as a gift?

Updated 11/25/2024

Title

Can I give a Costco membership as a gift?

Body

You can purchase a ‘Gift of Membership’ at the register at your local costco warehouse, or online. Find more information on the Gift of Membership page.

Your recipient will need to bring their Gift of Membership gift card to the membership counter at any Costco location. Their membership officially starts when it’s redeemed.

If the recipient is already an active Costco member, they can still use the Gift of Membership to upgrade to an Executive Membership or renew an existing membership.

Can other people use my membership card?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Can other people use my membership card?

Body

The Costco membership card is non-transferable, but there are several ways to share the experience with family and friends.

Anyone with a card can bring up to two guests to the Warehouse during each visit. Keep in mind that purchasing items is exclusive to Costco members.

If you’re the Primary Member or Account Manager, you can assign your free household card to one other person in your home. That way they’ll also get the benefits of a Costco membership. To add someone, sign in and head to Account Details in your Costco.com Account. You can add a person to your household in your Account Details section, and they can pick up their card at the membership counter of your closest Costco.

Do I need a membership to purchase prescription drugs?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Do I need a membership to purchase prescription drugs?

Body

You don’t need to be a Costco member to purchase Costco Pharmacy prescriptions online or at our warehouses. Our Pharmacy counter will accept several different forms of payment, including cash, debit/ATM cards, Costco Shop Cards, and Visa®.

Do I need to have my photograph on my membership card?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Do I need to have my photograph on my membership card?

Body

Your security is crucial to us. We recognize that identity theft is on the rise, and we want to make sure that you don’t have to deal with it.

To better protect your account, we require your photo to appear on your membership card. Since the membership card is non-transferable, this acts as an extra security measure should your card ever get lost or stolen.

Does Costco offer free or discounted memberships?

Updated 08/29/2023

Title

Does Costco offer free or discounted memberships?

Body

Costco doesn’t offer free or discounted memberships, but rest assured that the cost of membership can be recovered quickly thanks to massive price savings once you start shopping. Not to mention the substantial savings on our many Costco Services, which you can use everyday in your business and personal life.

Our goal is to provide the best products at the lowest prices available—and we mean anywhere. The membership fee is crucial to achieving this goal because it covers our overhead and allows us to operate the business at margins much lower than other retailers (these savings get passed on to you, of course).

How can I buy a membership on Costco.com?

Updated 01/24/2025

Title

How can I buy a membership on Costco.com?

Body

To purchase your membership online, please follow these steps:

  1. Browse your membership options here. You can choose between a Personal Membership and a Business Membership. You can then select an Executive Membership for either type.
  2. Complete the Membership Application on the next page. The application will require you to enter the Primary Cardholder and optional Household Cardholder’s personal information.
  3. Select Save & Continue to continue the process and purchase your membership.
  4. After completing your purchase you’ll receive an email with your new membership number. Please note, membership cards will NOT be mailed to you.
    • You can obtain your new membership cards by visiting the membership counter of your local Costco.

Please be advised that registering to shop online at Costco.com is not an application for membership. Nonmembers may order products from our website as well as members.

Want to buy a membership for someone else? Send them a Gift of Membership card here.

How can I find out when my Costco membership will expire?

There are several options to find out when your membership is ready for renewal, so that you can choose whichever route is easiest for you:

  • Simply stop by our helpful membership counter at any Costco location.
  • Sign In to your Costco.com Account to view your Account Details. Your membership will need to be verified to see your membership information.

And, of course, you’re always welcome to contact us.

How many people can be on my Costco membership?

Updated 03/16/2026

Title

How many people can be on my Costco membership?

Body

A total of two people can be on a Gold Star Membership (either regular or Executive): one Primary Member and one free household member who is over 16 and lives at the same address. If you are the Primary Member, we leave it up to you to decide who receives the second card—something you can change at any time by visiting your Account Details or the membership counter at your local Costco.

The notable difference between Gold Star Membership and Business Membership is that the Primary Member on a Business Membership can add more than one person for an additional fee.

Title

I renewed my membership online, will I get a new membership card?

Body

Fortunately, when you renew, all the cards on your account continue working, so you won’t be sent a new membership card.

However, if you need to replace your card, you’re always welcome to visit our membership desk at any warehouse for a replacement. Our Member Services team will be happy to help.

Membership Scanners at Warehouse Entrances

Updated 07/12/2024

Title

Membership Scanners at Warehouse Entrances

Body

Over the coming months, membership scanning devices will be used at the entrance door of your local warehouse. Once deployed, prior to entering, all members must scan their physical or digital membership card by placing the barcode or QR Code against the scanner*. Guests must also be accompanied by a valid member for entry.

If you have any questions or concerns, there will always be an attendant at the door to assist you!

*If your membership is inactive, expired, or you would like to sign up for a new membership, the attendant will ask that you stop by the membership counter prior to entering the warehouse to shop. Additionally, if your membership card does not have a photo, please be prepared to show your valid photo ID. We encourage you to stop by the membership counter to have a photo taken to have on your card.

What is a Business Membership?

Updated 09/01/2024

Title

What is a Business Membership?

Body

Business Memberships allow you to purchase products for business, personal, and resale use at any Costco Wholesale location worldwide and online at Costco.com. One additional card is also provided for a member of your household at no additional charge. Like all Costco memberships, it’s valid for one year and must be renewed annually.

To sign up, we require some form of business identification, such as a business license, or three pieces of business ID. You can sign up online, but you’ll need to stop by the membership counter at one of our warehouse locations to show your ID and pick up your cards.

Business Members may also add additional cardholders at $65 each per year. Each additional card also includes a free household card.*

You can find Costco’s Membership Terms and Conditions here.

*A household card is available to any cardholder’s spouse, domestic partner, or any immediate family member who is over the age of 16 and living at the same address.

What is a Gold Star Membership?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

What is a Gold Star Membership?

Body

The Gold Star Membership at Costco Wholesale lets you to purchase products for personal use at any Costco location worldwide and online at Costco.com. One additional card is also provided for a member of your household* at no additional charge. Membership is valid for one year and must be renewed annually.

No matter what type of membership you choose, be assured that Costco offers you the highest quality merchandise at the best value with excellent member service.

You’ll find more information about joining Costco right here. And if you would like to review the Privileges and Conditions of membership, you’ll find those here.

*A household card is available to any cardholder’s spouse, domestic partner, or any immediate family member who is over the age of 16 and living at the same address.

What is an Executive Membership?

Updated 03/16/2026

Title

What is an Executive Membership?

Body

It pays to be a Costco Executive Member:

Executive Members earn an annual 2% Reward on qualifying purchases at Costco, Costco.com, and Costco Travel. Plus, additional benefits are available on our suite of Costco Services. We offer a 100% satisfaction guarantee and will cancel and refund membership fees at any time if a member is dissatisfied.

Additional values are available to Executive Members such as earlier shopping hours, lower prices on check printing, auto buying, roadside assistance included at no charge for vehicles covered through the Auto Insurance program and additional travel benefits. View our full list of Costco Services. Additionally, Executive Members can save using Instacart.

Costco membership is $65 a year. An Executive Membership is an additional $65 upgrade fee per year. Each includes a free Household Card. Please include sales tax in all applicable states. (Business Members also pay an additional $65 for each additional cardholder - purchases made by the additional cardholders do not contribute towards the 2% Reward.) Members who upgrade for a partial year will be charged a prorated upgrade fee.

The Primary Member or Account Manager of an existing membership may upgrade to the Executive membership one of the following ways:

  • Online – Sign In or Register on Costco.com, then visit Account Details. Once your membership has been verified, you may upgrade your membership.
  • In Person – Visit any Costco membership counter.
  • By Phone – Call Member Services at 1-800-774-2678.

When to expect a 2% Reward Certificate:

The 2% Reward Certificate is mailed with the renewal notice approximately two months prior to the member’s annual renewal date. A member must be a paid Executive Member at the time the Reward is issued to receive the certificate. Members who reverse their Executive status will lose all rewards accumulated. Rewards are not prorated.

Eligible purchases:

Only purchases made by the Primary and active Primary Household Cardholder on the account will apply toward the Reward. Most U.S. warehouse purchases apply to the 2% Reward. Please see below for a list of ineligible purchases. Eligible purchases made in Canada, Puerto Rico or on Costco.com also contribute to the annual 2% Reward. For eligible Costco Travel purchases, Executive Members will earn a 2% Reward after travel is completed (must be an Executive Member when travel starts).

In most cases, pharmacy and over the counter (OTC) purchases apply to the 2% Reward. Only the amount not covered by insurance (co-pay) is calculated as part of the 2% Reward.

Refunds of eligible purchases will be deducted from the 2% Reward.

Ineligible purchases:

Purchases made in Australia, Japan, Korea, Mexico, Spain, Taiwan, Iceland, China, France and the U.K. will not be applied to the 2% Reward.

The following purchases do not apply to the 2% Reward:

  • Alcohol in AL, AZ, AR, CA, CT, GA, HI, KS, KY, MA, MI, MN, MS, NH, NC, ND, OH, OK, RI, SD, TN, TX, UT, and VT
  • Auto Purchasing
  • Battery Fees
  • Bottle Deposit
  • Cell Phones, Cell Phone Accessories and Cell Phone Service Contracts (purchased through 3rd parties)
  • Costco Shop Cards/Gift of Membership
  • Children’s Miracle Network Hospital Balloons and other charitable giving Costco.com
  • Shipping Fees
  • Costco Services
  • Food Court
  • Gasoline
  • Home Delivery Services (Delivery Charges, Installation)
  • Membership Fees
  • Misc. Taxes & Fees
  • Oil Disposal Fees
  • Optical Exams
  • Stamps
  • Tire Balance & Installation Fees
  • Tire Disposal Fees
  • Tobacco
  • Websites not hosted by Costco.com including online Costco Photo Center
  • 3rd Party Insurance Payments
  • Prescription drug purchases in certain states (including Arkansas, New Jersey, and New York)

What is the difference between each type of membership?

Updated 09/01/2024

Title

What is the difference between each type of membership?

Body

Executive Membership:

  • $130.00 Annual membership fee ($65 membership fee, plus $65 upgrade fee)*
  • Includes a free Household Card
  • Valid at all Costco locations worldwide
  • Annual 2% Reward on qualified Costco purchases (terms and conditions apply)
  • Additional benefits and greater savings on Costco Services
  • Extra benefits on select Costco Travel products

Business Membership:

  • $65.00 Annual membership fee*
  • Includes a free Household Card
  • Add Affiliate Cardholders for $65 each*
  • Valid at all Costco locations worldwide
  • Purchase for resale
  • Business Members must provide Costco with the appropriate resale information

Gold Star Membership:

  • $65.00 Annual membership fee*
  • Includes a free Household Card
  • Valid at all Costco locations worldwide

You can find more details on each type of membership and even sign up right here. Please keep in mind that the Costco double guarantee applies to all membership levels. That means we guarantee both your membership and our products with a refund†, if they don’t meet your satisfaction.

*Plus applicable sales tax.

†Limitations apply. See the membership counter or Costco.com for details.

Where can I find Costco’s Membership Privileges and Conditions?

Membership Privileges and Conditions are linked in the footer of most pages on Costco.com. Scroll to the bottom of the page, find the “Membership” heading and select “Membership Privileges”.

There you will find our terms and conditions of membership, rules for shopping in our warehouses and online, as well as general policies.

Where can I find my membership number?

Updated 09/01/2023

Title

Where can I find my membership number?

Body

You can find your Costco membership number in the following places:

  • On the back of your membership card. It’s a 12-digit number, and you can leave off any preceding 0s.
    • If you have the Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi, it’s located below the signature line, to the right of your photo.
  • On your annual renewal notice, under the “Billing Summary Information.”

If you can’t find your card or renewal notice, you’re more than welcome to visit the membership counter at your local Costco or contact us using the link on the left of this page to obtain your number.

Why does Costco charge an annual membership fee?

Updated 07/10/2024

Title

Why does Costco charge an annual membership fee?

Body

We strive to give our members the best products at the lowest possible prices, and our membership fee allows us to offset many of our operating costs and price our merchandise lower.

Your membership provides access to our tremendous buying power, which results in the lowest possible prices on the items our members want the most. We are committed to providing quality and value on the products we sell with a risk-free 100% satisfaction guarantee on both your membership and merchandise. Limitations apply. See Costco.com for details.

Why haven’t I received Costco Savings Mailers?

Updated 12/20/2024

Title

Why haven’t I received Costco Savings Mailers?

Body

The Costco Savings Booklet is sent to the Primary Member of Executive, Business and Gold Star Memberships of active shoppers. Each Affiliate Cardholder on a Business Membership also will receive the booklet if we have their personal address. If you have one of the above memberships, but still haven’t received your savings booklet, some of the following may have happened:

  • You may have previously opted out of receiving Costco mailers
  • Your mailing address was entered incorrectly
  • Your mailing address changed in the last three months
  • You joined Costco within the last two months
  • Your membership has expired
  • You haven’t purchased an item from the savings booklet for an extended period
  • Your savings booklet was already sent to the address on file (there’s a limit of one savings booklet per address)

If you need to update your mailing address, read the instructions here. If you would like to opt in to receive the savings booklet, visit the membership counter next time you’re at Costco or contact us using the Contact Us link on this page with your request.

Why must I show my membership card?

Updated 03/11/2026

Title

Why must I show my membership card?

Body

Costco is able to keep our prices as low as possible because our membership fees help offset our operational expenses, making our membership fee and structure important to us.

Costco’s membership policy has not changed. We have always asked for membership cards at our registers at time of checkout. Our membership policy states that our membership cards are not transferable and since expanding our self-service checkout, we’ve noticed that non-member shoppers have been using membership cards that do not belong to them. As we already ask for the membership card at checkout, we are now asking to see their membership card with their photo at our self-service checkout registers.

Primary and Affiliate Members can add one free Household Card for anyone over the age of 16 and living at the same address. To make changes to the cardholders on your membership, please review the article: How can I review or make changes to my membership online?

Mobile App Help

Costco Mobile App - Shopping FAQs

Updated 01/28/2025

Title

Costco Mobile App - Shopping FAQs

Body

Where are my orders?
Are there product sharing capabilities?
Can I view my purchases?
Can I check warehouse inventory?
Can I create a shopping list?
How do I add a payment card?
How do I check my Shop Card balance?
Are there receipt printing capabilities?

Costco Mobile App - Account FAQs

WHERE ARE MY ORDERS?

From the Menu go to the Membership section and select Orders & Purchases.

ARE THERE PRODUCT SHARING CAPABILITIES?

Yes, our mobile app offers a “Share” Button on each product page, which allows for sharing our products to social media websites.

CAN I VIEW MY PURCHASES?

From the Menu go to the Membership section. Select Orders & Purchases. You can view purchases made on the mobile app, as well as Costco.com.

CAN I CHECK WAREHOUSE INVENTORY?

For items sold both online and in the warehouse, you may be able to see the product availability in the warehouse on Costco.com. For more information pertaining to viewing warehouse inventory online, please click here.

CAN I CREATE A SHOPPING LIST?

Yes. From the Menu go to the Membership section and select Lists. You will be able to view your current shopping list and create new ones.

HOW DO I ADD A PAYMENT CARD?

From the Menu go to the Membership section. Select Account then Payment Methods. You may then edit or remove a card to be used for online orders.

HOW DO I CHECK MY SHOP CARD BALANCE?

From the Menu go to the Membership section and select Shop Card Balance. Enter the card number and PIN, then select Check Balance.

ARE THERE RECEIPT PRINTING CAPABILITIES?

No. Currently, there are no receipt printing capabilities associated with the mobile app. We look forward to offering this option in the near future.

Custom Cake Ordering in the Costco App FAQs

Updated 07/15/2026

Title

Custom Cake Ordering in the Costco App FAQs

Body

Where can I locate this feature in the Costco app?
Can I place a custom cake order on Costco.com?

WHERE CAN I LOCATE THIS FEATURE IN THE COSTCO APP?

The feature can be found under the Warehouse Ordering section of the Warehouse tab in the Costco app.

CAN I PLACE A CUSTOM CAKE ORDER ON COSTCO.COM?

Custom cake ordering is only available through the Costco app at this time.

I DON’T SEE THE OPTION FOR CUSTOM CAKE ORDERING IN THE COSTCO APP, WHAT SHOULD I DO?

If the option isn’t appearing under the Warehouse tab, ensure you have the most updated version of the Costco app.

ARE THERE ANY REQUIREMENTS TO PURCHASE A CUSTOM CAKE ORDER THROUGH THE COSTCO APP?

To place a custom cake order, you must be signed in to the Costco app and connected to a verified membership.

HOW QUICKLY CAN CUSTOM CAKE ORDERS BE MADE AFTER BEING PLACED?

Custom cake orders must be placed at least three days in advance.

At this time, custom cakes can be ordered up to two weeks in advance.

CAN I CANCEL OR MAKE CHANGES TO MY CUSTOM CAKE ORDER?

After a custom cake order has been placed, you will receive an order confirmation email. This email will provide details such as warehouse and pick-up instructions, the estimated total and information on how to cancel or modify the order.

CAN OTHER COSTCO BAKERY ITEMS BE PURCHASED IN ADVANCE?

At this time, only cookie trays and custom cakes can be ordered online through the Costco app.

WILL THE COSTCO BAKERY STILL TAKE PAPER ORDERS IN THE STORE?

Yes! We will continue to accept paper orders in the store.

New Age Requirement for the Costco App

Updated 06/22/2026

Title

New Age Requirement for the Costco App

Body

To comply with recent state regulations known as “App Store Accountability Acts” (or similar digital age assurance laws), Costco has updated the access requirements for our mobile application. These state laws are designed to ensure a safer digital environment for minors within mobile app ecosystems.

New App Access Policy

In states where digital access regulations have been enacted, users must be 18 years of age or older to access and use the Costco mobile app.

Please note that while a Costco membership is available to individuals aged 16 and older, this state-mandated age restriction applies strictly to the digital mobile app experience.

How it Works

  • Verification: Your age group is verified securely through your mobile device provider (Apple App Store or Google Play Store) during the app installation.
  • Access Block: If your app store signal indicates you are under the age of 18 and you are located in an applicable state, a restricted access screen will appear, blocking further use of the app.
  • App Closure: In compliance with local legal standards, users who do not meet the age requirement will not have the option to bypass this screen and must close the application.

Shopping at Costco.com
These requirements specifically pertain to mobile apps and DO NOT restrict individuals under the age of 18 from shopping in person or online. If you are under 18 and are blocked from using the app, you can still fully access our services and shop by visiting www.costco.com using a standard web browser on your mobile device or computer.

Frequently Asked Questions
Why am I being asked to verify my age or seeing a restricted access screen?
State laws require app developers to confirm that users meet age eligibility requirements before granting access to their applications. The Costco mobile app does not directly collect your identification; instead, it checks the secure age-range signals provided by the Apple App Store or Google Play Store. If those signals indicate you are under 18 and located in a state with an active law, access is automatically restricted to ensure compliance.

What states have this law?
Several states have recently enacted or are in the process of enacting App Store Accountability Acts and digital age assurance laws. Because these regulations are rapidly changing, please reference your state’s age verification laws to see if your location is applicable.

Does Costco see my sensitive personal data or verify my identity directly?
No. The Costco mobile app does not collect or process sensitive personal information (such as government-issued IDs or your full date of birth) for this process. Age verification is handled entirely by your app store provider, which only passes a limited, non-sensitive signal to Costco confirming your general age category or parental consent status.

Troubleshooting & Access Issues
I am 18 or older (or reside in a non-restricted state), but I am still blocked. How do I fix this?
The Costco app checks your age and residency signals during its initial launch after installation. It does not automatically revalidate your location or profile changes in the background. If you have updated your account details or moved to a different state, you must uninstall and reinstall the Costco app to trigger a fresh check.

What should I do if I am still restricted after reinstalling the app?
If a clean reinstall doesn’t resolve the issue, it is likely due to cached app store data, incorrect account settings, or network routing. Please walk through these steps in order:

Step 1: Verify Account Details

  • Make sure your Apple ID or Google Play account information (specifically your age and country/region) is fully accurate and updated.

Step 2: Check Device Location

  • Ensure your device’s Location Services are turned on and accurately reflect your current state. Turn off any active VPNs, as they can trick the app into thinking you are in a restricted region.

Step 3: Platform-Specific Settings

  • There could be other factors such as refreshing iOS restriction signals, flushing the Google Play Services cache, or parental control settings, etc.

For step-by-step troubleshooting, search the official Apple Support or Google Account Help centers for guides on managing app age restrictions and accessing age-restricted content.

Step 4: Perform a Clean Reinstall

  • Delete the Costco app, restart your mobile device and download the Costco app fresh from your official app store. Try launching the Costco app again.

Technical & System Requirements
What minimum operating system (OS) versions support these age assurance features?

To properly transmit the required legal compliance signals, your device must meet the following minimum software requirements:

  • Apple iOS: iOS 26.2 or later. Earlier versions lack the privacy frameworks required to securely pass age category and regulatory signals to apps.
  • Android: Android 6.0 or higher. Earlier versions do not integrate with the Google Play Age Signals needed for compliance.

Important Note: If your device is running an operating system older than the versions listed above, it cannot generate the necessary compliance signals and the app will remain restricted. Please ensure your device’s software and app store are fully updated to the latest available versions.

Still Need Help?
If you have verified all of your device settings, completed the troubleshooting steps, and are still experiencing a restriction screen, please contact our Customer Service team. To help us assist you quickly, please have a screenshot of the error screen, your device model, and your current OS version ready.

For more details on how Costco handles and protects your data, please review your Privacy Rights.

Costco Wallet FAQs
