Shop Card FAQs

WHAT IS COSTCO WALLET?

Costco Wallet is Costco’s digital wallet where you can easily store one saved payment method and Costco Shop Cards to use at checkout for your online and in-warehouse purchases. The total value of all the stored Shop Cards cannot exceed $10,000 at any given time and there is a $10,000 daily load limit.

HOW DO I ADD SHOP CARD FUNDS TO MY COSTCO WALLET?

In order to utilize the Costco Wallet feature, your membership must first be verified.

Once verified, follow these steps to add Shop Cards to your Costco Wallet:

  1. Go to Account.
  2. Select Costco Wallet.
  3. Scroll down until you see Shop Card.
  4. Select + Add Shop Card.
  5. Enter your Shop Card number and PIN.
  6. Select Add.

If you would like to add more than one Shop Card to your Costco Wallet, repeat steps 1-6 until each has been added. Please note that Costco Wallet has a daily load limit of $10,000 and can store up to $10,000 worth of Shop Cards at any given time.

HOW DO I ACCESS MY COSTCO WALLET?

In order to utilize the Costco Wallet feature, your membership must first be verified.

Once verified, follow these steps to access your Costco Wallet:

  1. Go to Account.
  2. Select Costco Wallet.
  3. Your saved payment method and Shop Card(s) will be displayed.

WHAT IS THE MAXIMUM VALUE OF SHOP CARDS THAT I CAN STORE IN MY COSTCO WALLET?

The total value of all Shop Cards stored in Costco Wallet cannot exceed $10,000 at any given time. There is also a $10,000 daily load limit.

DO PHYSICAL AND DIGITAL SHOP CARDS COUNT TOWARDS THE TOTAL VALUE STORED IN MY COSTCO WALLET?

Yes; both digital and physical Shop Cards count towards the $10,000 maximum amount and daily load limit that can be stored in Costco Wallet.

HOW DO I USE MY COSTCO WALLET BALANCE?

Online:

  1. Go to Account.
  2. Select Costco Wallet.
  3. Toggle on the ‘Enable Shop Card’ feature.
  4. In checkout, the Shop Card funds and saved payment method will be applied to the order total.

At the warehouse:

  1. On the Costco mobile app, select the Account tab.
  2. Next to the Costco Wallet, select the Settings Gear icon.
  3. Toggle on the ‘Enable Auto-Apply’ feature.
  4. At checkout, scan the Digital Membership Card QR code, located on the Account tab.
  5. When prompted, select “Yes” on the PIN pad or screen to use your Costco Wallet balance.

CAN I CASH OUT MY SHOP CARDS THAT ARE IN THE COSTCO WALLET OR TRANSFER THE FUNDS ONTO A NEW SHOP CARD?

Once Shop Card funds have been added to your Costco Wallet, the original card is depleted to show a $0 balance. Costco Shop Cards stored in the Costco Wallet are not redeemable for cash, except as required by law. The following states allow for cash back when your balance drops below a certain amount:

  • Balance less than $15: CA
  • Balance less than $5: CO, CT, HI, MA, ME, MT, NJ, NY, OR, PR, WA
  • Balance less than $2.50: TX
  • Balance less than $1: RI, VT

To transfer the funds from your Costco Wallet onto a new Shop Card, you can use your Costco Wallet funds to purchase a new Costco Shop Card online or at the Member Service area of any Costco location.

WHERE WILL MY REFUND BE ISSUED IF I USED MY COSTCO WALLET FOR THE ORIGINAL PURCHASE?

The destination of your refund depends on the payment method and location of the transaction:

  • Shop Card Payments: If you used funds from a saved Shop Card within your Costco Wallet, the refund will be redeposited directly back to Costco Wallet.
  • Costco.com Purchases:** **For online orders completed with a saved credit or debit card, your refund will be credited back to the specific card used.
  • Warehouse Purchases: If a saved credit or debit card was used for an in-warehouse purchase, the refund will be issued to the Shop Card portion of your Costco Wallet.

WHERE CAN I USE MY COSTCO WALLET?

Costco Wallet can be used at checkout on Costco.com or at the Costco warehouse registers, food court, pharmacy, or gas station.

DO THE SHOP CARD FUNDS IN MY COSTCO WALLET EXPIRE?

No. The Shop Card funds saved in your Costco Wallet do not expire.

However, the ability to redeem your Shop Card(s) in the Costco Wallet may change subject to the cancellation of your membership.

CAN I COMBINE MY COSTCO WALLET FUNDS ACROSS MULTIPLE MEMBERSHIP ACCOUNTS?

No. However, if your membership is reported as lost or stolen, the Shop Card funds saved in the Costco Wallet will still be available through your newly issued membership number.

WHY AM I RECEIVING AN ERROR MESSAGE WHEN ATTEMPTING TO ADD SHOP CARD FUNDS OR OTHER PAYMENT METHODS TO THE COSTCO WALLET?

The Costco Wallet has a daily Shop Card load limit of $10,000. If you exceed this limit, you may receive an error message.

When adding a credit or debit card to the Costco Wallet, verify that your Costco.com billing information aligns exactly with the details your bank has on record.

HOW CAN I VIEW MY COSTCO WALLET TRANSACTION HISTORY?

On a web browser:

  1. Go to Account.
  2. Select Costco Wallet.
  3. Under ‘Shop Card’, select Recent Activity.
  4. The Costco Wallet transaction history is shown in six month increments for up to two years. To change the time frame, select a six month period in the Showing dropdown.

On the Costco mobile app:

  1. Select the Account tab.
  2. Next to the Costco Wallet, select the Settings Gear icon.
  3. Under Shop Card, select Recent Activity.
  4. The Costco Wallet transaction history is shown in six month increments for up to two years. To change the time frame, select a six month period in the Showing dropdown.

WHAT HAPPENS TO MY SHOP CARD AFTER THE FUNDS ARE ADDED TO THE COSTCO WALLET?

After adding a Shop Card to the Costco Wallet, the Shop Card will show a zero balance. Please check the Costco Wallet to ensure the funds were successfully transferred.

IS IT REQUIRED TO ADD SHOP CARDS TO THE COSTCO WALLET?

Yes. To apply a Shop Card to an online order, the Shop Card must be added to the Costco Wallet for verified accounts.

CAN I USE MY COSTCO WALLET FUNDS TO MAKE PAYMENTS FOR HOME INSTALLATION SERVICES?

The Costco Wallet cannot be used as a direct payment method for home installation services. However, you can use your Costco Wallet funds to purchase Costco Shop Cards online or at the Member Service area of any Costco location. Once purchased, these Shop Cards can be applied as a payment method on Costco.com, through the Costco mobile app, or via one of our affiliated home installation dealers.

How can I update my email address that I use to log in to Costco.com?

How can I update my email address that I use to log in to Costco.com?

Body

To update your registered Costco.com email address simply:

  1. Visit the Costco.com home page and select Sign In / Register (located in the upper right-hand corner).
  2. Sign in using the existing email address and password.
  3. Visit Account Details in your Account.
  4. In the Sign In Information section, select Edit next to Sign In Email.
  5. Enter your current email address and select Send Verification Code.
  6. Enter the verification code sent to your email address, then select Verify Code.
  7. Enter your new email address, then select Update.

Updating your email address will change the email used for sign in on Costco.com and not where you receive exclusive offers and member benefits. Please note that agents cannot change a member’s sign in email; if you lose access to your current sign in email, you must register a new Costco.com account using your updated email address.

To subscribe to Costco.com email offers, just follow these steps:

You’re all set! Happy shopping!

How do I unsubscribe from email offers?

Body

There are several ways to opt out of our promotional emails.

  • Option 1: Simply scroll to the bottom of any promotional email and select the “Unsubscribe” link at the bottom to stop receiving Costco offers.
  • Option 2: If you’re a registered shopper on Costco.com, visit Preferences in your Costco.com Account, and uncheck the appropriate box. Note: if you’re receiving Costco emails at more than one email address, you’ll need to go ahead and opt out for each email address.
  • Option 3: Contact Us. Our team is happy to make the email-removal request on your behalf!
  • Option 4: Drop a note to us at Customer Service, P.O. Box 34622, Seattle, WA, 98124.

Passkey Login - General FAQs

Updated 02/25/2026

Title

Passkey Login - General FAQs

Body

Answers to frequently asked questions about logging in with a Passkey are below:

What exactly are passkeys?
How are passkeys different from passwords?
Why should I use passkeys?
Is using a passkey safer than a password?
How do I set up a passkey?
What multi-factor authentication methods are used to create a passkey?
Where can I manage my passkey?
How do I delete a passkey?
What happens if I lose my device with my passkey?
If I create a passkey, will I be forced to use it every time?
Can I use passkeys on all devices?
Can I still use my password to log in
I’m having trouble setting up a passkey. What should I do?
My biometric authentication isn’t working. What should I do?
I don’t see the option to create a passkey in my account settings. What should I do?

WHAT EXACTLY ARE PASSKEYS?

Passkeys are a secure and convenient way to sign into websites and apps without needing to remember traditional passwords. They use cryptographic keys that are unique to you and tied to your device (like your phone or computer). These keys are protected by your device’s built-in security features, such as biometrics (fingerprint or face recognition) or a device PIN.

HOW ARE PASSKEYS DIFFERENT FROM PASSWORDS?

Unlike passwords, which can be guessed, stolen, or forgotten, passkeys are generated using strong cryptography and are unique to each website or app. They’re also resistant to phishing attacks because they’re tied to the specific domain.

WHY SHOULD I USE PASSKEYS?

Passkeys offer several advantages:

  • Enhanced Security: They’re much more secure than passwords, making it harder for hackers to access your account.
  • Convenience: You can sign in quickly and easily using your device’s built-in security features, like fingerprint or face recognition.
  • No More Password Hassles: You don’t have to remember complex passwords or worry about them being compromised.
  • Phishing Resistance: Passkeys are resistant to phishing attacks.

IS USING A PASSKEY SAFER THAN A PASSWORD?

Yes, significantly. Passkeys provide a higher level of security because they rely on cryptographic keys and biometrics, which are much harder to compromise than traditional passwords.

HOW DO I SET UP A PASSKEY?

A passkey can be set up during account creation, sign-in, or through your account settings. The process will guide you through using your device’s built-in security features (biometrics or device PIN). The option to create a passkey will be prompted through the Account tab of your mobile app whether you are logged in or logged out. It will also be available during the signup process.

To set up a passkey in the Costco App:

  1. Select Account in the navigation bar at the bottom of the Costco app
  2. Under Settings, select Sign In & Security
  3. Select Passkeys

To set up a passkey in a web or mobile browser:

  1. On the Costco.com homepage, select Account
  2. Select Account Details
  3. Select Manage Passkeys and follow the provided steps to set up a passkey through your device and/or browser

You can create one passkey per browser and one passkey shared between your mobile devices. For more information on setting up passkey management on your device and/or browser, view the developer links below:

Mobile devices

  • iPhone / iPad
  • Android

Web and Mobile Browsers

  • Google Chrome
  • Safari - iPhone / iPad / Mac
  • Microsoft Edge

WHAT MULTI-FACTOR AUTHENTICATION METHODS ARE USED TO CREATE A PASSKEY?

For the initial passkey creation, we will utilize your device’s built-in multi-factor authentication such as biometric scans (fingerprint or facial recognition) or your device’s PIN. One-time passwords via SMS or push notifications are not available at this time.

WHERE CAN I MANAGE MY PASSKEY?

To manage your passkeys in the Costco App:

  1. Select Account in the navigation bar at the bottom of the Costco app
  2. Under Settings, select Sign In & Security
  3. Select Passkeys where you can view, add, or delete passkeys associated with your account

To manage your passkeys in a web or mobile browser:

  1. On the Costco.com homepage, select Account
  2. Select Account Details
  3. Select Manage Passkeys where you can view, add, or delete passkeys associated with your account

You can create one passkey per browser and one passkey shared between your mobile devices. For more information on managing passkeys on your devices and/or browsers, view the developer links below:

Mobile devices

  • iPhone / iPad
  • Android

Web and Mobile Browsers

  • Google Chrome
  • Safari - iPhone / iPad / Mac
  • Microsoft Edge

HOW DO I DELETE A PASSKEY?

To delete a passkey in the Costco app:

  1. Select Account in the navigation bar at the bottom of the Costco app
  2. Under Settings, select Sign In & Security
  3. Select Passkeys and then follow the on-screen instructions to remove the passkey

To delete a passkey in a web or mobile browser:

  1. On the Costco.com homepage, select Account
  2. Select Account Details
  3. Select Manage Passkeys, then follow the on-screen instructions to remove the passkey

WHAT HAPPENS IF I LOSE MY DEVICE WITH MY PASSKEY?

Passkeys are often synced across your devices if you use an iCloud or Google account. If your device is lost or stolen, you can remove the passkey from your account settings on another device or through your online account. You can also use your password to log in and remove the passkey.

IF I CREATE A PASSKEY, WILL I BE FORCED TO USE IT EVERY TIME?

Once a passkey is created, it will be the default login experience. However, we understand some members may prefer to use passwords. We will continue to support password login for those who choose to use it.

CAN I USE PASSKEYS ON ALL DEVICES?

Passkey compatibility depends on your device and operating system. Most smartphones, tablets, and computers support passkeys. Please ensure your device and browser are up-to-date for the best experience.

CAN I STILL USE MY PASSWORD TO LOG IN?

Yes, you can still use your password to log in if you prefer. We will continue to support password-based logins.

I’M HAVING TROUBLE SETTING UP A PASSKEY. WHAT SHOULD I DO?

Ensure your device’s operating system and browser are up to date. If you’re still experiencing issues, please use one of the Contact Us options on this page.

MY BIOMETRIC AUTHENTICATION ISN’T WORKING. WHAT SHOULD I DO?

Ensure your biometric settings are properly configured on your device. You may need to retrain your fingerprint or face recognition data. If issues persist, try using your device’s PIN.

I DON’T SEE THE OPTION TO CREATE A PASSKEY IN MY ACCOUNT SETTINGS. WHAT SHOULD I DO?

Ensure you have the most updated version of the Costco app and/or your device and browser are up to date. If you need further assistance, please use one of the Contact Us options on this page.

Order Changes

Can I add a gift message to my order after it has been placed?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Can I add a gift message to my order after it has been placed?

Body

Gift messages have to be added at the time of purchase, so we’re unable to add one to the package after the order has been placed.

We can still send a gift message for you, which would be sent via U.S. Postal Service. However, please keep in mind that it may not arrive at the same time as your order.

If you’d like to go that route, please contact us using the link on the left of this page. Once we receive your message request, you’ll receive an email confirmation.

Can I add items to my existing Costco.com order?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Can I add items to my existing Costco.com order?

Body

Once a Costco.com order has been placed, we’re unable to add any new items to it. We recommend placing a new order if you need additional items.

Can I downgrade or upgrade the shipping method?

Updated 09/01/2023

Title

Can I downgrade or upgrade the shipping method?

Body

Once you place your order, you cannot upgrade or downgrade your shipping type preference. However, depending upon how far along your order is in the shipping process, we may be able to cancel it.

If a cancellation is possible, you can then re-order the item using your preferred shipping method (so long as it’s offered). Please refer to the “Shipping and Returns” section on each item’s detail page to view all the shipping options available.

Should you choose to cancel your order, please contact us to determine if this may be possible. We’ll simply need the order number and the name of the item within that order.

Changes That Can Occur With a Shipping Address Change

Updated 12/04/2023

Title

Changes That Can Occur With a Shipping Address Change

Body

The delivery date is based on the availability of the product and the carrier for the address to which your order is being shipped. The taxes and fees applied to a purchase are also determined by the shipping address.

A change to the shipping address will alert our system to recalculate the information and update the delivery date and/or total charges.

Costco Grocery Delivery FAQs

Updated 03/16/2026

Title

Costco Grocery Delivery FAQs

Body

Below are answers to some frequently asked questions about CostcoGrocery:

Why did the item I ordered get cancelled when it shows available on CostcoGrocery?

How can I purchase CostcoGrocery items?

How do I find the CostcoGrocery home page?

Do you charge for delivery?

How will I know when I’ve spent enough to receive free shipping?

Can I purchase Costco.com and CostcoGrocery items at the same time?

Can orders be delivered to multiple addresses?

Will my CostcoGrocery shopping count towards my Executive 2% Reward or 2% cash back through my Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi?

Will I save more money if I purchase my groceries at my local warehouse?

Does CostcoGrocery price match with the warehouse?

If I’m not satisfied with an item, can I return it?

WHY DID THE ITEM I ORDERED GET CANCELLED WHEN IT SHOWS AVAILABLE ON COSTCO GROCERY?

At times, an item you order won’t be available in your area and we’ll be unable to deliver it to you. Rest assured—we’d never want to lose your business, so our fulfillment center will restock the item as quickly as they can. If this happens to you, we’d ask that you attempt your order at a later date.

HOW CAN I PURCHASE GROCERY ITEMS?

Ordering CostcoGrocery items is easy! Simply visit Costco.com and click the dropdown box next to the search bar. Select Grocery and search for the items you’d like.

Any items eligible for 2-day delivery will be tagged with 2-Day Delivery. For more details on the CostcoGrocery program, please head here.

HOW DO I FIND THE GROCERY HOME PAGE?

Any time you’d like to return to the CostcoGrocery home page, just click on Grocery in the blue banner at the top of the Costco.com page. You can also click here.

DO YOU CHARGE FOR DELIVERY?

When we’re able to ship a full box of items, it allows us to cut down on delivery costs. We’ll return those savings to you by waiving the delivery fee! 2-Day Delivery - Our non-perishable pantry items have a $3 per-item delivery fee which is waived when you spend $75 or more, tax not included.

HOW WILL I KNOW WHEN I’VE SPENT ENOUGH TO RECEIVE FREE SHIPPING?

CostcoGrocery 2-Day Delivery has a special tracking feature that’ll show your current order total at the top of the page. It’ll continuously update as you shop, so you’ll always know how close you are to avoiding the delivery fee.

CAN I PURCHASE COSTCO.COM AND GROCERY ITEMS AT THE SAME TIME?

Yes, you can! When checking out, the Costco.com and CostcoGrocery shopping carts are listed separately, so you can easily see which items will be fulfilled by CostcoGrocery and which will be sent through Costco.com.

CAN ORDERS BE DELIVERED TO MULTIPLE ADDRESSES?

Items designated as CostcoGrocery are available for delivery to only one address per order, and no more than 5 different addresses per member in the past month (even if ordered separately, including non-grocery orders).

WILL MY GROCERY SHOPPING COUNT TOWARDS MY EXECUTIVE 2% REWARD OR 2% CASH BACK THROUGH MY COSTCO ANYWHERE VISA® CARD BY CITI?

It absolutely will! If you have an Executive Membership or the Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi, you’ll accrue rewards just as you would if you were shopping at the warehouse.

WILL I SAVE MORE MONEY IF I PURCHASE MY GROCERIES AT MY LOCAL WAREHOUSE?

Our warehouses offer greater savings, but CostcoGrocery offers more convenience. Frequent warehouse shoppers will notice that the CostcoGrocery prices are slightly higher than what they typically see in their local warehouse. This difference accounts for the packaging and delivery of your groceries. For more details on the CostcoGrocery program, please head here.

DOES COSTCO GROCERY PRICE MATCH WITH THE WAREHOUSE?

CostcoGrocery does not price match with the non-delivery prices at the warehouse. It’s our mission to provide quality goods at the lowest possible prices; whether you’re shopping in person or arranging a convenient delivery, we’re dedicated to offering the most competitive prices we can.

IF I’M NOT SATISFIED WITH AN ITEM, CAN I RETURN IT?

Definitely! Our famous Costco Return Policy still applies! Feel free to use any of these options:

  • Visit the Returns Counter of any warehouse.
  • Request a return through Costco.com. Find step-by-step instructions here.
  • Call us at 1-800-955-2292.

How can I add a gift message to my order?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

How can I add a gift message to my order?

Body

We’ve made it easy to share the Costco experience with personalized gift messaging. There are just a few steps.

First, check if a gift message can be included with the item you ordered. You can check the “Product Details” and “Shipping & Returns” tabs on the item’s page to find out. Once you’ve added the item to your cart, simply proceed to checkout and select the desired “Ship To” address. You can then enter a gift message by selecting the “Add Gift Message” link.

A new window will appear, allowing you to enter the recipient’s first and last name, your name, and the desired message you want to send. Once the fields are completed, click “Save” and you’re done! You can continue with the rest of the checkout process and the recipient will receive your personalized message with the order.

How can I change my shipping or billing address on Costco.com?

Updated 04/09/2026

Title

How can I change my shipping or billing address on Costco.com?

Body

Changing your shipping or billing address for an order is as easy as selecting Change in the address box on the Shipping or Payment steps during checkout. If you’ve already placed your order and need to change the shipping address, head here for more details.

If you’d like to change an address you have saved, simply Sign In, visit your Address Book, select Manage, then select Edit on the address you want to update. You can even add a new address, set a different default shipping or billing address, or remove any existing addresses (you’ll need to keep one default address though).

How do I cancel my Costco.com order?

Updated 03/02/2026

Title

How do I cancel my Costco.com order?

Body

To cancel your order through Costco.com, simply follow these steps: Select My Account in the upper right-hand corner of the Costco.com homepage. You can log into your account through

To cancel your order through Costco.com, follow these steps:

  1. Visit Costco.com and select Orders & Returns.
  2. Find the order you want to cancel and select the Cancel Item button.
    • The amount of time you have to cancel will depend on the products you ordered and at what point they are in the shipping process. If you don’t see a “Cancel Item” button or need further assistance with your cancellation, please select a Contact Us option on this page.
    • Note: Due to processing times, the following items may not be eligible for online cancellation:
      • Grocery items
      • Appliance, furniture, or other large freight items
      • Floral items
      • Caskets
      • E-Vouchers
      • Tires
      • Gold and silver bullion
  3. Select the reason for the cancellation and click Cancel Order.

When an order is cancelled, the pre-authorization is immediately released by Costco.com and removed from your financial account based on the policies of the card. For more information on those policies, please contact the credit card company/bank.

Same-Day Delivery Powered by Instacart FAQs

Updated 04/08/2026

Title

Same-Day Delivery Powered by Instacart FAQs

Body

Answers to frequently asked questions about Same-Day Delivery powered by Instacart are below:

Account Sign In / Account Maintenance
How can I contact Instacart regarding my Costco Same-Day Delivery order?
Is Same-Day Delivery offered in my area?
When will my Same-Day order arrive?
Are there additional fees or minimum order requirements for Same-Day Delivery?
Will my Same-Day Delivery shopping count towards my Executive 2% Reward or 2% cash back through my Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi?
Can I shop Costco Same-Day Delivery if I’m not a member?
Can I purchase Costco.com and Costco Same-Day Delivery items at the same time?
Why did Instacart charge me more than the total of my order?
Why did Instacart charge me twice?
Can I use my Costco Shop Card to pay for my Same-day Delivery powered by Instacart order?
How can I get a refund?

ACCOUNT SIGN IN / ACCOUNT MAINTENANCE

  • Your Same-Day account uses information from your Costco.com account.
  • Any account changes must be done on the Costco.com site under “Account Details”, such as membership number, email address, and password.
  • In order for these changes to be reflected on your Same-Day account, you must sign out and sign back into the Same-Day site.
  • To sign out on the Same-Day site on your desktop, click on the black & white account profile icon on the top right hand side of your screen and select “Log out”. If you’re using “Same-Day” through the Costco app on your mobile device, you must log out of your Costco app completely and log back in.
    • If your information is still not updated after logging back in, clear your cache and cookies in the browser or the Costco app (Account > Settings > Reset App Data), whichever channel you are using the Same-Day experience on.

HOW CAN I CONTACT INSTACART REGARDING MY COSTCO SAME-DAY DELIVERY ORDER?

For any order or site support, please contact Instacart Support at 1-888-246-7822 or visit the Help Center page found at https://sameday.costco.com/help{rel=“noopener” target=“_blank”}.

IS SAME-DAY DELIVERY OFFERED IN MY AREA?

Same-Day Delivery Powered by Instacart is available to members in most metropolitan areas. Visit Costco Same-Day Delivery and enter your address to see if delivery is available in your area. All eligible items, including fresh foods, will be listed depending on your address.

If Same-Day Delivery is not available in your area, you may be interested in nationwide CostcoGrocery (excluding AK, HI, and PR). Find out more about CostcoGrocery 2-Day Delivery here.

WHEN WILL MY SAME-DAY DELIVERY POWERED BY INSTACART ORDER ARRIVE?

Orders are delivered by Instacart during the delivery window you’ve selected on the same day, next day, or day of your choice. Contactless delivery is available on orders excluding alcohol or Rx.

ARE THERE ADDITIONAL FEES OR MINIMUM ORDER REQUIREMENTS FOR SAME-DAY DELIVERY POWERED BY INSTACART?

Item prices are marked up higher than your local warehouse. Instacart uses this markup to pay for their delivery service. There’s also a minimum order requirement of $35.

WILL MY SAME-DAY DELIVERY SHOPPING COUNT TOWARDS MY EXECUTIVE 2% REWARD OR 2% CASH BACK THROUGH MY COSTCO ANYWHERE VISA® CARD BY CITI?

Yes! Same-Day Delivery Powered by Instacart shopping counts towards your Executive Reward on the original warehouse sell price, before the markup is added. For even more savings, pay with your Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi to earn 2% cash back.

CAN I SHOP COSTCO SAME-DAY DELIVERY IF I’M NOT A MEMBER?

Costco Same-Day Delivery is only offered to members. If you’re not a member, you can shop directly from Instacart.com, but you’ll pay a higher price than members shopping on Costco Same-Day Delivery.

Find out how to sign up for a Costco membership here.

CAN I PURCHASE COSTCO.COM AND COSTCO SAME-DAY DELIVERY ITEMS AT THE SAME TIME?

No. Your Costco Same-Day Delivery cart is separate from your Costco.com cart, so you cannot purchase Costco.com items and Costco Same-Day Delivery items at the same time.

WHY DID INSTACART CHARGE ME MORE THAN THE TOTAL OF MY ORDER?

Instacart puts a temporary hold on your credit/debit card that includes a buffer to take item additions or substitutions into account. The final charge represents the items picked, and will not include items cancelled due to being out of stock.

WHY DID INSTACART CHARGE ME TWICE?

You may see a temporary hold on your account before the final charge reaches your credit/debit account. The temporary hold should show as “pending,” and will be removed within a day or two.

CAN I USE MY COSTCO SHOP CARD TO PAY FOR MY SAME-DAY DELIVERY POWERED BY INSTACART ORDER?

The Costco Shop Card cannot be used as a form of payment for Same-day Delivery powered by Instacart orders. If you would like to use a Costco Shop Card to pay for your order, we recommend utilizing our 2-Day Delivery option.

HOW CAN I GET A REFUND?

To request a refund for any reason, you must make your request within 7 days of delivery. Please let Instacart know as soon as possible if your order:

  • Is missing items that you were charged for on the final receipt
  • Contains damaged, spoiled, or otherwise unusable items.

Refunds issued by Instacart are processed immediately, but it may take 5-10 business days to see the funds in your bank or credit card account, depending on your bank. You won’t see refunds in your Instacart account. You can also return items directly to a Costco warehouse for a full and immediate refund.

What happens to the bank pre-authorization hold when an order is cancelled?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

What happens to the bank pre-authorization hold when an order is cancelled?

Body

When you place an order, Costco.com contacts the issuing bank to confirm the validity of the payment method. Your bank reserves the funds until the entire transaction processes or the authorization expires. This isn’t an actual charge, it’s just pre-authorization.

When an order is cancelled, the pre-authorization is immediately released by Costco.com and removed from your financial account based on the policies of your bank. For more information on this please contact your bank.

“Perfect” Fit Guide - How do I Prepare for my In-Home Delivery?

“Perfect” Fit Guide - How do I Prepare for my In-Home Delivery?

  • Non-Appliance Guidelines

All-In-One Laundry Appliance Delivery Guidelines

Updated 12/15/2025

Title

All-In-One Laundry Appliance Delivery Guidelines

Body

Before delivery:

  • Verify a 3 or 4-prong grounded electrical outlet and hot and cold water supply with an accessible shut-off on the same floor level as the appliance and preferably within 3 ft. of the appliance location. Also verify, 240V grounded 30 AMP electrical service/outlet or gas supply line with an above shut-off valve is on the same floor as the dryer and preferably within 3ft. of the appliance location
  • Verify the dryer venting within the home is clean and clear vents outside to allow proper operation of the new dryer
  • Ensure water valves are corrosion-free. Signs that a shut-off valve needs replacement include:
    • Leaks or Corrosion: Visible water leaking from the valve or rust/corrosion on the body.
    • Difficulty Turning: The valve is hard to turn, binds, or grinds, indicating internal mineral buildup or damage.
    • Failure to Shut Off Water: The valve does not completely stop the water flow when closed.
    • Age: The valve is an older gate-style valve (prone to failure) or is over 15-20 years old.

Regular maintenance, which involves turning the valves on and off (exercising them) at least once every couple of years, can help prevent them from seizing and extend their lifespan.

  • NOTE: If your home does not have the proper gas, electrical or water supply lines and shut off valves required for your Home Delivery and/or Installation, we will not be able to complete the basic install. If you would like to reschedule your appointment, you can contact us 1-800-955-2292.

Our delivery team will:

  • Attach venting using new components - The delivery team will never use plastic venting or foil vent tubes
  • Connect the appliance per local code, using a new electrical cord or new gas connector. Existing cords and connectors are not permitted for use
  • Connect water, gas, and electrical
  • Level the unit
  • Short cycle test the unit to check for gas and water leaks
  • The delivery team is not able to side vent dryers and laundry centers
  • Be responsible for providing its own personal protective equipment (PPE) (e.g., mask, gloves, booties, etc.) and for complying with any legal requirements surrounding PPE

Haul Away Service Requirements:

Please note the following important information about our Haul Away service:

The delivery teams will not:

  • Haul Away items that are contaminated, infested, or pose a risk to personnel, new products, or equipment
  • Haul Away commercial appliances
  • Relocate the old appliance to a new address
  • Hook up the old appliance at a new location
  • Haul Away your items if the Haul Away unit(s) are not inside your home or your garage due to sanitary reasons
  • Haul Away more units than what is being delivered
  • Hoist merchandise or lift product above their waist

In the event these requirements are not followed, Haul Away will be declined.

Where Haul Away is available, it is on a one-for-one, like item, basis. The item must be ready for pick up at the time of delivery.

The Haul Away unit(s) cannot be returned once it is removed.

General Appliance Delivery Guidelines

Are there shipping limitations on Costco.com orders?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Are there shipping limitations on Costco.com orders?

Body

Currently, Costco.com offers shipping in the United States only. We are unable to deliver to APO (military) boxes, FPO (foreign) boxes, freight forwarders (mail drop locations), or international addresses. Additionally, within the U.S., Costco.com is unable to ship to PO boxes, hotels, and a few limited areas.

If a product is available for shipment to Alaska and Hawaii, there will be additional shipping charges. The charges are calculated for you at the time of checkout

Can I change the delivery address on an In-Home Delivery order I placed?

Updated 12/12/2025

Title

Can I change the delivery address on an In-Home Delivery order I placed?

Body

If your order is being fulfilled by Costco Logistics and doesn’t have a scheduled delivery date, you can change a delivery address within 60 minutes of placing your order. Visit Orders & Purchases and select Order Details on the order you want to update. Under the Shipping Address, select Change.

After 60 minutes, if your delivery address contains a minor error (wrong zip code, street, apt. number, etc.), please contact us so that we can determine if we will be able to make a change on your behalf.

In the event that you require a complete change in your delivery address for your In-Home Delivery & Installation items, please contact us so that we can attempt to cancel the order. That way, you can place a new order using the updated address.

Can I ship my order to a Costco warehouse?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Can I ship my order to a Costco warehouse?

Body

Some products on Costco.com can be shipped to your local Costco warehouse. If an item is eligible for Warehouse Pick Up, it will be indicated on the product page beneath the “Add to Cart” button.

However, most items must be shipped to a home or business. If shipping to your address is not an option for you, please contact us using the link on the left of this page and we’ll be happy to try and help sort out a solution.

Confirming Delivery and Installation Availability

Updated 02/25/2026

Title

Confirming Delivery and Installation Availability

Body

Before we arrive:

  • Make sure an adult 18 years or older, will be present during delivery
  • Any platform necessary to meet required local codes, including any elevation requirement, (proper height above garage floor for gas appliances, etc.) is in place.
  • Ensure that there is a clear path from the street to the residence entrance (clear ice, debris, etc.) and an accessible path through the home (move furniture, toys, etc.).

Our delivery team will:

  • Be responsible for providing its own personal protective equipment (PPE) (e.g., mask, gloves, booties, etc.) and for complying with any legal requirements surrounding PPE
  • Visually inspect the items for damage. If you see damage, contact us immediately for assistance
  • Connect new appliance(s) to existing utilities and test for proper operation (testing to be performed upon confirmation of properly installed utilities)
  • Remove all packaging materials
  • Haul away the appliances being replaced (move 1 item for each like item delivered) or move to a lower floor, basement, or garage. The delivery team is unable to relocate to a different address or re-connect relocated items
  • Recycle your used “haul way” item following strict, environmentally friendly guidelines
  • Provide appliance literature

For your safety, our team cannot:

  • Remove their shoes, but will act with due care to protect your home and merchandise
  • Lift or hoist merchandise over banisters, countertops or other obstructions, or through windows
  • Perform carpentry, electrical, plumbing work, or install appliance trim kits
  • Install a free-standing or slide-in range without installing a range anti-tip device
  • Connect LP gas. LP Gas Conversion kits are not included or provided.

Haul Away Services:
Haul Away of your old appliance will be completed at the time of delivery for basic hookup items such as refrigerators, freezers, or electric dryers/ranges/all-in-ones. Dishwashers, gas appliances, and built-in appliances such as microwaves, wall ovens, and cooktops will be completed at the time of installation if a secondary appointment is necessary.

Note: Haul Away is on a one-for-one, like item, basis. If Costco does not offer installation in your area for wall ovens, dishwashers, cooktops, or over the range microwaves and you want Haul Away, the item should be professionally uninstalled and ready for pickup prior to delivery.

Title

Delivery and Installation Guidelines - Non-Appliances

Body

Note: The following guidelines only apply to freight deliveries performed by Costco Logistics. They do not apply to UPS/FedEx deliveries.

The delivery team will be responsible for providing its own personal protective equipment (PPE) (e.g. mask, gloves, booties, etc.) and for complying with any legal requirements regarding PPE.

For your safety, our team cannot:

  • Remove their shoes, but they will act with due care to protect your home and merchandise
  • Lift or hoist merchandise over banisters, countertops or other obstructions, or through windows to complete a delivery
  • Perform carpentry, electrical, or plumbing work

Cabinets

The delivery team will:

NOTE: Haul Away is not applicable

Including treadmills, ellipticals, exercise bikes, and stationary bikes

Our delivery team will not:

  • Attach and insert chair buckets and bases

TABLES

Dishwashers, Microwaves, Built-in Ovens, Cooktops and Range Hoods Delivery Guidelines

Updated 02/24/2026

Title

Dishwashers, Microwaves, Built-in Ovens, Cooktops and Range Hoods Delivery Guidelines

Body

NOTE: The delivery team cannot connect dishwashers, built-in ovens, cooktops, microwaves or range hoods. Installation is performed by a specialized contractor and may require a secondary appointment separate from delivery. Code upgrades (air gap, GFCI, countertop modifications, etc.) are not included and additional site-specific work can be quoted separately at the time of installation.

Before delivery:

  • Verify 240V grounded, 50 AMP electrical or gas power supply availability
  • Ensure water supply with accessible shut-off valve on the same floor level and/or gas supply with an accessible shut-off valve that is preferably within 3 ft. of the appliance location
  • Ensure water valves are corrosion-free. Signs that a shut-off valve needs replacement include:
    • Leaks or Corrosion: Visible water leaking from the valve or rust/corrosion on the body.
    • Difficulty Turning: The valve is hard to turn, binds, or grinds, indicating internal mineral buildup or damage.
    • Failure to Shut Off Water: The valve does not completely stop the water flow when closed.
    • Age: The valve is an older gate-style valve (prone to failure) or is over 15-20 years old.

Regular maintenance, which involves turning the valves on and off (exercising them) at least once every couple of years, can help prevent them from seizing and extend their lifespan.

  • NOTE: If your home does not have the proper gas, electrical or water supply lines and shut off valves required for your Home Delivery and/or Installation, we will not be able to complete the basic install. If you would like to reschedule your appointment, you can contact us 1-800-955-2292.

Our delivery team will:

  • Connect delivered appliances to existing home utilities
  • Be responsible for providing its own personal protective equipment (PPE) (e.g., mask, gloves, booties, etc.) and for complying with any legal requirements surrounding PPE

Our installation contractor will:

  • Install the appliance if installation services were included in your purchase. The installation service will be a separate appointment with your installation contractor

Standard Installation Includes:

  • Delivery of product to residence / location
  • Inspection of installation work area prior to start of installation
  • Identify any additional work required and review cost prior to performing any work
  • Remove existing unit from accessible location and set aside
  • Hook up new unit to existing code approved utilities
  • Install the included dishwasher installation kit
  • Dispose of job related debris

**Additional installation services that the Installation Contractor may provide at an additional fee:

Note:** Additional services by our contracted providers is completely optional. Members can hire their own contractor to perform these services. However, installation and hookup may not occur if the site is not ready when the contractor arrives.

  • Code upgrades such as air gaps, GFCI outlets, etc.
  • Water line shut-off valve
  • New water line (if not purchased with dishwasher)
  • Cabinet modifications
  • Countertop modifications
  • Electrical upgrades
  • Electrical outlets

Haul Away Service Requirements

Please note the following important information about our Haul Away service:

The delivery teams will not:

  • Haul Away items that are contaminated, infested, or pose a risk to personnel, new products, or equipment
  • Haul Away commercial appliances
  • Relocate the old appliance to a new address
  • Hook up the old appliance at a new location
  • Haul Away your items if the Haul Away unit(s) are not inside your home or your garage due to sanitary reasons
  • Haul Away more units than what is being delivered
  • Hoist Merchandise or lift product above their waist
  • Connect LP gas. LP Gas Conversion kits are not included nor provided

In the event these requirements are not followed, Haul Away will be declined.

Where Haul Away is available, it is on a one-for-one, like item, basis. The item must be un-installed and ready for pick up at the time of delivery.

The Haul Away unit(s) cannot be returned once it is removed.

General Appliance Delivery Guidelines

Does Costco.com ship internationally?

Updated 08/30/2023

Title

Does Costco.com ship internationally?

Body

Currently, products on Costco.com are available for U.S. delivery only. We do offer shipping to Canada. You’ll find those items at our Canadian website: www.costco.ca.

Our Canadian members can also use Costco.com, the U.S. website, when shipping orders to a U.S. address. Costco.com accepts Canadian credit cards and Canadian billing addresses, too.

Likewise, members residing within the U.S. can order from Costco’s Canadian site (www.costco.ca) using their U.S. billing information. The only thing to keep in mind, however, is that orders placed via the Canadian site need to be shipped to a delivery address within Canada.

For items available for delivery within the United Kingdom, visit www.costco.co.uk.
For items available for delivery within Mexico, visit www.costco.com.mx.
For items available for delivery within South Korea, visit www.costco.co.kr.
For items available for delivery within Taiwan, visit www.costco.com.tw.

Dryer Delivery Guidelines

Updated 05/08/2026

Title

Dryer Delivery Guidelines

Body

Before delivery:

  • Note for pedestal installation: The delivery team will only install components purchased through Costco at the time of delivery
  • Garage installation code requires that the dryer must be a minimum of 18” above the parking surface
  • NOTE: If your home does not have the proper gas, electrical or water supply lines and shut off valves required for your Home Delivery and/or Installation, we will not be able to complete the basic install. If you would like to reschedule your appointment, you can contact us 1-800-955-2292.

Electric:

  • Verify 240V grounded 30 AMP electrical service/outlet within 3 ft. of dryer location and the dryer venting within the home is clean. Clear vents outside to allow proper operation of the new dryer

Gas:

  • Verify there is a 3-prong grounded electrical outlet is within 3 ft. of appliance location
  • Gas supply and shut-off valve is on the same floor as the install location, preferably within 3 ft. of the install location
  • The delivery team will connect to natural gas only – no liquid propane connections NOTE: In certain municipalities, local code may require a plumber or gas technician to connect gas appliances
  • Verify the dryer is venting clean and clear

Our delivery team will:

  • Connect the dryer to the conforming electrical using a new 240V electrical cord in the original packaging and not visibly damaged
  • Attach venting using new components. Delivery teams cannot use or connect to plastic venting, foil vent tubes or existing electrical cord
  • Attach feet as needed and level the dryer or attach the dryer to a laundry pedestal purchased through Costco at the time of delivery
  • Stack laundry appliances when purchased along with the manufacturer’s stacking kit on Costco.com.
  • Level the dryer
  • Short cycle test the electric dryer
  • Be responsible for providing its own personal protective equipment (PPE) (e.g. mask, gloves, booties, etc.) and for complying with any legal requirements surrounding PPE

Our delivery team is not able to side vent dryers.

  • Haul Away commercial appliances

  • Connect LP gas. LP Gas Conversion kits are not included nor provided

The Haul Away unit(s) cannot be returned once it is removed.

General Appliance Delivery Guidelines

How can I check the status of my Costco.com order?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

How can I check the status of my Costco.com order?

Body

You’ll find the status of your Costco.com orders by visiting the Orders & Purchases section of your Costco.com account. Or, you can select Orders & Returns on the Costco.com homepage and sign in with your email address and password.

If the Order Status still shows “Order Placed”, your order is still being processed. It’ll change to “Shipped” once it’s turned over to the shipper and has tracking information available. Simply select View Order Details for more detailed information.

Whenever you successfully complete an online order, you’ll be taken to a screen with your Order Confirmation number. You’ll also be sent an email with the same details. If you don’t see your order in Orders & Purchases and you did not receive the confirmation email, it’s likely that your order was not completed. In that case, you can return to Costco.com to place your order again.

How do I track my order on Costco.com?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

How do I track my order on Costco.com?

Body

Order tracking is available on many Costco.com orders. If you’d like to track your order, follow these simple steps:

  1. Select Orders & Returns on the Costco.com homepage and sign in using your email address and password. If you’re already signed in, you can also visit Orders & Purchases in your Costco.com Account.
  2. Select the View Order Details button next to the order you’d like to track.
  3. Select the Tracking link to be directed to the tracking website to track your shipment.

If you’d like to know the general shipping timeframe for the item you purchased, refer to the product page of the item you ordered. You can also check the Order Confirmation email you received after you placed your order. That email will contain the estimated delivery time information. Once the item has shipped, your Shipping Confirmation email will also include tracking details.

How do I track, schedule, or reschedule my Costco Logistics In-Home Delivery order?

Updated 12/12/2025

Title

How do I track, schedule, or reschedule my Costco Logistics In-Home Delivery order?

Body

After your purchase, and once the merchandise has been received at your local delivery center, you will receive a “Scheduling Email Confirmation,” which will summarize your In-Home Delivery order.

Below are instructions on how to track, schedule, or reschedule your order through Costco Logistics. For delivery issues with 3rd party carriers, reach out to the carrier that is delivering your item.

Tracking an order
Scheduling an order
Scheduling multiple orders for the same delivery day
Rescheduling an order

TRACKING AN ORDER

  1. Visit our Tracking Site.
  2. Enter the tracking number found in your Costco Logistics scheduling email.
  3. Select Track My Delivery.
  4. Tracking details will be displayed. Scroll down to see all the details.
Status Definitions
  • In Transit – Order is en route to Costco Logistics from vendor.
  • Received/unloaded DDC Xdock – Order received at Costco Logistic distribution center and is awaiting shipment to final mile warehouse.
  • Cancelled at Cross-Dock Receiving – The order was received by Costco Logistics distribution center, but was cancelled.
  • Received at MDO – Order received at Costco Logistics final mile warehouse. See order detail history for updates.
  • Cancelled at MDO Receiving – Order cancelled after receiving at warehouse.
  • Delivered – Items delivered to customer.
  • Return Order Created – An order was created to pick up previously delivered item/order from customer.
  • Order Picked Up From Member – Item/Order was picked up by Costco Logistics from the customer and is being returned to warehouse for return processing.
  • Out for Delivery – This status means the order is on a delivery truck.

SCHEDULING AN ORDER

  1. Visit our Scheduling Site.

  2. Enter the tracking or order number found in your Costco Logistics scheduling email and select Schedule Delivery or Return Pickup.

  3. Select your preferred date, then select Continue to Questionnaire.

  4. Answer the Questionnaire. Once complete, select Review Information.

  5. Review your information for accuracy and enter an alternative phone number, then select Submit Request.

SCHEDULING MULTIPLE ORDERS FOR THE SAME DELIVERY DAY

  1. Visit our Scheduling Site.

  2. Enter the tracking or order number found in your Costco Logistics scheduling email and select Schedule Delivery or Return Pickup.

  3. Select the orders you would like delivered on the same day, then Select Date.

  4. Follow the Scheduling an Order instructions to complete scheduling.

RESCHEDULING AN ORDER

If you need to reschedule a delivery (because of a missed appointment, missed delivery, etc.):

For In-Home Installation items, contact Costco.com Customer Service for assistance. Please have your Costco.com order number ready.

If your In-Home Delivery date or time window is not convenient and you wish to reschedule your delivery, follow the steps below (products that qualify for In-Home Delivery reschedule: Appliances, Furniture, Mattresses and Televisions).

  1. Visit our Scheduling Site.

  2. Enter the tracking or order number found in your Costco Logistics scheduling email and select Schedule Delivery or Return Pickup.

  3. The page will show the date on which the order is currently scheduled to be delivered. Select a new date and continue to complete the process.

Is my home ready for an appliance installation?

Updated 04/02/2026

Title

Is my home ready for an appliance installation?

Body

If you purchased an ice/water dispenser model refrigerator, dishwasher, or washer, your water shut-off valve must be located within 3 feet of the appliance and in working condition. Our delivery team cannot hook up the appliance if the shut off valve is not within the proximity of the final setup.

For gas model appliances such as ranges or dryers, the gas connection should be directly behind the appliance.

The shut off valves should be corrosion free prior to the delivery appointment to guarantee the connection. If any modifications are needed, we recommend arranging a plumber to come in prior to the delivery date. Our teams will not re-use existing appliance accessories.

Some Examples May Include: Refrigerator - No Shut Off Valve (Not Recommended) Refrigerator - Shut Off Valve Under the Sink (Recommended) Range - Gas Shut Off Valve (Recommended) Corroded Shut Off Valve (Not Recommended)

Is shipping available to Alaska, Hawaii & Puerto Rico?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Is shipping available to Alaska, Hawaii & Puerto Rico?

Body

We do our best to service as many locations as possible. That’s why many of the items found on Costco.com can be shipped to Alaska, Hawaii, and Puerto Rico. That said, due to shipping limitations, not all products will qualify. To determine if an item can be shipped to your location, please check the “Shipping & Returns” section located on the product page.

Please keep in mind that once you’ve added your “Ship To” address to the item during checkout, the shipping method will automatically be revised to reflect the level of service required for these destinations. We’ll also quote any additional shipping and handling costs during checkout. That way, you’ll see the total before you finalize the purchase.

Range Delivery Guidelines

Updated 12/24/2025

Title

Range Delivery Guidelines

Body

Before delivery:

  • Determine and prepare a clean accessible location with adequate space for the new range
  • Verify needed utilities: 240V grounded 50 AMP electrical
  • NOTE: If your home does not have the proper gas, electrical or water supply lines and shut off valves required for your Home Delivery and/or Installation, we will not be able to complete the basic install. If you would like to reschedule your appointment, you can contact us 1-800-955-2292.

Our delivery team will:

  • Review anti-tip installation and install the anti-tip device per member approval
  • Remove range from carton; provide appliance literature
  • Remove tape, protective film covering and packing material
  • Place range in location
  • Check 240V electrical outlet. Connect the range to the conforming electrical service following local code using new 240V electrical cord in the original packaging and not visibly damaged
  • Level the range; set grates & burners in place
  • Test the connected range power source; by activating burners and oven
  • Remove all packing material from the premises
  • Be responsible for providing its own personal protective equipment (PPE) (e.g., mask, gloves, booties, etc.) and for complying with any legal requirements surrounding PPE

Haul Away Service Requirements

  • Haul Away commercial appliances
  • Relocate the old appliance to a new address
  • Hook up the old appliance at a new location
  • Haul Away your items if the Haul Away unit(s) are not inside your home or your garage due to sanitary reasons
  • Haul Away more units than what is being delivered
  • Hoist merchandise or lift product above their waist
  • Connect LP gas. LP Gas Conversion kits are not included nor provided

In the event these requirements are not followed, Haul Away will be declined.

The Haul Away unit(s) cannot be returned once it is removed.

General Appliance Delivery Guidelines

Refrigerator and Freezer Delivery Guidelines

Updated 07/28/2026

Title

Refrigerator and Freezer Delivery Guidelines

Body

Before delivery:

  • Ensure water valves are corrosion-free. Signs that a shut-off valve needs replacement include:
    • Leaks or Corrosion: Visible water leaking from the valve or rust/corrosion on the body.
    • Difficulty Turning: The valve is hard to turn, binds, or grinds, indicating internal mineral buildup or damage.
    • Failure to Shut Off Water: The valve does not completely stop the water flow when closed.
    • Age: The valve is an older gate-style valve (prone to failure) or is over 15-20 years old.

Regular maintenance, which involves turning the valves on and off (exercising them) at least once every couple of years, can help prevent them from seizing and extend their lifespan.

  • NOTE: If your home does not have the proper gas, electrical or water supply lines and shut off valves required for your Home Delivery and/or Installation, we will not be able to complete the basic install. If you would like to reschedule your appointment, you can contact us 1-800-955-2292.

The delivery team will:

  • Remove three-pin house doors, refrigerator doors and handles to facilitate the delivery as needed
  • Reattach the house door prior to departure
  • Place all racks, crispers, ice trays, door handles, and other features in factory recommended settings
  • Plug in the appliance and turn to a mid-range cold setting
  • Connect the ice maker to an existing waterline, as long as the waterline meets manufacturer and local building requirements
  • Hook up the copper tubing
    • Note: Ice maker connection may require connectors and additional tubing
  • Turn on the icemaker if an ice maker is present and water is connected
  • Use a glass until water begins to flow if a water dispenser is present
  • Run the dispenser until water begins to flow
  • Align the doors as required
  • Level the product as required
  • Leave all “To be removed by member-only” literature on or in the product
  • Hand the member all other literature
  • Be responsible for providing its own personal protective equipment (PPE) (e.g., mask, gloves, booties, etc.) and for complying with any legal requirements surrounding PPE

The delivery team will not:

  • Hook up the refrigerator waterline if the waterline does not have an accessible shut-off valve in close proximity to the install location; preferably within 6 feet
  • Shut off the residential water main in order to complete a water line hook up
  • Haul Away items that are contaminated, infested, or pose a risk to personnel, new products, or equipment
  • Haul Away commercial appliances
  • Relocate the old appliance to a new address
  • Hook up the old appliance at a new location
  • Haul Away your items if the Haul Away unit(s) are not inside your home or your garage due to sanitary reasons
  • Haul Away more units than what is being delivered
  • Hoist merchandise or lift product above their waist

Refrigerator Door Reversal Request

  • Costco Logistics will perform door reversal for standard refrigerators
  • Doors cannot be reversed on French door and Side-by-Side refrigerators

Haul Away

  • Where Haul Away is available, it is on a one-for-one, like item, basis. The item must be un-installed and ready for pick up at the time of delivery.
  • Costco Logistics will Haul Away appliances, including built-in appliances (such as Viking, Sub Zero, Monograms, etc.), as long as they have been uninstalled. Refrigerator doors will be removed if required to remove the old appliance from the member’s home.

General Appliance Delivery Guidelines

UPS My Choice® FAQs

Updated 12/19/2023

Title

UPS My Choice® FAQs

Body

UPS My Choice® allows you to manage your UPS package deliveries. Below are answers to some frequently asked questions about UPS My Choice®.

What can I do with UPS My Choice®?
How do I sign up for UPS My Choice®?
How do I manage my delivery using UPS My Choice®?

WHAT CAN I DO WITH UPS MY CHOICE®?

Take control of your UPS delivery! Some of the features of UPS My Choice® include:

  • Hold for Will Call (UPS Customer Center)
  • Deliver Package on Another Day
  • Deliver to a UPS Access Point™
  • Delivery Alerts
  • Delivery Planner
  • Estimated Delivery Time
  • Authorize Shipment Release
  • “Leave at” Instructions
  • Leave with Neighbor
  • Confirmed Delivery Window

Note: Some options may not be available for every delivery. UPS may use discretion and deliver to an Access Point. Some of these features are available for a small fee. For more information, visit: https://www.ups.com/us/en/services/tracking/mychoice.page

HOW DO I SIGN UP FOR UPS MY CHOICE®?

Visit the UPS My Choice® page, then select Sign Up. Once you create a user ID and password and provide your name and address, you’ll receive a verification email to complete the setup process.

HOW DO I MANAGE MY DELIVERY USING UPS MY CHOICE®?

Log in to UPS.com, then enter your tracking number and select Track. After that, follow the prompts for whichever feature you wish to use.

Washer Delivery Guidelines

Updated 05/08/2026

Title

Washer Delivery Guidelines

Body

Before delivery:

  • Note for pedestal installation: The delivery team will only install components purchased through Costco at the time of delivery
  • Verify there is a 3-prong grounded electrical outlet and hot and cold water supply, with an accessible shut-off valve in close proximity to the install location
  • Drain your current washer water prior to delivery
  • Confirm standpipes are at least 34 in. tall
  • Ensure water valves are corrosion-free. Signs that a shut-off valve needs replacement include:
    • Leaks or Corrosion: Visible water leaking from the valve or rust/corrosion on the body
    • Difficulty Turning: The valve is hard to turn, binds, or grinds, indicating internal mineral buildup or damage
    • Failure to Shut Off Water: The valve does not completely stop the water flow when closed
    • Age: The valve is an older gate-style valve (prone to failure) or is over 15-20 years old

Regular maintenance, which involves turning the valves on and off (exercising them) at least once every couple of years, can help prevent them from seizing and extend their lifespan.

  • NOTE: If your home does not have the proper gas, electrical or water supply lines and shut off valves required for your Home Delivery and/or Installation, we will not be able to complete the basic install. If you would like to reschedule your appointment, you can contact us 1-800-955-2292.

Due to building codes in many areas, a drip pan may be required depending on the floor of the structure on which it is installed. If the washer will be placed on a first floor concrete slab, with no basement below, a drip pan is typically not required; however, if the washer is being installed above a basement or other floors, a drip pan is required.

Our delivery team will:

  • Install using new parts, for proper installation. Existing parts cannot be reused due to the risk of leaks
  • Connect the new washer to the home’s hot and cold water supply using new lines which meet the manufacturer’s requirements; new lines must be in original packaging with no visible damage
  • Attach feet as needed and level the washer or attach the washer to a laundry pedestal purchased through Costco at the time of delivery
  • If necessary, place the washer in a drip pan
  • Attach and secure the drain hose
  • Ground washer to a metal pipe or grounded receptacle and connect the drain hose
  • Short cycle test the washer to check for leaks at the washer connection
  • Stack laundry appliances when purchased along with the manufacturer’s stacking kit on Costco.com.
    *An exception is if stackable washer and dryer is an exchange and was purchased within 90 days.
  • Be responsible for providing its own personal protective equipment (PPE) (e.g., mask, gloves, booties, etc.) and for complying with any legal requirements surrounding PPE

Haul Away Service Requirements

Please note the following important information about our Haul Away service:

The delivery teams will not:

  • Haul Away items that are contaminated, infested, or pose a risk to personnel, new products, or equipment
  • Haul Away commercial appliances
  • Relocate the old appliance to a new address
  • Hook up the old appliance at a new location
  • Haul Away your items if the Haul Away unit(s) are not inside your home or your garage due to sanitary reasons
  • Haul Away more units than what is being delivered
  • Hoist Merchandise or lift product above their waist

In the event these requirements are not followed, Haul Away will be declined.

Where Haul Away is available, it is on a one-for-one, like item, basis. The item must be ready for pick up at the time of delivery.

The Haul Away unit(s) cannot be returned once it is removed.

What is Haul Away?

Updated 01/07/2026

Title

What is Haul Away?

Body

Haul Away is the removal and disposal of your old item by the delivery team. It is available in select areas. Items eligible include: Appliances*, Boxed Mattresses*, Innerspring Mattresses*. Any installed and/or commercial items are not eligible.

Where Haul Away is available, it is on a one-for-one, like item, basis. The item must be ready for pick up at the time of delivery.

*Items that are contaminated, infested, or pose a risk to personnel, new products, or equipment are not eligible for Haul Away. Costco.com products can be returned to any of our Costco warehouses worldwide.

What is my Costco Logistics In-Home Delivery time window?

Updated 12/12/2025

Title

What is my Costco Logistics In-Home Delivery time window?

Body

For In-Home Delivery Items:

  • The night before your delivery appointment, you will receive an automated call from Costco Logistics at your primary phone number listed on your order providing you with a two hour time window.

For In-Home Installations:

  • The night before your complex installation appointment, you will receive a call from your Installation Provider at your primary phone number listed on your order providing you with a four hour time window. On the day of the installation appointment, the provider will call 30 minutes before arriving to your home.

Once you’ve been assigned a time window for your delivery or installation, it cannot be changed for that day. If the time window doesn’t work for you, you’re welcome to reschedule your order using our scheduling website.

What to Expect for Alaska Deliveries - Costco Logistics

Updated 06/19/2024

Title

What to Expect for Alaska Deliveries - Costco Logistics

Body

Deliveries to Alaska require a special shipping process. Below is an overview of the process so you know what to expect:

  • Your order will be sent to our supplier for fulfillment. The timeframe for fulfillment is listed on Costco.com under the Shipping & Terms section for the item you ordered.
  • Your order will be shipped from the supplier to our delivery agent in Seattle, WA. You will receive an email with tracking information once it has shipped.
  • Our delivery agent will consolidate your order with other Alaska orders to be put on a container for ocean transit. You will receive another email notification once the container has shipped and your order is on the water.
  • Please allow 5 days for ocean transit from Seattle to Alaska.
  • Your order will ship to Anchorage unless you live in one of the following locations that ship directly to their own ports: Juneau, Ketchikan, Petersburg and Sitka.
  • Once your order arrives in port, you will be contacted within 2 business days to schedule the delivery appointment. Deliveries to remote territories may require additional delivery time based on the location and the type of transportation needed to deliver to your area.

Deliveries will be scheduled on an agreed upon day with a 4 hour delivery window either in the morning or afternoon. Please be sure to fully inspect your merchandise before signing your acceptance of the order with the delivery driver.

What to Expect for Hawaii Deliveries - Costco Logistics

Updated 07/06/2024

Title

What to Expect for Hawaii Deliveries - Costco Logistics

Body

Deliveries to Hawaii require a special shipping process. Below is an overview of the process so you know what to expect:

  • Your order will be sent to our supplier for fulfillment. The timeframe for fulfillment is listed on Costco.com under the Shipping & Terms section for the item you ordered.
  • Your order will be shipped from the supplier to our fulfillment center in Ontario, CA. You will receive an email with tracking information once it has shipped.
  • Once your order has been delivered to our fulfillment center, we will consolidate it with other Hawaii orders to be put on a container for ocean transit. You will receive another email notification once the container has shipped and your order is on the water.

The transit time from our facility in Ontario, CA to your island can take a few additional weeks. Below is a breakdown by island showing you how long it will take for your order to arrive on your island. This is in addition to the fulfillment timeframe listed on Costco.com. From that point, it will take up to an additional 2 business days for you to be contacted to schedule your delivery appointment.

Island Shipping Timeframe from California
Oahu 2 weeks
Hawaii (Big Island) 2 weeks
Kauai 2-3 weeks
Lanai 2-3 weeks
Maui 2 weeks
Molokai 2 weeks

What to Expect for Puerto Rico Deliveries - Costco Logistics

Updated 07/05/2024

Title

What to Expect for Puerto Rico Deliveries - Costco Logistics

Body

Deliveries to Puerto Rico require a special shipping process. Below is an overview of the process so you know what to expect:

  • Your order will be sent to our supplier for fulfillment. The timeframe for fulfillment is listed on Costco.com under the Shipping & Terms section for the item you ordered.
  • Your order will be shipped from the supplier to our delivery agent in Ocala, FL. You will receive an email with tracking information once it has shipped.
  • Our delivery agent will consolidate your order with other Puerto Rico orders to be put on a container for ocean transit. You will receive another email notification once the container has shipped and your order is on the water.
  • Please allow 11 days for transit from Ocala, FL to Puerto Rico.
  • Once your order arrives in Puerto Rico, you will be contacted within 2 business days to schedule the delivery appointment.

Deliveries will be scheduled on an agreed upon day with a 2 hour delivery window either in the morning or afternoon. Please be sure to fully inspect your merchandise before signing your acceptance of the order with the delivery driver.

Where can I find the estimated shipping time frame?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Where can I find the estimated shipping time frame?

Body

You can find information about Costco delivery times right on the product pages.

If you look about halfway down an item’s Product Details page, you’ll locate the Shipping & Returns tab, where you can find estimated shipping information.

You also should receive a confirmation email upon order, which includes the item’s shipping information. Upon actual shipment, you’ll be sent a second email, which confirms the item is on its way and includes tracking information for your convenience.

Why does my UPS tracking show my order was delivered last year?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Why does my UPS tracking show my order was delivered last year?

Body

If your order recently shipped via UPS, but the tracking information is already showing “delivered” with a date from before you placed your order, please check the tracking again after 24-48 hours.

Due to the sheer volume of orders that UPS handles every year, sometimes UPS tracking numbers are “recycled” and will initially show a delivery date from a previous order. However, once the package starts movement, the tracking will update to have accurate information.

We apologize for any confusion!

Why was my order cancelled?

Updated 03/04/2026

Title

Why was my order cancelled?

Body

When authorizing your purchase, Costco.com electronically requests a pre-authorization from your credit card issuer. If your credit card company does not provide that authorization, there are a number of likely reasons. For example:

  • The Costco.com billing information did not match the billing information that the credit card company has on file.
  • The card’s number and/or expiration date was entered incorrectly on Costco.com.
  • Exceeding the credit card company’s Internet transactions limits.

If the cancellation was due to entering the card information incorrectly, attempt to place your order again on Costco.com with the corrected information.

If the cancellation was caused by exceeding your credit card company’s transaction limits, the best way to address this issue is by contacting them directly.

When an order is cancelled, the pre-authorization is immediately released by Costco.com and removed from your financial account based on the policies of your bank. For more information on those policies, please contact your bank.

Costco is unable to reinstate cancelled orders. However, members may place an order again on Costco.com. If you encounter multiple cancellations when trying to place your order, or if you believe your order was cancelled for a reason other than what is listed above, please use the Contact Us options on this page.

Will my order arrive on time?

Updated 09/01/2023

Title

Will my order arrive on time?

Body

Tracking on your order may not show any movement or initial scan for several days. There’s no need to worry! Your order is secure and will move through the carrier’s network soon. You can continue to track your order in your Orders and Purchases.

We suggest you consider enrolling in UPS My Choice, a service that provides you text and/or email updates on delivery status, and provides you with options to reroute packages.

We’ll need your order number and your current membership number to process the refund back to the method of payment used on your order.

If you’d like to order a member-only item, you’re welcome to contact us for assistance.

If you’ve recently relocated to the U.S. and need to change your international membership to a U.S. membership, please visit the membership counter at your closest Costco warehouse.

When you pick a Preferred Arrival Date, your order will deliver on or (even better) before the date of your choice.

If your item has the feature enabled, a “Delivery Date” option will be available in the Shopping Cart. Click on the calendar icon to select a valid Preferred Arrival Date, then proceed to order completion.

Can I use an APO/FPO for my Billing Addresses?

Can I use an APO/FPO for my Billing Addresses?

Body

Here’s how to enter an APO / FPO address in your Billing information to match the address on your credit card statement:

Address Field 1:

  • Army, Navy and Marines: Enter Unit Number and Box Number
  • Ships: Enter Ship Name and Hull Number
  • Air Force: Enter PSC Number and Box Number.

  • AE–Armed Forces Europe, Middle East, Africa and Canada

  • AP–Armed Forces Pacific

How do I place an order on Costco.com?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

How do I place an order on Costco.com?

Body

Placing an order on Costco.com is simple. Just follow these steps:

  1. Sign In / Register in the upper right-hand corner of Costco.com. If you’re a returning Costco.com member, please enter your email address and password. If you’re a new Costco.com member, select Create Account, then enter your email address and your choice of password. If you’re a member, enter the membership number found on your membership card. You’re now ready to start shopping!

    Note: Maintaining an active Costco membership doesn’t automatically register you on Costco.com. After becoming a Costco member, you’ll still need to complete a new registration on Costco.com (if you didn’t previously register). Your membership number isn’t linked to your online account until you add it to Account Details or at the time of registration.
  2. Once you’re signed up, you can begin shopping! Search for merchandise by selecting a product category in the Shop menu on the left-hand side of the page, or by using the search box with the name of an item or its item number.
  3. When you find an item you’d like to purchase, choose your quantity and select Add to Cart. You’ll then receive a notification confirming that the item has been added to your cart. You can select Continue Shopping, or, if you’re done shopping and ready to checkout, select View Cart.
  4. When you’re done shopping and ready to checkout, select Cart at the top right corner of the page, then select Checkout.
  5. Enter your shipping and payment information, then select Continue to Review Order.
  6. Carefully review your order then select Place Your Order.
  7. After completing your purchase, you’ll receive an order confirmation email with your order number.
  8. Once your item has shipped, you’ll receive a shipping confirmation email with your tracking number (if tracking is available) and instructions on how to track the shipment of your order online.

Title

Body

  2. Open Orders & Returns.
  3. Choose the Buy Items Again button next to the appropriate order.
    • This option will only be available if at least one item can be purchased again.
  4. Check the box next to the item you wish to reorder.
  5. Select Add to Cart.

Please see How do I place an order on Costco.com? for complete checkout instructions.
