What qualifies as an “Extraordinary Delivery Requirement”?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

What qualifies as an “Extraordinary Delivery Requirement”?

Body

While we’re always looking for new ways to offer great savings and a prompt delivery, there are certain cases in which we have to charge an additional delivery fee in order to maintain a balance.

Here are three circumstances that might qualify as an extraordinary delivery requirement and necessitate a fee:

  • Additional stairs over two flights
  • Delivery outside of standard Monday–Friday, four-hour window
  • The delivery location requires use of equipment other than the delivery agent’s standard mode of delivery

Why am I being charged a non-member surcharge?

Updated 03/03/2026

Title

Why am I being charged a non-member surcharge?

Body

If you’re a member and have not yet placed the order, please head to the Account Details section of your Costco.com account. Once there, go ahead and enter your membership number (it should be 12 digits long and start with either a 1, 3, or 8), and store it for future purchases. The 5% surcharge will be removed if you take these steps prior to completing your order.

If you are a member and have already placed the order, simply Contact Us with your membership number and order number. Once we have confirmed this information, we can process the credit for the surcharge immediately.

If you are not a member, you will be charged a 5% surcharge over posted prices for members (except for prescription drugs). This is because our ability to offer low prices on brand-name items is due in large part to our membership program, which is designed to reward members for their loyalty.

Of course, we absolutely encourage you to join—there are plenty of benefits to follow should you choose to do so. You even have the opportunity to add a membership to your cart prior to checkout, at which point the 5% surcharge will be removed.

Why can’t I view prices or purchase member only items on Costco.com?

Body

It’s possible that your membership number has not been added to your Costco.com account. To add your membership number:

  1. Sign in here.
  2. Head over to Account Details in your Account.
  3. Enter your membership number in the field provided.
  4. Click Add Membership Number Only or Verify Membership.

Once this process is complete, you should be able to view member-only prices and purchase member-only items. If not, please contact us so we can assist you.

Chargebacks: Disputed or Rejected Payment FAQs‎‎

Updated 04/04/2024

Title

Chargebacks: Disputed or Rejected Payment FAQs‎‎

Body

A chargeback is when a member’s credit card or financial institution rejects Costco’s request for payment or is disputing the charge from Costco on the member’s behalf.

Costco.com will send emails to the address on the affected order followed by a mailed copy to the billing address on the order if we do not receive a response via email.

You will not be able to place an online order on Costco.com, Costco.ca, or create a booking with Costco Travel until all outstanding charges are resolved. Your ability to make purchases at your local Costco warehouse may be impacted if we are unable to reach you for response. If you would like to resolve the balance or have any pertinent information you would like to provide in reference to your chargeback(s), please refer to the notice sent to you for additional instruction.

Answers to frequently asked questions about chargebacks are below.

How do I make a payment? Can I simply update my card on file?
Can I make a payment in person?
Are payment plans available?
My CITI Costco Anywhere VISA® number changed. Why don’t you have my new number?
Why was my payment rejected?
Why was my credit card charged twice for my Costco.com order?
I didn’t receive an item or items on my order, so I disputed the charges.
Why can’t I see any chargebacks on my Costco.com Account?
I already closed this dispute with my bank. Why is Costco still contacting me?
Are my orders canceling because I have an active chargeback?
I paid for this item when I placed the order. Why would Costco send out items that have not been paid for?

HOW DO I MAKE A PAYMENT? CAN I SIMPLY UPDATE MY CARD ON FILE?

For security purposes, Costco cannot access any card information stored on your account unless the card has been previously charged. For details on how to make a payment, please see the notice emailed to you for additional instruction or contact us at 1-800-955-2292.

CAN I MAKE A PAYMENT IN PERSON?

Payments due to Costco.com cannot be made at physical Costco locations.

ARE PAYMENT PLANS AVAILABLE?

Costco.com does not offer payment plans for online purchases. Received items would need to be returned or the chargeback settled in full to bring your account to good standing.

MY CITI COSTCO ANYWHERE VISA® NUMBER CHANGED. WHY DON’T YOU HAVE MY NEW NUMBER?

Costco does not have access to any personal financial records. These are secure between you and your card issuer.
For CITI Costco Anywhere VISA® holders, we do not receive updated card details automatically if your card is replaced. We only receive notification of membership number changes. Costco is not a financial institution and only partners with CITI Bank to offer benefits and rewards with the Costco Anywhere VISA®.

WHY WAS MY PAYMENT REJECTED?

Your payment could be rejected for one of the following reasons:

  • Your credit card has been reissued prior to shipment.
  • Your financial institution may have closed the transaction prior to completion for your security resulting in the charge being declined.

To confirm you have not been charged, review your bank statements or contact your financial institution for additional support.

WHY WAS MY CREDIT CARD CHARGED TWICE FOR MY COSTCO.COM ORDER?

Credit Cards
Your credit card will be charged only after your item ships. When your order contains more than one item, you may see more than one charge from Costco.com on your credit card. Since shipping times vary by item, your credit card statement may reflect individual charges for each item as it is shipped.

Before your item ships, you’ll notice a pre-authorization hold in the amount of your order on your card statement. The pre-authorization validates the credit card number, status, available credit line and billing information to ensure that it matches exactly what the bank or credit card company has on file. At this point your card hasn’t been charged yet.

Once an authorization is received, you may notice a decrease in your available credit line. Your bank or credit card company may put a hold on this dollar amount for a specified period of time. Each financial institution has a different policy on the time period of authorization holds. Also, please note that before authorizing the final sale, your bank may attempt to contact you for additional information.

Debit Cards
When using a debit card, the full amount of the order will be deducted from your available balance upon authorization.

Contact your bank or credit card company directly to learn about their specific authorization policies.

I DIDN’T RECEIVE AN ITEM OR ITEMS ON MY ORDER, SO I DISPUTED THE CHARGES

Please reply to the chargeback notice sent to you via email or call us at 1-800-955-2292 to let us know what Items you did not receive and one of our specialists would be happy to assist you!

WHY CAN’T I SEE ANY CHARGEBACKS ON MY COSTCO.COM ACCOUNT?

At this time, Costco.com does not reflect disputes/rejected payments on your Costco.com profile. We are always working to better the experience for our members, and we hope to provide an option for this in the future.

I ALREADY CLOSED THIS DISPUTE WITH MY BANK. WHY IS COSTCO STILL CONTACTING ME?

It can often take 45-90 days for Costco to receive funds from a reversed dispute after they have been debited from your account. To speed up the process, you can respond to the email notice sent to you and provide us with a dispute closure notice or copy of your statements detailing that you have been rebilled so we may confirm the resolution with your financial institution.

ARE MY ORDERS CANCELING BECAUSE I HAVE AN ACTIVE CHARGEBACK?

If you have an active chargeback on your account, you will be unable to process a successful order on Costco.com, Costco.ca, or create a booking with Costco Travel until the chargeback has been resolved.

I PAID FOR THIS ITEM WHEN I PLACED THE ORDER. WHY WOULD COSTCO SEND OUT ITEMS THAT HAVE NOT BEEN PAID FOR?

Costco.com only charges you for items as they are shipped, however, when you place your order, you will see a temporary pre-authorization hold for the total order amount, which is replaced by the final charges upon shipment. We pre-authorize all transactions in order to ensure payment will be processed regardless of if a card is changed. Sometimes, your financial institution will close out the pending transaction for your protection or the pre-authorization may expire and we are not notified until the item has already been shipped.

Body

Please rest assured that your satisfaction is our top priority—that’s why we’re always working to bring you brand-name items at the lowest possible prices. However, because we already offer competitive prices at the point of purchase, we do not price match with other retailers.

What is a pre-authorization hold for an online order?

Updated 05/07/2024

Title

What is a pre-authorization hold for an online order?

Body

Once you place an order, you may have pending pre-authorization(s) on your debit or credit card account in the amount of the estimated order total. A pre-authorization confirms the account is open and reserves the funds until the order is processed or the order is canceled. Upon order shipment, Costco sends a transaction message to capture the actual amount of the order and reverse the pre-authorization. Depending on your financial institution’s timeframe for reversing or releasing pre-authorizations, the pending transaction may be removed immediately or up to the closing of your billing cycle.

It’s possible that you may have multiple authorizations and/or charges on your account for an order. Authorization holds may be requested for individual items if your order ships in multiple shipments or via different delivery methods. You may also have any combination of the following:

  • An authorization hold in the amount of the estimated order total.
  • Posted charge(s) for shipped item(s).
  • New authorization holds for remaining items that haven’t shipped.

If you have additional questions about the time frame for the reversal or release of pre-authorization holds, please contact your card issuing bank.

What is my daily debit card transaction limit?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

What is my daily debit card transaction limit?

Body

Financial institutions rely on daily limits to protect cardholders — as well as themselves — from criminal activity capable of depleting entire bank accounts in a short period of time.

It is the financial institutions, not merchants, who establish these limits.

If you want to determine your limit, it’s best to contact your issuing bank. Since we here at Costco do not have access to your account details, we’re not able to retrieve that information ourselves.

What is the CVV Card Identification Number?

Updated 09/22/2023

Title

What is the CVV Card Identification Number?

Body

The CVV is a series of numbers that are simply used for security purposes. When the CVV is required for a purchase, it authenticates that the buyer has the card in their possession, making fraud less likely.

Typically, a CVV consists of three or four numbers printed on either the front or back of your card. For Visa®, and MasterCard, the number is located on the back—just look for the signature strip.

We take your security very seriously, so we’re always trying to minimize the chance that you’ll ever experience Internet crime (and the headache that’s sure to follow). For that reason, we do ask for your CVV during the checkout process.

If you would like to learn more about the measures we have in place to protect your privacy, please review our Privacy Statement here.

Title

Disclaimer: Visa Checkout has transitioned to the new click to pay experience built on EMV® Secure Remote Commerce standards, or Visa . For more information, please view the Visa FAQs.

During checkout on Costco.com, you can select the Visa icon, sign in to your account, and select the stored payment method you wish to use.

For more information or to enroll, please visit the Visa website.

Why is there a shipping fee on each item in my order?

Updated 09/01/2023

Title

Why is there a shipping fee on each item in my order?

Body

Each product on Costco.com has its own shipping fee attached (although many items are shipped at no additional charge). If there’s a shipping charge, you’ll find it on the product detail page to the right of the product picture.

Most items on Costco.com are shipped directly from our vendors and manufacturers with whom we’re contracted. Each vendor and manufacturer has its own shipping charge, and it cannot be waived by Costco. There are cases when items may be shipped from different locations (even if the items are identical) depending upon inventory availability. Each item and any associated shipping fees are charged at the time that the item ships—this is why you might see multiple charges on your financial statement.

For CostcoGrocery, products with 2-Day Delivery will have an additional delivery fee per item, unless the order total is over $75. For more information, visit our CostcoGrocery page here.

Why was my credit card charged twice for my Costco.com order?

Title

Why was my credit card charged twice for my Costco.com order?

Body

Credit Cards
Your credit card will be charged only after your item ships. When your order contains more than one item, Costco.com will charge your card for each item individually after it ships. Since shipping times vary by item, your credit card statement may reflect individual charges for each item as it is shipped.

Before your item ships, you’ll notice a pre-authorization hold in the amount of your order on your card statement. The pre-authorization validates the credit card number, status, available credit line and billing information to ensure that it matches exactly what the bank or credit card company has on file. At this point your card hasn’t been charged yet.

Once an authorization is received, you may notice a decrease in your available credit line. Your bank or credit card company may put a hold on this dollar amount for a specified period of time. Each financial institution has a different policy on the time period of authorization holds. Also, please note that before authorizing the final sale, your bank may attempt to contact you for additional information.

Debit Cards
When using a debit card, the full amount of the order will be deducted from your available balance upon authorization.

Contact your bank or credit card company directly to learn about their specific authorization policies.

How can I locate a product on Costco.com?

How can I locate a product on Costco.com?

Body

To search for a product online at Costco.com, enter a keyword or an item number into the search engine at the top. If the item you’re seeking is in stock and available for purchase, your search will pull up its product page. You can view the item details on the product page, add it to your cart and proceed to checkout.

You can also browse through the drop-down menu of departments on the Costco.com homepage, shop by categories and locate products that match what you’re looking for.

Please keep in mind that the items available online are unique to Costco.com and may not be carried in your neighborhood Costco warehouse.

If the product you’re looking for doesn’t come up online, it’s possible that it’s in stock at the warehouse. For more information pertaining to viewing warehouse inventory online, please click here.

How can I see what products are available on Costco.com?

Updated 09/01/2023

Title

How can I see what products are available on Costco.com?

Body

To view products on Costco.com, either type what you’re looking for into the Search box, or mouse over “Shop” to view our different categories. Simply click on the name or picture of an item to navigate to its product page.

The great thing about shopping on Costco.com is that everything you see reflects our live, real-time and up-to-date inventory. The flip side is that the items on offer, including promotional offers, are limited to supplies on hand. We’re unable to guarantee how long those supplies will last.

To secure the best prices for our members, Costco.com purchases all items in bulk. Once inventory sells out, our buyers will negotiate for additional inventory. While it isn’t always possible to replenish the inventory of all items, Costco.com makes every effort to offer the best quality products at the lowest possible prices.

Once an item has sold out, it’s automatically removed from Costco.com, or you’ll see the “Out of Stock” banner added to the product page. Items are also continually added to Costco.com, re-stocking existing merchandise as well as expanding what we offer. That’s why we encourage our members to check back with our site frequently. There are always new products and promotions on offer. Happy shopping!

Is the inventory on Costco.com live?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Is the inventory on Costco.com live?

Body

The inventory on our website is real-time and updated frequently. If the item you’re looking for is not found on Costco.com, it’s either not available online or currently out of stock. When the item is available for purchase again, it will re-appear on the site. The site is completely up to date, so we encourage you to check the site frequently for changes and updates.

Online Prescription Eyeglasses FAQ

Updated 01/28/2025

Title

Online Prescription Eyeglasses FAQ

Body

What do I need to order prescription eyeglasses on Costco.com?
How do I submit my prescription?
What if I have more than one prescription?
How do I find my Pupillary Distance (PD)?
Can I order more than one pair of prescription eyeglasses?
Can online frames be ordered at the warehouse?
Can I order frames without the lenses on Costco.com?
Does Costco.com sell non-prescription eyeglasses?
What treatments and/or options are available on lenses?
How should I clean my new prescription glasses?
Can I split payment between my FSA and a different card?
Does Costco.com accept vision insurance?
What do I do if I’m experiencing issues with my prescription or require an adjustment/repair?
What is the return policy on prescription eyeglasses?
Does Costco.com offer expedited delivery for prescription eyeglasses?
What is included in the package?

WHAT DO I NEED TO ORDER PRESCRIPTION EYEGLASSES ON COSTCO.COM?

To order prescription eyeglasses on Costco.com, you will need the following:

  • A valid prescription which should include the doctor’s information, date of the exam, the doctor’s signature, and pupillary distance.
  • An active Costco membership.
  • A Costco.com account, which includes an email address and unique password that matches the name the eyeglasses are for. If you do not have an individual account, you can create one using the same membership number.

HOW DO I SUBMIT MY PRESCRIPTION?

There are multiple ways to submit your eyeglass prescription. Here are the three options:

  1. Upload the prescription as a PDF, JPEG, or PNG when submitting the order.
  2. An existing member who has ordered prescription eyeglasses on Costco.com in the past can use their on-file prescription by selecting ’Select from my Account’ when asked to provide a prescription during the ordering process.
  3. Upload the prescription within 5 days of the order being placed.

Costco cannot start manufacturing the eyeglasses without a valid prescription. If a valid prescription and Pupillary Distance is not uploaded within 5 days of the order being placed, the order will be automatically canceled. Three reminder emails will be sent over the course of 5 days from the time of order with instructions to add your Pupillary Distance and/or upload your prescription.

WHAT IF I HAVE MORE THAN ONE PRESCRIPTION?

Each pair of eyeglasses will prompt the option to upload its own prescription. If there have been previous orders for prescription eyeglasses on Costco.com, sign into your Costco.com account and select a saved prescription (if it is still valid).

HOW DO I FIND MY PUPILLARY DISTANCE (PD)?

The Pupillary Distance is the distance between the center of each pupil and it is required to manufacture the lenses. The Pupillary Distance can be obtained two ways:

  1. Use the Virtual Try On tool and the Pupillary Distance will automatically be added to the order.
  2. The Pupillary Distance may be listed on your prescription.

Costco cannot start manufacturing the eyeglasses without a valid prescription. If a valid prescription and Pupillary Distance is not uploaded within 5 days of the order being placed, the order will be automatically canceled. Three reminder emails will be sent over the course of 5 days from the time of order with instructions to add your Pupillary Distance and/or upload your prescription.

CAN I ORDER MORE THAN ONE PAIR OF PRESCRIPTION EYEGLASSES?

Yes, up to 10 pairs of eyeglasses can be ordered at a time. Each additional pair of eyeglasses must be added individually (one at a time) even if it is identical to the original. The quantity in the cart cannot be modified to add additional pairs; however, the quantity can be adjusted to remove eyeglass items prior to checkout.

CAN ONLINE FRAMES BE ORDERED AT THE WAREHOUSE?

The frames available online are unique to Costco.com and are not available to be ordered at the warehouse.

CAN I ORDER FRAMES WITHOUT THE LENSES ON COSTCO.COM?

No, only complete eyeglasses can be ordered online.

DOES COSTCO.COM SELL NON-PRESCRIPTION EYEGLASSES?

Yes, non-prescription clear and sunglass lenses are available for purchase with all available frames on Costco.com. Choose ’No Prescription’ when selecting your Single Vision type.

WHAT TREATMENTS AND/OR OPTIONS ARE AVAILABLE ON LENSES?

The following treatments are included on all prescription and non-prescription eyeglasses:

  • Anti-Reflective and Bluelight Filter Treatment.
  • UV filters that block harmful UVA and UVB lightwaves.
  • All lenses are scratch resistant.

Additional add-ons include:

  • Clear and Transitions (light adaptive) lenses with a Hi-Index 1.60 lens material.
  • Satin finish edge polish is an available upgrade for all lenses.

Upgrade options include (for an additional charge):

  • Hi-Index 1.67 ultra thin lenses.
  • Blue, Silver, or Gold Flash Mirror is an available upgrade for prescription and non-prescription polarized lenses.

Costco.com currently offers only single vision and non-prescription lenses. However, the Optical Department within your local Costco Warehouse may be able to assist with ordering eyeglasses with BAL or Balance, bifocals, or progressive lenses.

HOW SHOULD I CLEAN MY NEW PRESCRIPTION EYEGLASSES?

It is recommended to clean the eyeglasses with warm water and an eyeglass cleaning solution, then blot the lenses dry with the included microfiber cloth.

CAN I SPLIT THE PAYMENT BETWEEN MY FLEXIBLE SPENDING ACCOUNT (FSA) AND A DIFFERENT CARD?

No, Costco.com does not offer the option to split the payment between more than one method, unless the second method of payment is a Costco Shop Card. The Flexible Spending Account (FSA) card can be used as payment like any other credit or debit card that is accepted on Costco.com.

DOES COSTCO ACCEPT VISION INSURANCE?

Unfortunately, Costco.com does not accept vision insurance; however, vision insurance may be accepted within the Optical Department in your local Costco Warehouse. You may also contact your insurance provider to inquire about possible reimbursement for your online purchase.

WHAT DO I DO IF I’M EXPERIENCING ISSUES WITH MY PRESCRIPTION OR REQUIRE AN ADJUSTMENT/REPAIR?

Please visit the Optical Department within your local Costco Warehouse for assistance.

WHAT IS THE RETURN POLICY ON PRESCRIPTION EYEGLASSES?

Costco has a 100% member satisfaction guarantee. If the prescription eyeglasses are unsatisfactory for any reason, you may return them to the Optical Department in your local Costco Warehouse for a repair, replacement, or refund.

DOES COSTCO.COM OFFER EXPEDITED DELIVERY FOR PRESCRIPTION EYEGLASSES?

No, Costco.com does not offer expedited delivery on prescription eyeglasses at this time.

The estimated delivery time is approximately 7-10 business days from the time of order including manufacturing. The eyeglasses are manufactured in the U.S. by Costco’s Optical Labs using the latest technology. Manufacturing cannot begin until your prescription has been provided.

Once the order ships, an automated email containing the tracking information will be sent. The order can also be tracked through the Orders & Purchases tab of your Costco.com account.

WHAT IS INCLUDED IN THE PACKAGE?

The following items are included with each pair of prescription eyeglasses:

  • One pair of eyeglasses per shipment
  • A Kirkland Signature eyeglass case
  • One Costco cleaning cloth
  • A package insert describing use and care instructions, as well as the Costco member satisfaction guarantee and Eyeglass Member Service contact information and operating hours.

Why did Costco discontinue the Polish Dog?

Updated 03/10/2026

Title

Why did Costco discontinue the Polish Dog?

Body

In order to simplify our menu and make room for healthier options, we have decided to offer only the all-beef Hot Dog. Sales show this is what the majority of members prefer. Though we understand many members loved the Polish Dog, we hope you enjoy our new offerings.

If you would like to share your feedback with us, please visit the Costco.com homepage and select the blue “Feedback” tab on the right side of the page.

You can find more detailed instructions on submitting your feedback here.

Part of Costco’s mission is to provide the best quality products at the lowest possible prices to our members. To accomplish that, we sell many of our products as part of a set or bundle. Selling or changing individual items in a bundle isn’t possible, since our products come bundled that way by the manufacturer.

Costco Wholesale’s Forest Conservation Commitment

Costco Wholesale’s Forest Conservation Commitment

Does Costco accept manufacturer’s coupons?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Does Costco accept manufacturer’s coupons?

Body

Costco Wholesale doesn’t accept general manufacturer coupons for a simple enough reason: We distribute our own offers and savings to our members by mail and at our locations on many occasions throughout the year.

Costco makes every effort to offer consistently low prices because our buyers negotiate the best deals with our vendors. Manufacturers, meanwhile, often will simply “load” the cost of a coupon program into the original pricing of their product. Because it ultimately doesn’t provide any advantage to the member, we don’t permit our vendors or buyers to carry out that practice at Costco.

Does Costco sell GMO salmon?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Does Costco sell GMO salmon?

Body

Here at Costco, we strive to always be as transparent as possible when discussing our sourcing policies.

While the FDA has approved the sale of GMO salmon, we have not sold and don’t intend to sell GMO salmon at this time.

Does Costco Wholesale sell cloned animal products?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Does Costco Wholesale sell cloned animal products?

Body

We believe that you have a right to know about products sold here at Costco. At this time, Costco does not sell cloned animal products.

While the U.S. Food and Drug Administration has determined that milk and meat from cloned animals are safe for human consumption, we have only just begun what will be an extensive and ongoing conversation regarding future policies. Rest assured, however, that we are closely monitoring both the latest in research and in public input.

Shop Card FAQs

Updated 04/01/2026

Title

Shop Card FAQs

Body

Answers to frequently asked questions about the Costco Shop Card can be found below:

What is a Costco Digital Shop Card?

Are Costco Shop Cards refundable?

What is the daily Shop Card purchase limit?

Can I change the value of my Costco Shop Card or cancel the transaction?
I am having trouble viewing my Digital Shop Card email. How can I access it?
What should I do if my Costco Shop Card is lost or stolen?
Can I transfer my digital Costco Shop Card to someone else?
Can I transfer the balance of my Digital Shop Card to a physical Shop Card?
When will I receive the Shop Card from your recent promotion?
Can I use my Costco Shop Card to pay for my Same-day Delivery powered by Instacart order?
Can I use my Costco Shop Card on Costco Next?

WHAT IS A COSTCO SHOP CARD?

Costco Shop Cards provide an excellent alternative to carrying cash or credit cards and here’s why: they never expire, you can recharge your card, and they come in a full range of denominations.

To purchase a Costco Shop Card, you must be a Costco member at any Costco warehouse or online at Costco.com. When ordering a Costco Shop Card online, it will be shipped (or emailed) to the address you specify in your order.

If you’d like to add a gift message, consider purchasing through Costco.com, as that particular feature is available during the checkout process. Additionally, members may reload Costco Shop Cards at any Costco warehouse.

Non-members as well as members may use Costco Shop Cards to shop at any Costco location in the U.S., Canada, or Puerto Rico, as well as online at Costco.com. You can check your Costco Shop Card balance here.

Costco Shop Cards may be used toward a membership and merchandise.

WHAT IS A COSTCO DIGITAL SHOP CARD?

A Costco Digital Shop Card is an electronic gift card that is sent via email and redeemed using your smartphone or by bringing a printed copy with you when you visit a Costco location.

Costco Digital Shop Cards are available for purchase on Costco.com, and are issued for select promotions and a variety of other uses. Digital Shop Cards received from costco@digitalshopcard.costco.com are valid and yours to use!

Costco Digital Shop Cards may be used to shop at any Costco location or online at Costco.com and Costco.ca. At this time, Digital Shop Cards cannot be redeemed at Food Court kiosks.

I RECEIVED A LETTER FROM COSTCO THAT SAYS I HAVE AN UNCLAIMED SHOP CARD. I LOST THAT CARD. HOW DO I GET THE FUNDS?

If you received a letter from the Unclaimed Property Department at Costco, you may either visit the membership counter at your local Costco warehouse within 30 days from the date on the letter you received to have them assist you with getting a replacement Shop Card or you may follow the instructions on the letter to receive a check refund.

If it is after 30 days from the date on your letter, please visit your state’s unclaimed property website to request the funds. To locate your state’s website, open a new browser window and search “your state” followed by “unclaimed property”.

ARE COSTCO SHOP CARDS REFUNDABLE?

No, Shop Cards are non-refundable.

WHERE CAN I USE MY COSTCO SHOP CARD?

You may redeem your physical or digital Costco Shop Card in any Costco within the United States, Canada or Puerto Rico or online at Costco.com or Costco.ca. Costco Shop Cards cannot be redeemed at a cell phone carrier kiosk inside a Costco warehouse.

Redeeming your Costco Shop Card on Costco.com is easy! Here’s how:

  1. During check out, select Add in the Costco Shop Card section, then enter the unique Costco Shop Card number, which you’ll find on the back of your Costco Shop Card. It’s a unique number and is used to identify your Costco Shop Card. For digital Costco Shop Cards, you’ll find card numbers and the PIN in the emailed receipt.
  2. Then, enter the four digit PIN to complete the process.

If you’d like to know your Costco Shop Card’s available balance, you can view the balance via the checkout prompt.

CAN COSTCO SHOP CARDS OR DIGITAL SHOP CARDS BE REDEEMED FOR CASH?

Costco Shop Cards (physical and digital) are not redeemable for cash, except as required by law. The following states allow for cash back when your balance drops below a certain amount:

  • Balance less than $15: CA
  • Balance less than $5: CO, HI, ME, MA, MT, NJ, OR, WA
  • Balance less than $2.50: TX
  • Balance less than $1: RI, VT

HOW DO I ACTIVATE MY SHOP CARD?

To activate a Shop Card purchased on costco.com:

  • Call 1-866-781-1014 and follow the prompts.

To activate a Shop Card received from a promotion:

  • Visit costcoshopcard.com and follow the prompts.

If you have trouble activating your Costco Shop Card, please visit the membership desk at your local warehouse OR call 1-800-774-2678 and select option 2, 2, then 3.

HOW CAN I CHECK MY COSTCO SHOP CARD BALANCE?

Members may check their balance at Costco.com. Here’s how:

  1. Visit the homepage, and find Costco Shop Card Balance (located near the bottom).
  2. Enter your Costco Shop Card number. This number can be found on the back of your Costco Shop Card. It’s a unique number and is used to identify your Costco Shop Card.
  3. Type in the four-digit PIN, which is also located on the back of your Costco Shop Card.
  4. Select the Check Balance button. The balance should immediately be displayed.

Costco Digital Shop Card balances can be checked the same way! Simply use the Shop Card number and PIN found in your email.

CAN I RELOAD MY COSTCO SHOP CARD OR DIGITAL SHOP CARD ONLINE?

As of now, Costco does not provide a reloading option for Costco Shop Cards (physical or digital) online. To reload your card, simply stop by any warehouse register and a team member will be happy to assist you.

Something to keep in mind: you can always check your Costco Shop Card balance on Costco.com.

WHAT IS THE DAILY SHOP CARD PURCHASE LIMIT?

Governing laws require us to limit the total value we can sell of certain gift cards and similar items to $10,000 or less per day. This limit applies to all purchases made at Costco in a single day, whether in-person, online, or both.

WHERE IS MY COSTCO SHOP CARD ORDER?

All physical Costco Shop Card orders are processed the following business day. USPS First Class Mail delivery times cannot be guaranteed but generally take 7-10 business days to arrive. If you purchased expedited shipping, you can visit FedEx to track your shipment. For further assistance, please contact us at 1-800-955-2292.

All digital Costco Shop Card orders are usually fulfilled within 2 hours, but may take up to 24 hours to process. If you need to resend the email, visit the tracking link in your Orders & Purchases.

I WAS SUPPOSED TO RECEIVE A DIGITAL SHOP CARD FROM COSTCO, BUT IT HAS NOT ARRIVED. WHAT SHOULD I DO?

Occasionally Digital Shop Cards are intercepted by spam filters; please check your spam folder for an email from costco@digitalshopcard.costco.com.

If you purchased the Digital Shop Card on Costco.com, you can use the Track My Package feature in Orders & Purchases to resend the email.

If you didn’t purchase it on Costco.com and are still unable to locate it, please contact us for further assistance.

CAN I CHANGE THE VALUE OF MY COSTCO SHOP CARD OR CANCEL THE TRANSACTION?

We’re sorry, but all Costco Shop Card transactions are final. If you want to increase the Shop Card value, then a new transaction will be required.

I AM HAVING TROUBLE VIEWING MY DIGITAL SHOP CARD EMAIL. HOW CAN I ACCESS IT?

Some email systems and browsers are set to block images and pop-ups, which can prevent a recipient from receiving their Digital Shop Card. Please select the “via web browser” link, which is located in the upper right hand corner of the email. Also, check to ensure any pop-up blockers are temporarily disabled; this is typically a yellow box of text just below the browser tabs.

WHAT SHOULD I DO IF MY COSTCO SHOP CARD IS LOST OR STOLEN?

Costco is not responsible for lost or stolen cards and unfortunately, they cannot be replaced.

If you misplaced your Digital Costco Shop Card, select the link in the original email to access it.

If you purchased the Digital Shop Card on Costco.com, you can resend the email to yourself using the tracking link in your Orders & Purchases.

If you didn’t purchase the Shop Card on Costco.com and no longer have the original email, please contact us for further assistance.

CAN I TRANSFER MY DIGITAL COSTCO SHOP CARD TO SOMEONE ELSE?

Yes. Simply forward the email to the new person.

CAN I TRANSFER THE BALANCE OF MY DIGITAL SHOP CARD TO A PHYSICAL SHOP CARD?

Yes. You can take your Digital Shop Card to your local warehouse, where you can use it to purchase a physical Shop Card of the same value.

WHEN WILL I RECEIVE THE SHOP CARD FROM YOUR RECENT PROMOTION?

If you made an online purchase that qualifies you for a promotional Shop Card, please review your order confirmation email for the Shop Card delivery timeframe.

CAN I USE MY COSTCO SHOP CARD TO PAY FOR MY SAME-DAY DELIVERY POWERED BY INSTACART?

The Costco Shop Card cannot by used as a form of payment for Same-day Delivery powered by Instacart orders. A list of acceptable forms of payment can be found here.

If you would like to use a Costco Shop Card to pay for your order, we recommend utilizing our 2-Day Delivery option.

CAN I USE MY COSTCO SHOP CARD ON COSTCO NEXT?

The Costco Shop Card cannot be used as a form of payment on Costco Next. For more information about Costco Next, visit What is Costco Next?.

What documents will I receive with my Costco.com jewelry order?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

What documents will I receive with my Costco.com jewelry order?

Body

Here at Costco, we are committed to creating well-crafted and comfortable jewelry. Costco’s experienced Buying Team works with our suppliers to select timeless styles, and our team of Graduate Gemologists inspects each piece for quality craftsmanship.

All diamond jewelry items from Costco come with some type of certification or documentation. Depending on the characteristics of the item, it may come with one or more of the following reports:

Costco Article of Description
When you purchase an article of jewelry with a diamond of less than 1.00ct, you will receive an Article of Description written by one of our Gemologists, with a degree from GIA, detailing your jewelry piece. Due to fluctuations in the commodity markets of diamonds, platinum and gold, the Article of Description provided with each item does not state the estimated appraised value. If you are inquiring about the retail replacement value for insurance purposes, we recommend that you visit your local independent jewelry appraiser.

GIA Diamond Grading Report
When you purchase an article of jewelry with a 1.00ct center diamond or larger, you will receive a Diamond Grading Report from the Gemological Institute of America (GIA) detailing and mapping the diamond.

Jewelry Grading/Gemological Report
When you purchase an article of jewelry with a 1.00ct center diamond or larger, it will also include a Jewelry Grading/Gemological Report describing the jewelry piece. This report will not have a monetary value listed, however, you can contact the company that issued the report and request an insurance appraisal. In this case your Costco purchase price will be documented as the insurance replacement value.

What does basic installation for appliances include?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

What does basic installation for appliances include?

Body

Our delivery and installation teams are not authorized to perform any modifications to the home; no carpentry, plumbing or electrical work. Any work that would exceed the included basic installation will require additional payment by you to the installer. For your convenience and while at your home, the installer will be able to determine if any additional work and or permits are needed that would exceed the included basic installation. You will always be given a quote before work starts. You will be responsible for direct payment to the installer for any work exceeding basic installation.

Basic installation varies depending on the appliance you are purchasing. For the most accurate information about installation, please see the product page of the item you are considering for purchase. Be prepared for your delivery by verifying your plumbing and electrical connections and reading the Delivery Guidelines PDF in the Manuals, Information & Guides section of the appliance’s product page.

What is Apeel and does Costco purchase items that have it?

Updated 02/25/2026

Title

What is Apeel and does Costco purchase items that have it?

Body

Apeel is an organically certified, edible coating that is applied to certain fruits and vegetables to help keep moisture in. It is approved for use in multiple countries.

Costco is not currently purchasing items with Apeel on them.

What is Costco’s statement on eggs?

Costco is committed to procuring cage‐free eggs and continues to increase the percentage of cage-free eggs it sells worldwide. In the U.S., Costco has increased its percentage of cage-free shell eggs to 97% as of September 2022. In addition, Kirkland Signature™ Liquid Eggs are 100% cage-free in the U.S. The transition to cage-free eggs will continue to increase with added availability and capacity of cage-free production.

What’s Costco’s plan to reduce the use of harmful chemicals?

Updated 09/01/2023

Title

What’s Costco’s plan to reduce the use of harmful chemicals?

Body

Costco historically has tested many different consumer product categories for chemicals regulated in the countries where we do business. Examples of these products include items found in and around the home, such as toys, products intended for children and food use, furniture, electrical, apparel, and home textiles. Examples of the regulated chemicals include soluble heavy metals, heavy metals found in surface coating, SVHCs (Substance of Very High Concern), formaldehyde, flame retardants, dyes, phthalates, BPA and many others. Additionally Costco tests non-foods packaging for heavy metal exposure, per U.S. and UK regulations.

We continue to work with the chemical and consumer product industries to find appropriate replacements for many chemicals of concern and to develop a green approach to chemical use. Costco is working with major third-party laboratories to test certain items for chemicals of regulatory and social concern. We call it the Costco Smart Screening Program, and have begun its implementation.

Returns & Exchanges

Can I return jewelry purchased on Costco.com?

Updated 06/04/2024

Title

Can I return jewelry purchased on Costco.com?

Body

You may return jewelry purchased on Costco.com for a refund including all shipping and handling fees. There are two ways to return your jewelry:

  1. Stop by any Costco location with your item including any IGI and/or GIA certificates provided with your order. It’s important to note, diamonds over 1.00 carat will receive a Jewelry Credit Memo upon return. A Costco Graduate Gemologist will inspect for authenticity and process your refund accordingly. Please allow 3-5 business days for processing.

  2. If you can’t make it to a Costco location, you can contact us using the links on the left of this page. Please have the following information ready to help us with processing your request:
  • Your name
  • Order confirmation number
  • Phone number
  • The reason for your return
  • Whether or not credit card used for the order is still active for the refund

Once we receive this information, we’ll arrange for a UPS pickup and your item will be shipped to our Costco Graduate Gemologist to inspect for authenticity. This method of return takes a bit longer (up to two weeks due to shipping and verification). Your refund, including all shipping and handling fees, will be processed once verification is complete.

Does Costco offer an extended warranty on products?

Updated 01/24/2024

Title

Does Costco offer an extended warranty on products?

Body

We offer our members two ways to extend their warranty on many of the products sold at Costco:

Allstate Protection Plans:

Purchasing extended warranty coverage through Allstate Protection Plans.

Costco Technical and Warranty Services:

Costco extends the manufacturer’s warranty on the following items for up to 2 years from the date of warehouse purchase or date of delivery if the item was purchased online:

  • Televisions
  • Projectors
  • Computers*
  • Major Appliances†

*Excluding Touchscreen Tablets.
†Refrigerators (above 10 cu. ft.), freezers, ranges, cooktops, over-the-range microwaves, dishwashers, water heaters, washers and dryers.

See the manufacturer’s warranty for specific coverage terms. Costco reserves the right to fulfill the second-year warranty obligation through, at Costco’s choosing, repair, replacement, or a refund up to the purchase price. Common exclusions are data backup and software replacement, physical damage, or liquid damage. Costco Technical and Warranty Service advises backing up your computer frequently.

Call Costco Technical and Warranty Service at 1-866-861-0450 to obtain warranty information and assistance.

  • English and Spanish speaking agents available.
  • All calls are quickly answered and handled in the U.S.
  • This service is available exclusively for U.S. Costco members.

Benefits of Using Both Options:

Using both Allstate Protection Plans and Costco Technical and Warranty Services can get you up to 5 years* of warranty coverage on qualifying products!

*For example, if you purchase a television and also purchase an Allstate Protection Plan, the total warranty coverage is 5 years (the first 2 years through Costco Technical and Warranty Service and the following 3 covered by Allstate). Please see each warranty for specific coverage terms.

These are just some of the benefits of membership - Thank you for being a Costco Member!

How are freight returns handled?

Updated 03/11/2026

Title

How are freight returns handled?

Body

If your item was delivered by a freight carrier, it will be picked up the same way it was delivered (Curbside or Threshold or White Glove). There are a few exceptions, however: freight pickup does not include Special Order Kiosk, Custom Installed Programs, or Costco Warehouse Special Event purchases that may include delivery.

When the return is initiated, the carrier will contact you within 5-7 business days using the phone number listed on your order. You can set up a time for them to come by and pick up your order.

Once we have confirmed the order is picked up and in transit, we’ll process your refund. You’ll receive a confirmation email once we’ve issued your credit. We ask that you allow a few business days for your credit card company or bank to process the credit.

How can I reschedule or cancel the pickup of my large item return?

Updated 09/01/2023

Title

How can I reschedule or cancel the pickup of my large item return?

Body

To reschedule or cancel the pickup of a large item return:

  1. Visit Costco.com and select Orders & Returns
  2. Select the order that contains the item being returned.
  3. Select View Return/Refund Status.
  4. Select to either Reschedule Return Pickup or Cancel Return accordingly.*

*A pickup cannot be rescheduled if you are within 72 hours of the originally selected date, as your pickup has already been placed on a truck.

If Reschedule Return Pickup was selected, follow these steps to complete the process:

  1. Select New Delivery Date.
  2. When the calendar appears, select a day for pickup. Please keep in mind that only the dates in white can be chosen. Any grayed out dates are not available for the reschedule.
  3. If successful, you will receive a confirmation message. If not, a red box will display instructing you to Contact Us for assistance.

Once completed, you will receive a confirmation email of the change/cancellation.

When will I see my Costco.com refund?

Updated 05/22/2026

Title

When will I see my Costco.com refund?

Body

If you’re returning an item you purchased on Costco.com, the typical time frame for the refund to be issued depends on how it’s being returned:

  • UPS Return Label: 2-3 business days after the label has been scanned by UPS
  • UPS Mail Innovations: 2-3 business days after the label has been used to return the item
  • UPS Pickup: 2-3 business days after UPS takes the item
  • Freight Pickup: 2 business days after the carrier takes the item

Some items may require an additional 3-10 business days for verification.

Once the refund is issued, it may take additional time before you receive it, depending on the refund method:

  • Credit/Debit Card: The refund will appear on your card statement in accordance with the policies of your financial institution.
  • Check: You will receive the check in the mail within 15 business days.
  • Shop Card: The Shop Card used for the order will be reloaded with the refund amount within 48 hours. If we’re unable to reload the original Shop Card, a new Digital Shop Card will be issued from costco@digitalshopcard.costco.com within 1-2 business days.

Title

Can I get a duplicate receipt for my warehouse purchase?

Body

There are two ways to obtain a duplicate receipt for an in-warehouse purchase:

  • If your purchase was done within the last two years, you can view or print a copy of your receipt from your Costco.com account. Sign in to your Costco.com account, select Orders & Purchases, and select the In-Warehouse tab. Find the purchase you’re looking for then select View Receipt. Select Print Receipt to print it out.
    • Don’t have a Costco.com account? Find out how to set one up.
  • If your purchase is older than 2 years, please visit the membership counter of your local warehouse with your picture ID, Costco membership number, and the date of your purchase. We’d be happy to print a copy for you.

How do I view my receipts for in-warehouse purchases?

Title

How do I view my receipts for in-warehouse purchases?

Before in-warehouse purchase receipts can be viewed, your membership must be verified. Once this is done, you can sign in to your Costco.com account and follow the steps below. Don’t have a Costco.com account? Find out how to set one up.

Receipts are shown in six month increments for up to two years’ worth of in-warehouse purchases. To change the time frame, select a six month period in the Showing dropdown. If you need a receipt for a purchase older than two years, please visit the membership desk at your local warehouse.

It can take up to 24 hours for new in-warehouse purchases to reflect in your purchase history.

Do you charge sales tax on discounts that are taken from displayed selling prices?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Do you charge sales tax on discounts that are taken from displayed selling prices?

Body

Taxability can vary from item to item, depending on the law of the state that governs taxation, whether Costco Wholesale or a supplier or both are providing funding for the discounts, and the terms of the agreement between Costco and its supplier. We seek to structure arrangements with our suppliers to allow us to charge our customers the lowest tax required by law.

How can I place an order with holiday sales tax exemption?

Updated 03/03/2026

Title

How can I place an order with holiday sales tax exemption?

Body

If your order is being shipped to a state with a tax holiday, please email your request to taxrequest@costco.com with the following subject line: “Sales Tax Holiday Request.”

In the body of the email, we ask that you please include:

  • Your membership number
  • Your first and last name
  • The Costco.com order number
  • The item submitted for consideration (please include the item number and product description)
  • The state in which the tax holiday occurred
  • Whether this purchase was for personal or business use

Once we get your email, we’ll be happy to issue a credit for tax on any qualifying purchases after the order has shipped. Note: only orders placed during the tax holiday qualify for a tax refund.

How do I purchase for tax exempt or resale on Costco.com?

Updated 08/29/2023

Title

How do I purchase for tax exempt or resale on Costco.com?

Body

Currently, Costco.com doesn’t offer sales tax exemption at the time of sale. For resale, Costco.com requires the appropriate resale certificate from the state where the shipment was delivered. For exempt organizations, such as schools and nonprofit organizations, most states require that purchases be made with a credit card in that name of the tax-exempt business or agency. Payment with personal funds to be reimbursed by the organization may nullify an exemption.

Our ability to provide you a refund of the tax you paid on your purchase will be based on the Ship-to state’s resale/tax exemption laws. If your purchase doesn’t meet that state’s guidelines, Costco won’t be able to refund the tax.

Our Tax Department will process your exemption after you’ve placed your order and submitted the following documentation:

  • A copy of your resale/tax exempt documentation.
  • A copy of the Costco.com shipping confirmation email from your purchase or printed receipt from your online “Order Status” page.
  • A sales tax exemption claim form. You can download a PDF of our sales tax exemption claim form right here or by copying and pasting the following URL into the address bar of your web browser: https://goo.gl/LXu8NA
  • The completed and signed sales tax exemption claim form MUST be included with your request. The Tax Department will respond to your exemption request with either a sales tax refund or an explanation why a refund was denied.

All Costco memberships will continue to include one free card for a designated member of the household who is 16 years of age or over.

Our membership fees help to offset operational costs so we can keep our prices low. A modest increase in fees will allow us to bring our members even greater value. To learn more about why we charge a membership fee, click here.

New Sign-ups
New members will be charged the new fee structure effective Sept.1, 2024.

Renewals
The fee increase took place beginning with September 2024 renewals, which members began receiving in mid-July.

Auto Renew
Memberships that are auto renewed will be charged the new fee effective Sept. 1, 2024.

Upgrades
All Executive upgrades will be based on the new fee structure effective Sept. 1, 2024.

Executive
Effective Sept. 1, we also increased the cap on the Executive Membership 2% Reward from $1,000 per year to $1,250 per year.

What is a Mattress Recycle Fee?

Updated 04/01/2026

Title

What is a Mattress Recycle Fee?

Body

Costco is required to collect mattress fees in California, Connecticut and Rhode Island in addition to any other taxes legally due.

Please keep in mind that the following fees are charged per unit, meaning each mattress and each box spring piece is considered a separate unit:

  • California - Charges a flat rate fee of $18.00, regardless of the mattress size or type.
  • Connecticut - Charges a flat rate fee of $16.00, regardless of the mattress size or type.
  • Oregon - Charges a flat rate fee of $22.50, regardless of the mattress size or type.
  • Rhode Island - Charges a flat rate fee of $20.50, regardless of the mattress size or type.

The mattress recycling fee is applied if the shipping address is in any of the above states.

What is the auto battery core fee?

Updated 02/25/2025

Title

What is the auto battery core fee?

Body

Core fees for auto batteries are a legal requirement and non-negotiable. This fee is automatically charged at the time of purchase and is refunded once the used battery is returned.

In an effort to continue our commitment to sustainability, members are encouraged to recycle their batteries rather than disposing of them in landfills. Please keep in mind that the core fee must be paid, even if there is no old battery to return.

Where can I find the shipping and handling charges?

Updated 09/01/2023

Title

Where can I find the shipping and handling charges?

Body

Shipping and handling info is usually found to the right of the product picture on its detail page. If shipping and handling is included, there won’t be an extra charge.

If you see “plus shipping and handling” to the right of the picture, the fee will be calculated using your shipping ZIP code. You can view the calculated fee when you proceed to checkout (before you purchase the item) so that there aren’t any surprises.

Why does Costco charge sales tax on orders?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Why does Costco charge sales tax on orders?

Body

You can shop confidently at Costco because we operate responsibly. That means we follow state and local laws, including those that require us to collect sales tax. The law that applies to the location where your delivery is received will determine if you need to pay tax on your purchase.

If an order is delivered to an address in the following states and territories, you can expect sales or use tax on both merchandise and shipping and handling charges: AL, AK (Juneau only), AZ, AR, CA, CO, CT, DC, FL, GA, HI, IL, IN, KS, KY, LA, MD, ME, MI, MN, MO, MS, NE, NV, NJ, NM, NY, NC, ND, OH, PA, PR, RI, SC, SD, TN, TX, VT, VA, WA, WI, WV, and WY.

However, if an order is delivered to an address in the following states, sales or use tax is charged on merchandise only: ID, IA, MA, OK, and UT.

Will I be charged a California Electronic Waste fee?

Updated 03/03/2026

Title

Will I be charged a California Electronic Waste fee?

Body

On January 1, 2005, retailers began collecting the Electronic Waste Recycling Fee on covered electronic devices from consumers. “Covered” means covered by the law and includes most video display products, such as computer monitors and televisions. The fee is collected at the time of retail sale—including online and catalog sales—to California consumers.

Additional information is available here.

Warehouse

Are guns & personal firearms allowed in Costco Warehouses?

Updated 09/01/2023

Title

Are guns & personal firearms allowed in Costco Warehouses?

Body

Costco Wholesale is a membership-only warehouse club. It isn’t open to the general public. As such, it restricts membership to qualified individuals who agree to our membership conditions. We have the right, and the obligation to our members, to enact and enforce our membership rules.

By obtaining a Costco membership card, our members agree to comply with the Membership Rules and the Privileges and Conditions of membership. The Member Service Employees at the exit doors are also obliged to follow these rules.

At Costco, we don’t believe it’s necessary to bring firearms into our warehouses, except in the case of authorized law enforcement officers. For the protection of all our members and employees, we feel this is a reasonable and prudent precaution to ensure a pleasant shopping experience and safe workplace.

Our policy is meant to protect our members and employees in all warehouses around the world. This isn’t a new policy, and we don’t customize this policy for each city/county/state/country where we do business.

Our primary goal at Costco Wholesale is to keep our members happy. If you believe that our policy restricting members from bringing firearms into our warehouses is unfair or excessively burdensome, or you cannot agree to abide by this policy, Costco will promptly refund your annual membership fee upon request. Thank you for your understanding.

Are non-profit campaigns allowed on Costco property?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Are non-profit campaigns allowed on Costco property?

Body

Since we are a private membership club, our warehouses (including parking lots) are only open to Costco members and their guests. Nonprofit campaigns are not permitted on Costco property.

While there are many nonprofits we applaud, a court in California has ruled that we cannot allow certain nonprofit organizations onto our property while excluding other nonprofit organizations. Meaning, if we allow one, we have to allow them all.

Furthermore, if we allow nonprofit campaigns — which are open to the general public — to take place on Costco property, we cannot later choose to restrict access to our members and their guests.

Because these complexities might seriously impact your shopping experience, we have opted to adopt a policy that does not allow nonprofit campaigns to take place on Costco property.

Are service animals allowed in Costco Warehouses?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Are service animals allowed in Costco Warehouses?

Body

In compliance with the Americans with Disabilities Act (ADA), Costco has adopted a service animal policy. Be assured that Costco members accompanied by service animals have immediate access to our warehouses.

Costco is permitted under the ADA to ascertain whether the animal is a service animal. Accordingly, and in full compliance with the ADA, Costco employees may ask two questions in order to determine whether an animal is a service animal:

  • Is that a service animal?
  • What task or function is it trained to perform?

Our policy effectively provides notice to everyone encountering the service animal that:

  • The allowance of a service animal into our warehouse is in compliance with the Americans with Disabilities Act
  • The animal is trained to perform a task or function
  • Health and safety laws are not being broken

Does Costco have self-service or express lanes?

Updated 08/29/2023

Title

Does Costco have self-service or express lanes?

Body

At Costco, we strive to offer the best shopping experience for our members. We’ve found that most of our members purchase an average of sixteen items per visit, which would mean that, on average, the express aisle would be the least used of all our registers. That’s why we made the decision not to include express lanes—at least for now.

However, we have implemented the use of self-service lanes at select locations and may expand to more warehouses in the future.

Does Costco offer free shopping passes?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Does Costco offer free shopping passes?

Body

Costco does not offer free shopping passes. When you visit a Costco warehouse, you’ll discover all the valuable savings we have to offer. As a nonmember, you may still purchase prescriptions from a Costco pharmacy with cash, debit, Costco Shop Cards or a Visa® card. However, you must be a paid member to enter the warehouse and purchase any other items.

To pick up a membership application, please stop by the membership counter at any Costco warehouse or sign up online. We take great pride in the quality and savings we offer our members. If you’re ever dissatisfied, you can cancel your membership for a refund (of course, we’ll do our best to ensure this isn’t the case!).

Does Costco Wholesale make political contributions or provide other political support?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Does Costco Wholesale make political contributions or provide other political support?

Body

As a company, we respect the choices our members make, so we do not take positions on social issues, we are not involved in partisan politics, and we do not contribute money to political candidates or issues.

When you visit a Costco warehouse, we want you to feel welcome. And, after gathering much feedback, we have found that the majority of our members appreciate the fact that our atmosphere is devoid of politics—so we strive to keep it that way. Your comfort and satisfaction is our No. 1 priority.

Is Costco the same as Cosco?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Is Costco the same as Cosco?

Body

Costco and Cosco are two different and unrelated companies. We — Costco Wholesale — are a publicly owned company with corporate offices in Issaquah, Washington. We are committed to offering low warehouse prices on brand-name products, which includes everything from jewelry to electronic appliances (and more)!

Cosco, on the other hand, is a leading manufacturer of juvenile and home furnishing products.

To learn more about us, visit our About Us page. Learn more about Cosco here.

What is Costco’s Mission Statement and Code of Ethics?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

What is Costco’s Mission Statement and Code of Ethics?

Body

Here at Costco, we have a very straightforward, but important mission: to continually provide our members with quality goods and services at the lowest possible prices.

In order to achieve our mission, we will conduct our business with the following Code of Ethics in mind:

  • Obey the law.
  • Take care of our members.
  • Take care of our employees.
  • Respect our suppliers.

If we do these four things throughout our organization, then we will achieve our ultimate goal, which is to reward our shareholders.

Why aren’t shopping bags available at checkout?

Updated 08/29/2023

Title

Why aren’t shopping bags available at checkout?

Body

At Costco, we’re committed to providing our members with high-quality products at the lowest possible prices. Part of that means trimming down the costs associated with conventional wholesalers and retailers. Not only do shopping bags contribute to waste, they would also result in higher operating costs—and, ultimately, higher product pricing.

Why do I have to show my receipt at the door?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Why do I have to show my receipt at the door?

Body

It is standard practice at all our warehouse locations to verify purchase receipts when customers exit our buildings.

We do this to double-check that the items purchased have been correctly processed by our cashiers. It’s our most effective method of maintaining accuracy in inventory control, and it’s also a good way to ensure that our members have been charged properly for their purchases.

Why do prices vary between Costco Warehouse locations?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Why do prices vary between Costco Warehouse locations?

Body

Many factors play into why there can be price differences on the same items at different Costco warehouse locations. Our location managers and buying teams keep a close watch on inventory and pricing. At times, our suppliers may adjust item prices on additional shipments. Sometimes, local competition might affect the price of that item.

We apologize for any inconvenience our pricing fluctuations from location to location may have caused. We encourage you to speak to a manager at your local Costco for further assistance regarding any price difference on your particular purchase.

Why does Costco have a treasure hunt atmosphere?

Updated 08/28/2023

Title

Why does Costco have a treasure hunt atmosphere?

Body

One of the most exciting things about shopping in our warehouses is you never know the kind of incredible deals you’ll find from one visit to the next! Costco members know the trick to getting the best value on exclusive or one-time-buy merchandise: Visit often!

And, because we rotate out and introduce new merchandise all the time, we encourage you to purchase items that interest you sooner rather than later to avoid missing out.

Miscellaneous

Can I change the shipping address on an order I placed?

Updated 10/02/2025

Title

Can I change the shipping address on an order I placed?

Body

You can change a shipping address within 60 minutes of placing your order. Simply visit My Orders and select “Order Details” on the order you want to update. Under the Shipping Address, click “Change” to make your edits.

You can change a shipping address within 60 minutes of placing your order. Simply visit Orders and Returns and select “View Order Details” on the order you want to update. Under the Shipping Address, select “Change” to make your edits.

Note: GE/Haier appliances, Google Play and iTunes codes, floral items, caskets, and e-vouchers are ineligible for online address changes.

After 60 minutes, if your shipping address contains a minor error (wrong ZIP code, street, apt. number, etc.), or if the item is shipping through a freight carrier, please contact us so that we can determine if we will be able to make a change on your behalf.

In the event that you need a complete change in your shipping address information for a small package delivery, please contact us so that we can either attempt to cancel the order or request a “return to sender.” That way, you can place a new order using the updated address.

Changes that can occur with a shipping address change.

Costco Gas Station FAQs

Updated 08/22/2025

Title

Costco Gas Station FAQs

Body

As our valued member, you expect Costco to always offer the finest quality products at the best possible price. Our Kirkland Signature™ fuel is no exception to the quality and value you trust. For more information about quality, cash back, rewards, safety practices and FAQs visit us here: Kirkland Signature™ Gasoline. Members can also find “pre auth credit card hold” information on this page as well.

Q: Why does Costco “hold” my funds if I use a debit card?

A: When fuel is purchased with a PIN debit card or Visa card, Costco requests a “pre-authorization” from the card issuer before beginning the fueling process. This is a temporary hold that assures Costco will be paid for fuel dispensed. The “pre-authorized” amount is approved by the card issuer and may be up to $150. After fuel is dispensed, Costco IMMEDIATELY sends a “completion transaction” message to the card issuer with the actual purchase amount. It is the responsibility of the card issuer to remove the “pre-authorization” amount promptly. Please call the bank customer service number on the back of your card if you have questions or concerns.

Find your local Costco Gas Station Location, Hours & Prices

Costco Home Improvement Installation Services Payment FAQs

Updated 03/10/2026

Title

Costco Home Improvement Installation Services Payment FAQs

Body

Can I make payments online for home installation services?
How can I provide my payment information online?
How do I know if I have successfully provided my information to Costco?
Can I provide payment information to the Dealer instead of using Costco.com?
Can I provide payment information using a mobile device?
When will I be charged for my purchase?

CAN I MAKE PAYMENTS ONLINE FOR HOME INSTALLATION SERVICES?

Our convenient online payment method (for Costco Members only) allows you to securely provide your payment information for your Costco Installation Services purchase via Costco.com.

HOW CAN I PROVIDE MY PAYMENT INFORMATION ONLINE?

  1. Select the Submit Payment Information button provided in the email from costcoinstallationservices@costco.com (Costco Installation Services)
  2. Sign into your Costco.com account
    • If you do not have an account, you will need to create one
    • If you have a Costco.com account using a different email address and would like to link it to this order, please advise the dealer to update your email address for this order and they will resend an updated payment request to you
  3. Enter your payment information. You can enter multiple payment methods:
    • Option 1: Credit or Debit Card
      1. Enter the card number, expiration date, CVV code and cardholder name
      2. Select Add Card
      3. Enter the amount to be processed
      4. If additional credit and/or debit cards need to be entered, select the + Add a Debit or Credit Card button and repeat the steps above
    • Option 2: Costco Shop Card
      1. Select Add
      2. Enter the Shop Card number and the PIN
      3. Select Apply
      4. If additional Shop Card(s) need to be entered, select the Add Another Card button and repeat the steps above

Once the full balance of the order has been satisfied by the payment method(s) that have been entered, the remaining balance will display and you may click the green Submit Payment Information button.

HOW DO I KNOW IF I HAVE SUCCESSFULLY PROVIDED MY INFORMATION TO COSTCO?

Once your payment information has been submitted, you will receive a confirmation.

Example:

CAN I PROVIDE PAYMENT INFORMATION TO THE DEALER INSTEAD OF USING COSTCO.COM?

Yes, please reach out to the dealer referenced In the email you received.

CAN I PROVIDE PAYMENT INFORMATION USING A MOBILE DEVICE?

Yes.

WHEN WILL I BE CHARGED FOR MY PURCHASE?

  • If your order is installed in Maryland, Massachusetts, New Jersey, Ohio, Vermont or Wisconsin, you will be charged in full upon Costco’s receipt of your signature on the project completion paperwork.
  • For all other states, you will be charged after the state’s 3 or 5-day right-to-cancel period.

CAN I USE MY COSTCO WALLET FUNDS TO MAKE PAYMENTS FOR HOME INSTALLATION SERVICES?

The Costco Wallet cannot be used as a direct payment method for home installation services. However, you can use your Costco Wallet funds to purchase Costco Shop Cards online or at the Member Service area of any Costco location. Once purchased, these Shop Cards can be applied as a payment method on Costco.com, through the Costco mobile app, or via one of our affiliated home installation dealers.

HOW DO I VERIFY MY MEMBERSHIP?

Need help completing the one-time online membership verification process? Follow these step-by-step instructions:

  1. Sign in or register on the Mobile App.
  2. From the Menu go to the Membership section and select Account, then Account Details.
  3. Under Membership Information, select the Verify Membership link to begin the one-time verification process.
  4. Enter your Membership number.
  5. Enter your first name, last name, and ZIP code.
  6. Answer public record questions to verify your membership.*

After correctly answering security questions, you will see confirmation that your account has been verified and be able to view and edit all features associated with your membership.

Note: If you have already verified your membership online at Costco.com, you will not be asked to verify your membership when setting up your Costco Mobile App account.

*Questions used to confirm your identity and allow you to access your membership information online are provided by a third-party public records service. Costco does not have access to the questions or your answers, and Costco only receives a Pass or Fail notification from the third-party service. This process is necessary for the security and privacy of your personal information.

Learn More About the Costco App

HOW DO I RENEW MY MEMBERSHIP?

You can renew your Costco membership in the mobile app. Choose from two options:

  • From Menu go to the Membership section, select Renewal.
  • From Menu go to the Membership section, select Account then Renew Membership.

Learn More About the Costco App

WHY IS MY MEMBERSHIP RENEWAL NOT UPDATING?

After you renew your membership, please give 24 hours for the system to update and reflect your renewed status.

Learn More About the Costco App

HOW DO I CHANGE MY PASSWORD?

From the Menu go to the Membership section. Select Account then Account Details. Find Password and select the Edit button next to it.

Learn More About the Costco App

HOW DO I CHANGE MY COOKIE SETTINGS?

From the Menu go to the Other section. Select App Settings then Cookie Settings.

Learn More About the Costco App

HOW DO I RESET MY COSTCO APP DATA?

Resetting the data on the mobile app is easy. Choose from two options:

  • From the Menu go to the Other section and select Reset App Data.
  • From the Menu go to the Other section, select App Settings then Reset App Data.

Learn More About the Costco App
