****CALLS FROM “CHINESE COSTCO MEMBER SERVICE”****

We have been notified of people receiving scam calls disguised as our home office number (425-313-8100), Ecommerce number (800-955-2292), or Membership number (800-774-2678), stating they are from the “Costco Chinese Member Service.” The scammers will have the person’s name, address, and phone number and attempt to convince them that an account in China has been opened in their name that now has fraud activity and that the police have been notified. The scammers are offering to communicate with our members in Chinese, if they prefer.

Deli Platter Ordering in the Costco App FAQs

Updated 07/20/2026

Title

Deli Platter Ordering in the Costco App FAQs

Body

Where can I locate this feature in the Costco app?
Can I place a deli platter order on Costco.com?
I don’t see the option for deli platter ordering in the Costco app, what should I do?
Are there any requirements to purchase a deli platter order through the Costco app?
How quickly can deli platter orders be made after being placed?
How far in advance can I place a deli platter order?
Can I cancel or make changes to my deli platter order?
Can I place deli platter orders for pick-up in all U.S. and Canadian warehouse locations?
Is there a limit to the amount of deli platters I can order?
How can I view my deli platter order details after the order has been placed?
Can other Costco deli items be purchased in advance?
Will the Costco deli still take paper orders in the store?

WHERE CAN I LOCATE THIS FEATURE IN THE COSTCO APP?

The feature can be found under the Warehouse Ordering section of the Warehouse tab in the Costco app.

CAN I PLACE A DELI PLATTER ORDER ON COSTCO.COM?

Deli platter ordering is only available through the Costco app at this time.

I DON’T SEE THE OPTION FOR DELI PLATTER ORDERING IN THE COSTCO APP, WHAT SHOULD I DO?

If the option isn’t appearing under the Warehouse tab, ensure you have the most updated version of the Costco app.

ARE THERE ANY REQUIREMENTS TO PURCHASE A DELI PLATTER ORDER THROUGH THE COSTCO APP?

To place a deli platter order, you must be signed in to the Costco app and connected to a verified membership.

HOW QUICKLY CAN DELI PLATTER ORDERS BE MADE AFTER BEING PLACED?

Deli platter orders must be placed at least three days in advance.

HOW FAR IN ADVANCE CAN I PLACE A DELI PLATTER ORDER?

Deli platters can be ordered up to two weeks in advance.

CAN I CANCEL OR MAKE CHANGES TO MY DELI PLATTER ORDER?

To cancel or modify a deli platter order, please contact the Costco warehouse you selected for pick-up. More information can also be found in the confirmation email for your order.

CAN I PLACE DELI PLATTER ORDERS FOR PICK-UP IN ALL U.S. AND CANADIAN WAREHOUSE LOCATIONS?

Deli platter ordering through the Costco app is not available at warehouse 1076 (Juneau, AK), 1342 (Fairbanks, AK), or any Canadian warehouses.

IS THERE A LIMIT TO THE AMOUNT OF DELI PLATTERS I CAN ORDER?

Ordering on the Costco app is limited to five platters. For orders requiring more than five platters, please contact the Costco warehouse you are intending to pick up the orders from.

HOW CAN I VIEW MY DELI PLATTER ORDER DETAILS AFTER THE ORDER HAS BEEN PLACED?

After a deli platter order has been placed, you will receive an order confirmation email. This email will outline essential details, including designated pick-up locations, collection instructions, the estimated total cost, and procedures for order modification or cancellation.

CAN OTHER COSTCO DELI ITEMS BE PURCHASED IN ADVANCE?

Platters are the only items from the deli department that can be pre-ordered through the Costco mobile app.

WILL THE COSTCO DELI STILL TAKE PAPER ORDERS IN THE STORE?

Yes! We will continue to accept paper orders in the store.

Executive 2% Reward FAQs

Updated 06/05/2026

Title

Executive 2% Reward FAQs

Body

See below for answers to frequently asked questions about the Executive Membership 2% Reward:

Where can I use the Reward?
What purchases qualify for the Reward?
What if I believe the amount is incorrect?
Have I used my Reward?
How can I see my current 2% Reward estimate?
Can I receive my Reward early?
What if my Reward is less than the upgrade fee?
Can I use my Reward to renew my membership?
How many Executive Memberships can I have?
Is there a minimum or maximum Reward amount?
When does the 2% Reward begin accruing?
What does the date on the 2% Reward certificate represent?
What if I’m unable to redeem my Reward at the warehouse?
What’s the difference between the 2% Reward and the Citi Credit Card Reward Certificate?
What if I never received my 2% Reward Certificate?
Do you have to be an active member to redeem the Reward?
Why has my 2% Reward calculation amount decreased?

WHERE CAN I USE THE REWARD?

The Reward may be redeemed toward purchases of most merchandise through the front-end registers (excluding self-checkout) at Costco warehouses in the U.S.

For a list of exclusions, visit our website.

If you wish to use the reward amount towards a purchase on Costco.com, you may purchase a Costco Shop Card (which is redeemable online) with your reward at a warehouse location.

WHAT PURCHASES QUALIFY FOR THE REWARD?

The Reward is approximately 2% of pre-tax purchases (less refunds) of most merchandise purchased through: front-end registers, most ancillary business registers at Costco warehouses, and Costco websites in the U.S. and Canada by U.S. and Canadian residents. Also included are Special Order Kiosk item purchases as well as qualifying purchases made directly from Costco Travel* once travel is completed.

For a list of exclusions, visit our website.

Only purchases made by the Primary and active Primary Household Cardholder on the account will apply toward the Reward. If one of these cardholders are removed during the accumulation period, their purchases will be removed from the Reward amount.

**Starting June 4, 2026, Costco Travel is launching a partnership with the online travel platform Viator. This partnership will offer U.S. members access to over 400,000 activities, tours and excursions worldwide.

Members will book Viator events with a Costco-exclusive 15% discount automatically applied at checkout through CostcoTravel.com. However, at this time, any excursions booked through this partnership will not count towards the Executive Member Annual 2% Reward. Members can elect to book add-ons for their events through the co-branded website or by calling a dedicated 24/7 Viator phone number.*

WHAT IF I BELIEVE THE AMOUNT IS INCORRECT?

The Reward is not calculated on all purchases. For a list of exclusions, visit our website.

If you believe your issued Reward amount is incorrect, please contact us using the options on this page and speak with an agent.

HAVE I USED MY REWARD?

The 2% Reward is issued via the Renewal Statement, and reflects the Reward earned up until the issue date. It comes as a paper certificate and is redeemed at the register with a cashier. If you believe you should have received it and have not used it, please contact us using the options on this page and speak with an agent.

HOW CAN I SEE MY CURRENT 2% REWARD ESTIMATE?

If you’re the Primary member on an Executive Membership, you can check your current 2% Reward estimate by signing in to your Costco.com account. As long as you’re verified, you’ll see the estimate in the Membership Information section of your Account Details. Once your Reward is issued, your Costco.com account will show the estimate of your next Reward.

CAN I RECEIVE MY REWARD EARLY?

No, the 2% Reward cannot be issued early. It is issued approximately three months prior to the member’s renewal date, in the Renewal Statement, and reflects the Reward earned up until the issue date.

WHAT IF MY REWARD IS LESS THAN THE UPGRADE FEE?

The Reward is not guaranteed to be equal to or greater than the Executive upgrade fee paid. To receive a refund for the current Executive upgrade fee, the membership must be canceled or downgraded to a Gold Star or Business Membership. Any 2% Reward issued or accrued will be subtracted from the refund and forfeited to the extent it exceeds the amount of any refund.

There are additional benefits to being an Executive Member such as Costco Travel benefits and greater discounts on Costco Services. For more information, visit our website and select Services.

CAN I USE MY REWARD TO RENEW MY MEMBERSHIP?

You can use your Reward to renew your Costco membership at any front-end register (excluding self-checkout) or at the membership counter of your local Costco.

HOW MANY EXECUTIVE MEMBERSHIPS CAN I HAVE?

Limit one Executive membership per household and business. Costco reserves the right to treat businesses with the same or similar addresses or with identical, similar or related ownership as a single business and as such, limited to a single membership.

IS THERE A MINIMUM OR MAXIMUM REWARD AMOUNT?

Reward accumulations less than $1 will not be issued. The Reward is capped at and will not exceed $1,250 for any 12-month period.

WHEN DOES THE 2% REWARD BEGIN ACCRUING?

The Reward is calculated on purchases made after the Executive upgrade occurs. Purchases made prior to upgrade do not qualify for the 2% Reward.

WHAT DOES THE DATE ON THE 2% REWARD CERTIFICATE REPRESENT?

The date on the top right corner of the 2% Reward Certificate in the Renewal Statement is the date the Reward was issued. It is not an expiration date.

WHAT IF I’M UNABLE TO REDEEM MY REWARD AT THE WAREHOUSE?

A 2% Reward can be converted to a Costco Shop Card under the below circumstances only:

  • Incapacitated
  • Moved out of the area from a Costco (this could include Military)

As the Primary Cardholder or Account Administrator, you can request the 2% Reward certificate to be converted to a shop card. Please contact us using the options on this page to speak with an agent.

Please note that processing and mail delivery of the Costco Shop Card is approximately 7-10 days from receipt of your Reward certificate.

WHAT’S THE DIFFERENCE BETWEEN THE 2% REWARD AND THE CITI CREDIT CARD REWARD CERTIFICATE?

Costco Executive Members receive a 2% Reward on qualified purchases at Costco. The Reward is issued by Costco approximately 2.5 months prior to the member’s renewal date, in the Renewal Statement.

Costco Anywhere Visa® Card by Citi cardholders receive an annual Credit Card Reward Certificate on or shortly after the February credit card statement closing date. More information can be found on Citi’s website, or in their FAQs.

WHAT IF I NEVER RECEIVED MY 2% REWARD CERTIFICATE?

The Reward certificate is mailed with the Executive Member’s annual renewal notice and mailed to the Primary Member, approximately three months prior to the member’s renewal date. Rewards totaling less than $1 will not be sent. If the Reward is not received, the Primary Member or Account Administrator may contact us using the options on this page or visit the membership counter at any Costco warehouse.

DO YOU HAVE TO BE AN ACTIVE MEMBER TO REDEEM THE REWARD?

Yes, 2% Certificates can only be redeemed while making a purchase or renewing memberships, and you must be an active member to make purchases.

WHY HAS MY 2% REWARD CALCULATION AMOUNT DECREASED?

The 2% Reward calculation may be decreased for a few reasons, such as:

  • Purchases were returned, subtracting from your total eligible spending.
  • One of the Executive cardholders were removed or replaced, which removed their purchases from the 2% calculations.
  • Your 2% Reward was issued, which allowed your accumulation to restart.

How can I make changes to my account?

Updated 03/03/2026

Title

How can I make changes to my account?

Body

To update your account, begin by selecting Sign In / Register in the upper right-hand corner of the Costco.com homepage. Once you are signed in, select Account.

To change your Sign In email address:

  1. Choose Account Details in the Account menu.
  2. Select Edit next to Sign In Email.
  3. Enter your current email address and select Send Verification Code.
  4. Enter the verification code sent to your email address, then select Verify Code.
  5. Enter your new email address, then select Update.
  6. Use your new email address the next time you sign in at Costco.com.

To change your password:

Follow these steps to update your password.

To change your membership information:

Keep in mind, you will need to be the Primary Member or Account Manager and verified before you can make any membership changes online. Once verified, you can make changes to your membership such as phone number and address. Be sure to check the list of changes that can be made online. If the membership changes you are wanting to make are not listed, contact us.

  1. Choose Account Details in the Account menu.
  2. Select Edit next to the information you wish to change and enter your new information.
  3. Select Update.

To change your billing or shipping address:

Follow these steps to update your billing or shipping address information.

How can I manage membership Auto Renewal online?

Updated 06/26/2026

Title

How can I manage membership Auto Renewal online?

Body

Follow the steps below to add, remove or change your membership Auto Renewal settings: Go to Costco.com and select Sign In / Register in the upper right corner of the page.

Costco members may charge their membership fees automatically on any Visa® credit/debit card or Mastercard debit card. The card you set up for Auto Renewal will be charged on the first day of your renewal month.

Follow the steps below to add, remove or change your membership Auto Renewal settings.

  1. Go to Costco.com and select Sign In / Register in the upper right corner of the page (just make sure your membership is verified beforehand).
    • Proceed to the next step if you are signed in and see “Account” to the left of Orders & Returns.
  2. Under “Account,” choose Renew Membership.
  3. Add, remove or select a Visa® or Mastercard® card for Auto Renew.
  4. For the automatic renewal of your membership, check the box to confirm your consent of the following:
    • Your Costco membership fee will renew automatically and be charged annually on the first day of your renewal month until you choose a different form of payment or cancel Auto Renewal from your Costco membership.
    • Future membership fees will be charged based on your membership level. Currently, this is $65 per year for Gold Star and Business members, plus an additional $65 per year for Executive Members ($130 total), and $65 per year for each affiliate business member, plus applicable taxes.
    • Membership fees are subject to change. If you wish to cancel Auto Renewal or pay your Costco membership fee using a different method of payment, you can visit a Costco membership counter or access your membership account on Costco.com. You may also chat with Costco Member Services or call 1-800-774-2678 at least 72 hours prior to the first day of your renewal month.

Changes to your membership type or level must be completed at least two business days before your Auto Renewal takes place. If you want to let your membership expire at the end of your current membership year, remove Auto Renew at least two business days before the first day of your renewal month.

If you remove Auto Renewal within 48 hours of your Auto Renewal date (typically on the first of the month), your credit card on file will likely be charged. However, Costco will systematically refund the charge back to the card on file within two business days.

To make changes to your membership at the time of your next Auto Renewal, use the options on this page to contact us or visit the membership counter at your local Costco. If your membership was automatically renewed and you would like to request a refund, please click this link. Refunds take approximately 5-7 business days to process.

How can I provide feedback?

Updated 08/22/2025

Title

How can I provide feedback?

Body

Costco is always looking for ways to improve, so we’d love to hear any feedback you might have for us! To leave a comment, please follow the directions listed below based on the device you’re using:

  • Desktop or Laptop Computer: Head to Costco.com and click the Feedback tab on the right side of the screen or scroll to the bottom of the page and click Feedback.

  • Costco Mobile App: Tap the Account button, then select Feedback.

  • Mobile Browser: Scroll to the bottom of the page on Costco.com and tap Feedback or select the Feedback tab on the right side of the screen.

To submit feedback for a product featured online or to suggest a product not yet featured, select Website. To submit feedback for a product purchased at a warehouse, a suggestion for a product to be available at a warehouse, or feedback regarding warehouse service, select Warehouse.

When submitting feedback for a product featured online or to suggest a product not yet featured, enter your rating between Poor to Excellent. You’re welcome to add comments and rate how likely you are to recommend Costco to a friend or colleague. Once complete, select Send Feedback.

When submitting feedback for a product purchased at a warehouse, a suggestion for a product to be available at a warehouse, or feedback regarding warehouse service, enter a city, state or mailing code for a location to leave feedback about, then select Search.

Next, find the preferred warehouse location, then select Use This Location.

Next, enter your rating between Poor to Excellent. You’re welcome to add comments and rate on how likely you are to recommend Costco to a friend or colleague. If you would like to be contacted, please enter your information in the “Member Name” and “Member Email Address” fields. Once complete, select Submit.

How can I see if an item sold on Costco.com is also available at my local Costco?

Updated 03/11/2026

Title

How can I see if an item sold on Costco.com is also available at my local Costco?

Body

There are many ways to check for item availability at your local Costco warehouse.

For items sold both online and in the warehouse, you may be able to see the product availability in the warehouse on Costco.com

The first step to view the availability is to confirm that you have “My Warehouse” set to your desired Costco location. If it’s not at the proper location, follow these steps to adjust it:

  1. Select the location under “My Warehouse” in the upper left corner of Costco.com.
  2. Choose the desired warehouse by selecting “Set as My Warehouse”

If the warehouse inventory availability feature is active for the selected item at your chosen warehouse, you’ll see this information below the price on the right hand side of the product information page.

Important notes regarding online inventory:

  • If a product is only sold in the warehouse, its availability will not be displayed online.
  • Our online inventory isn’t a duplicate of our warehouse product inventory, but rather an extension of it. There are items you’ll find online that you won’t find in the warehouse, and vice versa.
  • Some items can be purchased online and shipped to your local warehouse for pickup. If a product is eligible, it will be noted on the product page beneath the “Add to Cart” button.
  • Many food items and household supplies found in our warehouse locations can also be delivered right to your door through our CostcoGrocery program.

You can also find product availability at your local Costco warehouse by:

  • Visiting the membership counter at your local Costco in person.
  • Calling your local warehouse and choosing the phone menu option for Membership.
  • Using the Contact Us options on this page.

It’s important to note that availability is subject to change, since we are unable to predict the volume of members purchasing the same product.

How can I verify my membership online?

Updated 05/28/2026

Title

How can I verify my membership online?

Body

Membership verification is NOT required to purchase Member Only Items on Costco.com.

When registering your account on Costco.com, it’s important to verify your identity to ensure the security and privacy of your personal information.

To complete the one-time online verification process, follow the instructions below:

  1. After signing in or registering on Costco.com, select Account and select Account Details from the menu on the right.
  2. If your membership number is not listed, enter your membership number to the account details.
  3. Click Verify Membership.
  4. Answer questions to verify your membership.*
  5. After correctly answering the questions, you’ll be able to view and edit features associated with your membership.

Unable to Successfully Verify Your Account?

Please complete the following steps:

  1. Visit the membership counter at your local Costco warehouse or use the Contact Us or** Chat Us** option on this page.
  2. Provide your membership card and photo ID at the membership counter. They will send you an email containing a link valid for 48 hours. If you don’t see the email, be sure to check your spam or junk folder.
  3. Upon receipt of your email, follow the steps provided to create a new password and access your membership account.

*Questions used to confirm your identity and allow you to access your membership information online are provided by a third-party public records service. Costco does not have access to the questions or your answers, and Costco only receives a Pass or Fail notification from the third-party service. This process is necessary for the security and privacy of your personal information.

How is sales tax calculated on my order?

Updated 09/18/2023

Title

How is sales tax calculated on my order?

Body

Many factors determine the sales tax charged on an order. The amount listed on the checkout page represents a sales tax estimate, which may be different than the sales tax charged when the order is finalized. Factors that contribute to how sales tax is calculated include:

  • Where the order is ultimately shipped to or picked up.
  • The tax rate in effect on the date the order is finalized.
  • The applicable state’s sourcing and sales tax laws.

Preventing Fraud

Updated 03/16/2026

Title

Preventing Fraud

Body

Currently Known Scams

Keeping our customers’ personal information secure and confidential is one of Costco Wholesale’s highest priorities. Below are some of the most common types of scams and suggestions on how you can be aware and help protect yourself against identity theft.

Phishing and Spoofing
Fictitious Job Offers
Pop-up Advertisements
Check Scam
Fraudulent Phone Calls
PayPal Phishing Scam
What Can You Do?

PHISHING AND SPOOFING

It is an unfortunate fact of the Internet that at any given time there are numerous illegitimate pop-up ads, surveys, websites, emails, social media posts and advertisements that purport to be from or authorized by Costco. This type of fraud is prolific, and it is common for bad actors to merely create new email addresses or websites from which to send their scam messages. This is something we can not prevent. It is unlikely that Costco is affiliated with these promotions. Rather, their purpose is to entice you to disclose personal information such as the following:

  • Name and address
  • Social Security Numbers
  • Credit card numbers / bank account numbers
  • PINs / passwords

They may also ask you to purchase something that may or may not be delivered to you.

We’ve provided some tips below to help you determine a promotion is authentic.

  • Unsolicited electronic communications from Costco do not ask for your personal information such as username, password, credit card information, birth date or Social Security number. Never provide personal information in response to an electronic communication.
  • If you receive an order confirmation for something you did not order from Costco.com, do not click on any links or open any attachments.
  • If you receive a communication that looks like it is from Costco, check to see who sent it. Be aware of typos and misspellings and, in particular, of return addresses and contact links that do not end with a plain “@costco.com.” The sender of any email communication from Costco would be from a Costco.com domain. When in doubt, don’t respond.
    • Digital Costco Shop Cards are the only exception to this rule. Digital Costco Shop Cards will arrive from the email: costco@digitalshopcard.costco.com
  • Be particularly cautious of pop-up solicitations, job-finding sites, and opportunities to win Costco Shop Cards.
  • Review the terms and conditions of any Costco-related app before downloading to ensure that the app is a genuine Costco app, created and supported by Costco.

Here is an example of a recent scam email with a few things to look out for:

  1. The message does not come from a costco.com domain and is not a Digital Costco Shop Card coming from costco@digitalshopcard.costco.com
  2. Repeated language, multiple links to the same location
  3. Hovering over a link shows a non-Costco url
  4. Fake address, disclaimer, and opt-out gives appearance of legitimacy

Here’s a recent example of a fraudulent website using Costco’s logo and images of warehouses:

  1. There are numerous spelling errors throughout the site
  2. The website domain name and email addresses on the site are not related to Costco.com
  3. Obvious grammar errors
  4. The time zone is a non-U.S.time zone; in this example, it gives a European time zone (GMT +2)

This is not an exhaustive list of the many ways you might become a victim of fraud or overreaching practices. The best policy is to stop and think before you click. Delete any questionable emails and texts, and be suspicious of any posts or ads with offers that are too good to be true. Visit https://www.stopthinkconnect.org{rel=“noopener” style=“text-decoration: underline;” target=“_blank”} for up-to-date advice about web safety and security. Additionally, Norton Internet Security has a useful article with instructions about how to get rid of spam emails.

The Federal Trade Commission (FTC) has additional information and alerts about email scams. We also encourage you to report rip-offs and imposter scams to the FTC as well as suspicious calls, emails, and text messages.

FICTITIOUS JOB-RELATED OFFERS

There are many illegitimate emails, messages in messaging apps, and texts that purport to be from Costco or advising that Costco Wholesale is offering or may offer them a job. These messages can be quite authentic-looking. They may contain a subject line such as “Provisional Job Offer,” and purport to have been sent directly from one of our executives or from our human resources department. Enclosures to emails, such as company information sheets and detailed questionnaires, may display the Costco Wholesale logo. The messages may include a request for payment of a processing fee of several hundred dollars and/or they may offer reimbursement for certain job application or relocation expenses. These messages are fraudulent. Costco Wholesale does not extend job offers via email, message app, or text to individuals with whom it has had no prior contact. We do not ask prospective employees to pay a fee to be considered for a position or to receive a job offer. We do not authorize recruiters or agents to do any of these things on our behalf. Although we sincerely regret that anyone may have been deceived by these fraudulent job offers, we will not honor them in any way.

You should not respond to any of these job offers by revealing personal information, nor should you send any funds in response to them. You can report job scams{saferedirecturl=“https://urldefense.com/v3/https://reportfraud.ftc.gov/?orgcode%3DCOST;!!OuofLoc!uhnk1pJGnrh3DQjS02iIpEWnw3p_1eYZr8jz5BXSLar3Zq_DHpwi98F_JG6ScjNkDKDIEArN3pp_JprHhWzaJtw6$ rel=”noopener” target=“_blank”} as well as suspicious calls, emails, and text messages to the U.S. Federal Trade Commission (FTC){saferedirecturl=“https://urldefense.com/v3/__https://reportfraud.ftc.gov/*/?orgcode%3DCOST__;Iw!!OuofLoc!uhnk1pJGnrh3DQjS02iIpEWnw3p_1eYZr8jz5BXSLar3Zq_DHpwi98F_JG6ScjNkDKDIEArN3pp_JprHhQHYrcT9$ rel=“noopener” target=“_blank”} and/or the FBI’s Internet Crime Complaint Center{saferedirecturl=“https://urldefense.com/v3/https://www.ic3.gov/;!!OuofLoc!uhnk1pJGnrh3DQjS02iIpEWnw3p_1eYZr8jz5BXSLar3Zq_DHpwi98F_JG6ScjNkDKDIEArN3pp_JprHheuUG4it$ rel=”noopener” target=“_blank”}.

POP-UP ADVERTISEMENTS

Some advertisements “pop up” in a separate browser window advising that you have won a contest or request that you participate in a survey to collect a prize. They may then ask that you provide personal information in order to receive your gift. By clicking on the link it is possible that you are also downloading viruses designed to capture or destroy information on your computer.

CHECK SCAM

A similar but low-tech scam circulates occasionally, in the form of a check, purportedly from Costco, and instructions to deposit it right away, usually in order to receive the balance of a larger sum you have “won” or that has otherwise come to you unexpectedly. You will be instructed to immediately send a payment, in the form of a second personal check, wire transfer, or similar, to some third party to cover taxes, processing fees, administrative costs, or some similarly vague expense. The check you received in the mail likely will look quite authentic, and probably has our Costco Wholesale trademark on it. Do not deposit the check or follow the instructions you receive with it. The check you deposit will bounce; the check you write or funds you wire will clear, before you know about the bounce, and certainly before you can effect stop payment on your own check.

FRAUDULENT PHONE CALLS

We have received reports of both members and non-members being contacted in an apparent phone scam/phishing scheme attempting to collect personal information. The caller may offer a $500 Costco Travel credit or Shop Card as a reward for completing a survey, or may be calling from the “Costco Complaint Department” and asking to provide some personal information. The fraudsters may be using software to show a local phone number on Caller ID, such as the number from your local Warehouse, but hide the real phone number the calls are originating from.

Here is an example:

Please know that these are fraudulent calls attempting to obtain personal information. These are in no way affiliated with Costco or Costco Travel. If you believe you have provided these callers your personal information, we recommend that you take appropriate steps such as notifying the consumer credit reporting agencies and signing up with a credit monitoring service. You can also report fraudulent calls as well as confirmed cases of identity theft to the U.S. Federal Trade Commission.

PAYPAL PHISHING SCAM

Our Legal Team has been investigating reports of phishing emails that appear to be sent by PayPal regarding a Costco.com flower order. Members have been understandably concerned when they don’t recognize the order or the recipient’s information.

Costco is not affiliated with these fraudulent emails and the emails don’t reference a legitimate order. Furthermore, we don’t accept PayPal as a payment method in our warehouses or on Costco.com.

We believe these emails have been sent to entice you to disclose personal information and/or to purchase something that may or may not be delivered to you. If you’ve received one of these emails, please delete it immediately and do not provide any personal information to the sender. You can also report such emails to the FTC.

WHAT CAN YOU DO?

  • Never respond to emails that cannot be verified.
  • Never provide personal information via e-mail.
  • Contact the business by using legitimate phone numbers to verify the request.
  • Enter websites using your browser and not by clicking on provided links.
  • Be cautious of any solicitation requesting that you deposit a check or pay a fee to collect a prize, get a job, or cover vaguely described “costs.”
  • Also consider reporting fraud to the U.S. Federal Trade Commission, the FBI’s Internet Crime Complaint Center, or your state Attorney General’s office.

Please use the Contact Us options on this page to report any suspicious emails or contacts that are using the Costco Wholesale name.

Price Match - Costco.com Orders

Updated 05/15/2026

Title

Price Match - Costco.com Orders

Body

Please read the following to understand what purchases may be eligible for a price match:

  • Items with specific sale dates (e.g., Holiday Savings Promotions): A price match request must be submitted within both the active promotional dates and 30 days of the purchase date. Requests submitted after the promotion has ended may be denied.
  • Eligibility is limited to non-resale members and/or products. Resellers must purchase items during valid promotional dates in order to receive the promotional price. Limits apply.
  • For members other than resellers, purchases that reduce in price within 30 days of the date of purchase are eligible for a price match. Products that have promotional item limits will be eligible up to the limit quantity.
  • Costco does not price match with other businesses or retailers.
  • Costco does not price match Costco warehouse prices for Costco.com purchases. Costco.com prices take into account shipping and handling fees not applicable to warehouse purchases. Please view this information to learn more about the difference between online and warehouse prices.
  • We reserve the right to deny a price match request at our sole discretion.
  • Costco may in the future restrict its price match policy. Restrictions will be shown at the point of purchase.

How to Request a Price Match

Request a price match

  • Precious Metals: Precious metals are not eligible for a price match and/or any promotions.
  • Costco.com purchases: Select the Request a Price Match button above and complete the form. Once approved, credits are typically issued within 5 to 10 business days to the original form of payment. Check if a promotional price was already deducted from your order
  • Costco warehouse purchases: If you purchased the item at a Costco warehouse location, please inquire at the Returns counter at the Costco location where you made the purchase.
  • Special Order Kiosk and Home Improvement Installed Products: Promotions are based off of qualifying products, the signed contract date and/or the pre-tax price. Exceptions to qualify for additional Costco Shop Card value are limited to the 14 days before and 14 days after the official promotion dates. Costco Shop Card promotions can not be combined with any other manufacturer consumer promotion or incentive. Please contact your Costco authorized dealer to determine if your project qualifies for past or current promotions.
  • Tire and Wheel Promotions: Tire and wheel purchases qualify for a price match if all other conditions are met. If the purchase was made online, please submit a request using the button above. If the purchase was made at the warehouse, please visit the warehouse where your purchase was made to inquire about a possible price match.

For additional assistance, please select a Contact Us option from this page.

Product Review FAQs

Updated 03/16/2026

Title

Product Review FAQs

Body

Below are answers to some frequently asked questions about Product Reviews:

How can I submit a product review?
What are the criteria for submitting a product review?

How can I remove or revise my product review?

Why am I not able to see product reviews?

HOW CAN I SUBMIT A PRODUCT REVIEW?

Your feedback is crucial to helping us improve our offerings.

Here are the steps to submit a review:

  1. Locate the product you want to review.
  2. Go to the “Reviews” section and select Write a review.
  3. Log in (if you haven’t already) and you can begin the review process.

Please remember, a verifiable purchase from Costco or Costco.com, of the product you are reviewing, is required.

Here are a few tips:

  • Create a nickname for yourself. It’s best not to use your real name!
  • Provide an overall product rating based on a scale of 1 to 5 stars (5 being best).
  • Give your review a title.
  • Share your opinion of the product in detail. You’re welcome to explain anything you liked or disliked about it.
  • Indicate if you’d recommend this product to a friend.
  • Enter your email address, membership number, and Costco.com order number (if available).
  • If the item is no longer available on Costco.com, or you have suggestions for new products, we value your thoughts and encourage you to submit your feedback.
  • For shipping or customer service issues, please use one of the Contact Us options on this page.

Please keep in mind that once you’ve submitted your review, it may take up to 72 hours to appear.

WHAT ARE THE CRITERIA FOR SUBMITTING A PRODUCT REVIEW?

Here at Costco, we’re always eager to gather your feedback. Something to keep in mind: all product reviews submitted on Costco.com are screened prior to posting. This is to ensure that they comply with our Terms and Conditions of Use.

We lean on the following criteria when screening reviews:

  • The focus is on specific product details
  • The product and model match what the member has purchased, and it must be a verifiable purchase from Costco

We also ask that you avoid the following:

  • Mentioning other sites or businesses
  • Using inappropriate language or tone
  • Divulging personal information
  • Including a URL in your review
  • Requesting different models, styles or colors of the item be carried

Once a review is approved, it should be live on the site within 72 hours.

HOW CAN I REMOVE OR REVISE MY PRODUCT REVIEW?

We are unable to accept revisions or updates to previously submitted reviews. If you have new feedback to provide on a product or would like to add more information, you may request removal of a previous review so you can create a new one for the product. Please allow 1-2 business days to process your request.

To request removal of your previous review, please submit the following form: Costco.com Review Deletion Request

WHY AM I NOT ABLE TO SEE PRODUCT REVIEWS?

We are unable to accept revisions or updates to previously submitted reviews

To stay legally compliant, the cookie settings for personalized advertising control the rendering of reviews on our site. If it is disabled, then reviews will not show up. Use the following steps to enable this feature:

1. On Costco.com, scroll down to the footer and select Cookie Settings.

2. Click Personalized Advertising in the left column.

  1. Next, toggle this option to Active and select Confirm My Choices. Product reviews should now be visible.

At your convenience, you can stop by your nearest Costco for a full refund.

    • Purchased on or between October 27th, 2023 through February 6th, 2024 in all US locations
  • Item # 11545 Chicken Street Taco Kits, due to the Cilantro Lime Crema sauce
    • Purchased on or between January 25, 2024 and February 6, 2024
    • A list of affected warehouses can be found here

Listeria FAQs

What is Listeria?

Listeria monocytogenes (L. monocytogenes) is a species of disease-causing bacteria that can be found in moist environments, soil, water, decaying vegetation and animals, and can survive and even grow under refrigeration and other food preservation measures. When people eat food contaminated with L. monocytogenes, they may develop a disease called listeriosis.

L. monocytogenes is generally transmitted when food is harvested, processed, prepared, packed, transported or stored in environments contaminated with L. monocytogenes. Environments can be contaminated by raw materials, water, soil, and incoming air. Pets can also spread the bacteria in the home environment if they eat food contaminated with L. monocytogenes.

Who is at risk?

Listeria is most likely to sicken:

  • pregnant people and newborns
  • adults aged 65 or older
  • people with weakened immune systems

Other people can be infected with Listeria, but they rarely become seriously ill. Pregnant people typically experience only fever, fatigue, and muscle aches. However, Listeria infection during pregnancy can lead to miscarriage, stillbirth, premature delivery, or life-threatening infection of the newborn.

What are the symptoms?

Symptoms usually start within two weeks after eating food contaminated with Listeria but may start as early as the same day or as late as 10 weeks after. Mild symptoms may include a fever, muscle aches, nausea, tiredness, vomiting, and diarrhea. If the more severe form of listeriosis develops, symptoms may include headache, stiff neck, confusion, loss of balance, and convulsions.

Call your healthcare provider right away if you have symptoms of a Listeria infection.

How can I prevent foodborne illnesses at home?

The longer ready-to-eat refrigerated foods contaminated with L. monocytogenes are stored in the refrigerator, the more opportunity this pathogen has to grow. To slow down or prevent the growth of L. monocytogenes, set the refrigerator to 40 degrees Fahrenheit (4 degrees Celsius), and the freezer to 0 degrees Fahrenheit (-18 degrees Celsius).

Consumers should also follow these simple steps:

  • Wash the inside walls and shelves of the refrigerator, cutting boards and countertops, and utensils that may have contacted contaminated foods; then sanitize them with a solution of one tablespoon of chlorine bleach to one gallon of hot water; dry with a clean cloth or paper towel that has not been previously used.
  • Wipe up spills in the refrigerator immediately and clean the refrigerator regularly.
  • Wash hands with warm water and soap for at least 20 seconds before and after handling food and following any cleaning and sanitation process.
  • Pregnant women, the elderly and those with weakened immune systems should avoid certain foods, including unpasteurized or raw milk, cheeses made from unpasteurized milk, raw fish raw sprouts and some other raw foods, which carry a high risk for L. monocytogenes.
  • People with pets should take special care to avoid cross-contamination when preparing their pet’s food. Be sure to pick up and thoroughly wash food dishes as soon as pets are done eating, and prevent children, the elderly, and any other people with weak immune systems from handling or being exposed to the food or pets that have eaten potentially contaminated food.
  • Consumers can also submit a voluntarily report, a complaint, or adverse event (illness or serious allergic reaction) related to a food product.
Where can I find out more information on Listeria?

More information about Listeria monocytogenes can be found on the CDC website.

Title

Return or Replace your Costco.com Online Order

Body

We are committed to offering the best value to our members, with a risk-free 100% satisfaction guarantee on both your membership and merchandise. View Costco’s Return Policy.

We are committed to providing quality and value on the products we sell with a risk-free 100% satisfaction guarantee on both your membership and merchandise. Limited exceptions apply. See Costco’s Return Policy for more information.

We offer two convenient options to return an online purchase:

Return to Warehouse:

For an immediate refund, including shipping and handling fees, return your purchase to any Costco warehouse. To find your nearest location, please visit our warehouse locator.

Accepted payment methods differ online and in the warehouse. If you are returning an online purchase to the warehouse, please check our list of accepted payments to which the refund can be placed. If possible, the refund will be placed on the card used for the purchase.

Information on Warehouse Returns

Return or Replacement on Costco.com:

To request a return or replacement on Costco.com:

  1. Log into your account through Sign In / Register.
  2. Select Orders & Returns.
  3. Choose the Return or Replace Items button next to the appropriate order, and follow the prompts to complete your return or replacement request.
  4. Receive your return label within the hour* or schedule a pickup (where eligible). For large items, you will be sent an email with additional instructions.

*Items such as food (including pet products), high-end jewelry, floral products, and paper products may require additional processing time.

You can return or replace your online purchase through Costco.com for the following reasons:

  • You are dissatisfied with the item
  • You purchased it by mistake
  • The item received is defective or damaged
  • The item received is incorrect
  • You received duplicate items
  • You refused upon delivery due to damage to the item or box
  • An item is missing (replacement only)
  • The shipment is lost

Please Note:

  • If you are having issues scheduling your online return or replacement, please check our list of items that cannot be returned through Costco.com Self Service.
  • Items returned through Costco.com will be refunded** - including shipping and handling fees - and will be credited to the original payment method used to place your order. You won’t be charged for the return shipping.
  • Select items require additional processing time before a refund is issued.
  • If you are needing a replacement part or piece of your item, please contact us.

**Limitations apply. See Costco’s Return Policy for details.

A 1% Public Improvement Fee will be added to applicable warehouse purchases and online orders placed for pick up in the Sheridan, CO warehouse location. Ubio Labs 10,000mAh Slim Portable Power Banks Recall

Body

IMPORTANT RECALL NOTICE

Costco is recalling select Ubio Labs 10,000mAh Slim Portable Power Banks purchased between September 2018 and December 2020. Members who purchased the Ubio Labs 10,000mAh Slim Portable Power Banks during this timeframe will receive an email or mailed letter.

If you have one of the recalled power banks listed below in your possession, please immediately stop using it. The lithium-ion battery inside the power bank can overheat and ignite posing a fire hazard. Costco has notified the U.S. Consumer Product Safety Commission about this issue.

Our highest priority is the safety of our members; for this reason, we are urging all Ubio Labs 10,000mAh Slim Power Bank users to stop using their device, and return it to Costco for a full refund.

What is a Retail Delivery Fee?

Updated 08/22/2025

Title

What is a Retail Delivery Fee?

Body

Colorado

On July 1, 2022, Colorado imposed a retail delivery fee on all deliveries by motor vehicle to a location in Colorado with at least one item of tangible personal property subject to state sales or use tax.

The retailer or marketplace facilitator that collects the sales or use tax on the tangible personal property sold and delivered, including delivery by a third party, is liable to collect and remit the retail delivery fee. Deliveries include when any taxable goods are mailed, shipped, or otherwise delivered by motor vehicle to a purchaser in Colorado.

If the Colorado Retail Delivery Fee is applicable to your purchase, the fee will be displayed on the pricing breakdown at checkout and will be included in the total purchase price.

Minnesota

Effective July 1, 2024, a retail delivery fee will be imposed on transactions totaling $100 or more delivered into Minnesota.

What is Costco’s return policy?

Updated 10/02/2025

Title

What is Costco’s return policy?

Body

Risk-Free 100% Satisfaction Guarantee

On Membership: We will cancel and refund your membership fee at any time if you are dissatisfied.

On Merchandise: We guarantee your satisfaction on every product we sell, and will refund your purchase price*, with the following exceptions:

  • Electronics: Costco will accept returns within 90 days (from the date the member received the merchandise) for Televisions, Projectors, Major Appliances (refrigerators above 10 cu. ft., freezers, ranges, cooktops, over-the-range and under-counter microwaves, range hoods, dishwashers, water heaters, washers and dryers), Computers, Touchscreen Tablets, Smart Wearable Devices, Cameras, Aerial Cameras (drones), Camcorders, MP3 players and Cellular Phones (return details will vary by carrier service contract).
    • Our Costco Technical & Warranty Services representatives are available to assist with technical support and warranty information for many of the products above. You’ll find a list of eligible items here.
  • Diamonds: 1.00ct or larger: Members returning a diamond over 1.00ct must also present all original paperwork (IGI and/or GIA certificates) at which time they will receive a Jewelry Credit Memo. Within 48 hours, our Costco Graduate Gemologist will inspect for authenticity.
  • Cigarettes and alcohol: Costco does not accept returns on cigarettes or alcohol where prohibited by law.
  • Products with a limited useful life expectancy, such as tires and batteries, may be sold with a product-specific limited warranty.
  • Special Order Kiosk and Custom Installed Programs: Custom product(s) manufactured to our member’s personal and unique specifications cannot be returned or refunded, except for warranty repair/replacement due to failure to meet specifications.
  • Airline and Live Performance Event items are non-refundable.
  • Precious metals are non-refundable.
  • Shop Cards are non-refundable.
  • Gift Card and Ticket items are non-refundable.
  • Recreational Carts ordered online: Costco will accept returns within 90 days from the date the member received the product; see product page for return instructions.
  • Contact Lenses: Please see How can I return my contact lens order?.
  • Eyeglasses: For warehouse purchases, please visit the Optical Department of your Costco Warehouse. For online purchases, please see Online Prescription Eyeglasses FAQ.
  • Large, freight delivered items: Please see How are freight returns handled?.
  • Hearing Aids: Please contact the Hearing Aid center at your Costco Warehouse.

How to Return an Item

Products purchased at a Costco location need to be returned at the returns counter at any Costco warehouse.

Items ordered online can be returned at any Costco warehouse -or- you can initiate a return through Costco.com.

Accepted payment methods differ online and in the warehouse. Please click here for a complete list of accepted payments.

For additional questions regarding an online purchase, please check the product details page and review the Shipping and Returns information.

*Limitations apply. See the membership counter or Costco.com for details.

What is Costco’s Sustainability Commitment?

Updated 09/18/2023

Title

What is Costco’s Sustainability Commitment?

Body

First and foremost, our aim is that our business remains responsible, resilient, and relevant in accordance with our mission statement and code of ethics.

Our mission: to continually provide our members with quality goods and services at the lowest possible prices. In order to achieve and stay true to our mission, we conduct our business with the following code of ethics in mind:

  • Obey the law.
  • Take care of our members.
  • Take care of our employees.
  • Respect our suppliers.

If we adhere to these four code of ethics elements at every level of our organization, then we achieve our ultimate goal, which is to reward our shareholders.

Our Sustainability Commitment page details how we incorporate sustainable practices in key aspects of our business. If you have a moment, we encourage you to visit the page and learn more about our goals.

What shipping methods are available on Costco.com?

Updated 06/26/2026

Title

What shipping methods are available on Costco.com?

Body

At Costco.com, we want to make sure your online ordering experience is as easy and enjoyable as possible. Shipping on most items is included, and we’ve created a comprehensive delivery policy for effortless shopping.

Costco.com Offers Many Delivery Options

We know that getting your item delivered in good condition and on time is important to you, and that’s why we have several delivery choices. We take great care to offer shipping methods and delivery terms that meet your needs:

  • Standard Delivery: We ship most items with this method via reliable delivery services like UPS, FedEx or USPS. If express shipping is chosen, an additional Shipping and Handling fee will be quoted at checkout.

  • Standard Delivery w/ No Pre Scheduled Appointment: Your item is shipped by a freight carrier, however it will be shipped in a similar manner to a Standard Delivery “UPS-like” item, left near your front door and will not require an appointment or a signature.

  • Curbside Delivery: With this option, your item is shipped by a freight carrier, which will then deliver the item right to your driveway. This method allows you to choose a designated appointment day and a signature upon delivery.

  • Threshold Delivery: This method provides delivery of a large item to the first dry area on your property, such as a garage or inside your front door, so you can be sure your purchase is safe and protected from the elements. This method allows you to choose a designated appointment day and requires a signature upon delivery.

  • Room of Choice Delivery: If you’re buying furniture or other bulky pieces, the Room of Choice option means the carrier will deliver the item and put it in any room you want, as long as there’s a clear and safe path. This method allows you to choose a designated appointment day and will require a signature.

  • White Glove Delivery: The “best of the best”, our White Glove option means that the item will be delivered at your chosen appointment day, and then the carrier will unpack, set up and inspect it to ensure it’s just what you expected. This delivery also includes removal of packaging. **see the product page for any additional White Glove services that may be included with your purchase.

  • Appliances: Costco offers in-home delivery with basic hookup and haul away for certain items, with some exceptions. **see the product page for any additional appliances services that may or may not be included with your purchase and are dependent on the carrier and installer network as well as state and local code requirements.

  • Costco Grocery Delivery: Costco grocery delivery is only available in select markets and is only available to Costco Members. Orders are limited to one address per delivery. Costco grocery delivery is available via two options:

    • 2-Day Delivery of non-perishable items. Items marked 2-Day Delivery are subject to a $3 delivery surcharge per item. This delivery surcharge does not apply to orders of $75 or more before taxes.
    • Same Day Delivery powered by Instacart, which can be found at sameday.costco.com.

For more information on our grocery delivery options, go to 2-Day Delivery or Same-Day Delivery.
For a side-by-side comparison of these options, please see our CostcoGrocery FAQs on Costco.com.

To find the specific delivery options for a particular item, just check the Shipping and Returns section of the product page. In most cases, the delivery method is pre-determined based on the item.

What’s this charge from Costco on my Visa® card or Mastercard®?

Updated 08/22/2025

Title

What’s this charge from Costco on my Visa® card or Mastercard®?

Body

If you see a charge from Costco on your Visa® card or Mastercard® for approximately $65 or $130 around the first or second day of the month, it’s most likely the automatic renewal of your Costco membership. Having your annual membership renewed automatically is a convenient way to ensure your membership is always up to date.

Check out the example below for what may appear on your bank statement:

A $65 charge is for a standard Gold Star or Business Membership while a $130 charge is for an Executive Membership before applicable sales tax.

If your membership was automatically renewed and you would like to request a refund, please click this link. Refunds take approximately 5-7 business days to process.

Need help with something else? Check out these answers:

  • What is an Executive Membership?
  • How can I review or make changes to my membership online?
  • Why do I have a $0 pre-authorization for my annual Costco renewal on my credit card?

When will I receive my order?

Updated 08/22/2025

Title

When will I receive my order?

Body

Each item on Costco.com has a unique delivery time frame that you can check both prior to and after ordering.

To check the estimated delivery time frame before you order, click into the item. Near the “Add to Cart” button you’ll see either an estimated delivery date based on your ZIP Code, or a general delivery time frame.

If you’ve already placed an order, review your Order Confirmation email. The estimated time frame will appear below the heading “Shipping & Terms.”

Alternatively, if you know your order number (also available in your Order Confirmation email) and you’d like to track your shipment, here’s what you can do:

  1. Select Order Status (you’ll find it near the bottom of the Costco.com home page) and sign in with your email address and password.
  2. Click the order number you’d like to track.
  3. You will find a tracking number link next to the item. This will direct you to the carrier’s website to track your shipment.

Please keep in mind that the estimated delivery time is an approximation, so we recommend referring to the tracking information provided by the delivery company for more specific details.

Why am I receiving emails from Costco?

Updated 07/07/2026

Title

Why am I receiving emails from Costco?

Body

Costco Wholesale may send you emails for marketing or transactional purposes. The following email domains are valid Costco Wholesale addresses:

  • Transactional emails are sent from the @trx.costco.com domain
  • Marketing emails are sent from the @digital.costco.com domain
  • Shipping and delivery emails are sent from the @orders.costco.com domain
  • Digital Costco Shop Cards are sent from costco@digitalshopcard.costco.com
  • Membership invoices are sent from membershipannualrenewal@invoice.costco.com

Please note that the above listed email addresses are not monitored. For further assistance, please Contact Us.

Title

Why am I receiving my Costco membership renewal notice via email?

In an effort to continue our commitment to sustainability, some members may have received membership renewal notices digitally to the email address on their Costco membership account.

Please note that emails sent from membershipannualrenewal@invoice.costco.com or annualrenewal@invoice.costco.com are legitimate.

Why can I no longer purchase tobacco products for my business to sell?

Updated 09/18/2023

Title

Why can I no longer purchase tobacco products for my business to sell?

Body

To purchase tobacco products for resale, Business Members must report and qualify, which includes the following:

  • The business address on the membership must match the commercial business address (P. O. Boxes are not eligible)
  • The business must report resale tobacco purchases to the MSA (Management Science Associates).

If these requirements are not met, the member will not be eligible to purchase tobacco products for resale from Costco.

To file a report, submit the information to DSC@msa.com.

To ensure your address information is accurate, call 1-866-446-5756 Monday thru Friday 9am - 5pm PT.

Why has the packaging changed for the Kirkland Signature hearing aid batteries?

Updated 08/22/2025

Title

Why has the packaging changed for the Kirkland Signature hearing aid batteries?

Body

Costco is required by law to follow certain packaging guidelines related to button cell batteries, including the zinc-air batteries used in hearing aids. In 2022, Congress enacted Reese’s Law, which requires packaging for these batteries to be certified child-resistant.

The new battery card has dotted lines on the front (as visible in the photo below) directing where to cut a V-wedge to remove a battery from the card. Refer to the packaging for more information on storing cell batteries. Please exercise extreme care when using scissors.

To express concerns regarding Reese’s Law, please contact your local congressional representative or the Consumer Product Safety Commission (CPSC) Consumer Ombudsman at 800-638-2772 or via their contact form{target=“_blank” saferedirecturl=“https://www.cpsc.gov/About-CPSC/Contact-Information/Contact-Specific-Offices-and-Public-Information/Information-Center rel=”noopener”}.
